# 第 21 章　献给破晓的失控

*4.1 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Unraveled for Daybreak　·　献给破晓的失控

## 176　Portrait of a Middle-Aged Artist　一位中年艺术家的肖像

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Graphia Academy　|　角色：March 7th; Trailblazer; Himeko; Ryusuke; Asat Pramad; Welt; Pom-Pom; "Shush"; Ashveil; Kuchiba; Feiron　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''The Indifference of Those Who Yet Live'' completed

### Story Recap

After receiving an invitation from Jade's Anti-Destruction Alliance, the Astral Express set its course for Himeko's homeland, the world from which the Express set off again after its fall — Planarcadia.

In this world blessed by the Elation, there exists the Phantasmoon Games. Wearing ancient masks of the Elation, eight chosen participants strive to create miracles that move the hearts and souls of Planarcadia's people, hoping to share in the power of the Aeon.

Having managed this world for many years, the IPC seeks to profit by controlling this Aeonic game. In response, the Astral Express, too, has decided to enter the fray.

Meanwhile, General Yao Guang, the Xianzhou envoy to the Anti-Destruction Alliance, foresaw through divination the great crisis looming over Planarcadia. In her reading, the Stellaron Hunter Blade, who had arrived for the Phantasmoon Games, would become the source of this impending catastrophe.

To uncover the truth behind the coming apocalypse, Yao Guang seized a Mask of Elation meant for the Stellaron Hunters, and placed it upon Sunday's face. Thus, due to this unexpected turn of events, Sunday was made a participant in the games.

Meanwhile, we journeyed to the school tied to Himeko's family in search of the Mask of Elation that had once belonged to her. Yet before we could reach it, we were attacked by the streamer Sparxie, a double of Sparkle, who seized the mask for herself.

After seeing through the illusion, I encountered a mysterious girl named Evanescia in the school's archive room. With her help, I obtained another mask of her own creation, and thus became a Supplicant of the Phantasmoon Games.

To defeat Supplicant Sparxie, we ventured deep into the Fool's base of operations, the "World's End" Tavern, and later returned to the school to overcome her. In doing so, we achieved the first miracle of the Phantasmoon Games: "The Express hero defeats the villain spreading the virus."

Yet at the close of the Supplicant's first battle, greater mysteries emerged. A stranger appeared, claiming himself to be Himeko's father, and revealed a long-buried tale: fifteen years ago, during the Phantasmoon Games, someone known as the Herald of Death went on a murderous rampage...

### Head to Graphia Academy and continue your adventure

(Quick read)

Aberration Threat Levels in Planarcadia

Planarcadia has a five-tier rating system for uncontrollable Aberrations and the scale of damage they cause. Ghost-level: Weak presence, essentially harmless; or causes only minor discomfort, small annoyances, troubling a handful of people; Typically ideogenitive imagenae born from collective consciousness: Such as villains from comics, panic-inducing objects spawned from rumors, animated smart appliances; ghosts with distorted cognition; Can be subdued by civilian ghost-busting squads. Even if left alone, they won't escalate into complex mass disasters. Fiend-level: Poses a clear and present danger, capable of harming dozens of people or causing structural damage, throwing multiple city blocks or an entire small town into chaos. Typically powerful imagenae that have accumulated massive Wishpower and spiraled out of control, such as unspeakable entities lurking in urban legends for ages, or vengeful manifestations born from collective trauma (say, mass casualties caused by the Herald of Death). Calamity-level: A severe threat capable of causing casualties in the hundreds or more. If left unchecked, they could lead to the destruction of major cities in arcadia's core regions. The Herald of Death falls into this category. In the Phantasmoon Games, the upper limit of destruction caused by a Supplicant going out of control also falls roughly within this range. Cataclysm-level: Catastrophic threat capable of crippling an entire continent, causing mass casualties and environmental upheaval. Reality-warping abilities manifest. Apocalypse-level: A level of threat capable of causing civilizational collapse or even the extinction of humanity on Planarcadia's surface. Their very existence is a great catastrophe.

### Go to the old classroom to regroup with Welt

> *Has it started? A new Phantasmoon Games? Ready to grab a bucket of popcorn and settle in on the couch, in a cinema, or on your phone to enjoy the show? Oh wait, you're still stuck in the middle of some mystery, with no way to escape.*

[Recap] Welt mentioned that Himeko had never spoken of her father, and asked if you know anything about what happened between them. March 7th chimed in, saying she's curious as well. Throughout Himeko's life, Ryusuke was never there, not during her struggle with the "Weathering Curse" nor the Phantasmoon Games. Himeko left behind a grave marked with her own name and became the Navigator of the Astral Express. Ryusuke insisted he had been searching for a cure for her curse all along, but he didn't even have the courage to find out if the famous Navigator was actually his daughter.

*(Approach Welt)*

**Welt:** You're here, (Trailblazer).

> **瓦尔特：**你来了啊，开拓者。

**Welt:** Himeko hasn't mentioned her father at all in the time since I've boarded. So, what is going on between them? As you were the only one with her at the time, you're the only one I can ask.

> **瓦尔特：**打从我上车以来，就没听姬子提过自己的父亲。所以，她和她父亲之间是怎么一回事？毕竟当时只有你跟在姬子的身边，我也只能找你来打听了。

**March 7th:** Yeah, it really threw me for a sec when you said "Himeko's father." But when you think about it, it's only natural that Himeko has a father, right? Not everyone's like me, thawed out of an ice block.

> **三月七：**是哦，我乍一听你们说「姬子的父亲」还愣了一下。仔细一想，姬子姐有父亲这不是很合理嘛？也不是谁都像我一样是从冰块里化开的。

- ▸ So, here's what happened...

A while ago...

**Ryusuke:** Fifteen years ago, the Phantasmoon Games of that year came to be remembered as the "Bloodstained Games"... So many parents lost their children. So many people lost those dearest to them.

**Ryusuke:** One of the victims... was my very own daughter. Upon my return, I stood before the grave, where she was laid to rest.

> **隆介：**十五年前的「幻月游戏」，被人们称为「血涂游戏」。许多人失去了自己的孩子，许多人失去了自己的至亲。

**Ryusuke:** ...Her name was Himeko.

**Ryusuke:** So, this lady who bears her name...

> **隆介：**她叫姬子。

**Ryusuke:** ...Who exactly are you?

> **隆介：**所以，眼前这位顶着她名字的女士……你到底是谁？

**Himeko:** Fifteen years ago, when I made up my mind to join this Phantasmoon Games, my father wasn't with me.

> **姬子：**十五年前，当我决心加入这场幻月游戏时，我的父亲不在我身边。

**Himeko:** Even as I fought for my life against the Herald of Death, my father was still off somewhere, wandering the cosmos.

> **姬子：**在我与告死魔对抗，命悬一线的时刻，我的父亲依旧不知道在银河哪儿游荡。

**Himeko:** Strictly speaking, ever since I can remember, his presence in my life has been as thin as the air in space...

> **姬子：**严格来说，打从记事起，他在我成长过程中的存在感就像太空里的空气一样稀薄……

**Himeko:** So, I would ask you who bears his name, who exactly are you?

> **姬子：**所以，眼前这位顶着他名字的先生，你又是谁？

**Ryusuke:** ...

> **隆介：**……

**Ryusuke:** Well, well, well, look at you... What are you getting so worked up over?

**Ryusuke:** I thought about contacting the Express, but what if their Navigator was just someone who happened to share the same name?

### Show Ryusuke around the Astral Express

### Head to the Party Car

> *Poor little raccoon, caught in the middle of a father-daughter conflict. There's no other choice but to bear with it a little longer.*

*(Talk to March 7th, optional)*

**March 7th:** What're you still doing here? Himeko asked you to go to the Party Car!

> **三月七：**干嘛还杵在这儿？姬子姐叫你去派对车厢啦。

> **三月七：**我明白了，你是怕破坏这对父女重逢的气氛是吧？那确实，等等再去也不迟。

*(Talk to Welt Yang, optional)*

**Welt:** I hope a drink will help the two of them set aside their past differences.

> **瓦尔特：**希望这两位能在喝一杯后冰释前嫌。

> **瓦尔特：**开拓者，要是父女俩拌起嘴来，就拜托你做和事老了。

[Recap] In their conversation, Ryusuke hoped Himeko would stay, but she explained that her path lay among the stars and she had only come back for the Phantasmoon Games. She saw defeating the Herald of Death copycat in the Dovebrook case as a perfect opportunity to demonstrate the Nameless's resolve to all of Planarcadia. Ryusuke was gratified and proposed meeting at the school library later to hand over the Graphia family's records regarding the Phantasmoon Games.

**"Shush":** ...

> **「闭嘴」：**要打破一对父女间冰冷的沉默，最理想的饮品莫过于一杯「热水」。

**"Shush":** The person who drinks it will yelp, "HOT!" and be immediately met with a concerned, "How could you be so careless?" from the other party. And just like that, the conversation starts flowing.

> **「闭嘴」：**饮下的人只要大呼「好烫」，就能立刻获得另一位「怎么这么不小心」的责备和关切，话题也就能顺利展开了。

**"Shush":** Friendly reminder: That is not a joke, but a practical ice-breaking tip.

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Ryusuke:** ...

> **隆介：**……

**Himeko:** Being a Nameless is neither "relaxing" nor "comfy." Most of the time, we are facing down Voidrangers from the Antimatter Legion, bugs left by the Swarm Disaster, Borisin, and various organizations that are suspicious of the Express or have malicious intentions for us...

> **姬子：**无名客的工作并不「轻松舒适」。大部分时候，我们要面对反物质军团的虚卒、蝗灾留下的虫子、步离人、还有一些对列车怀有戒心和不良意图的组织……

**Ryusuke:** ...That dangerous? Maybe... this trip home is a great chance to quit your job?

> **隆介：**…这么危险吗？那要不…干脆趁这次回老家的机会，辞掉这份工作？

**Ryusuke:** Talk about coincidence! Just so happens I'm getting on in years, and Graphia Academy is in need of a new dean.

> **隆介：**巧了，我年纪也大了，绘世学院正需要一位新的校长。

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Ryusuke:** ...

> **隆介：**……

**Himeko:** You know that's not what I meant. Once I've set my mind on something, I never look back.

> **姬子：**你知道我不是这个意思。我认准的事情从来就不会回头。

**Himeko:** Ever since I was six, when you told me those stories about traveling through the cosmos, I knew that someday, I would walk among the stars.

> **姬子：**打从六岁时，你给我讲起在银河里旅行的故事，我就明白自己将来一定会踏进星空。

**Ryusuke:** Actually, I was just kidding. I'm not completely clueless about what the Express and what you do. Oh, right! Once I knew the Express was coming, I even bought that comic series that's based on your adventures.

> **隆介：**其实，爸爸也是说笑呢。对于列车平时是做什么的，我并不是一无所知的。对了，知道列车要来，我特地买了一套以你们的冒险故事为原型的漫画。

**Himeko:** ...Please don't tell me it's Fluffy Across the Blue. I never thought you'd be into comics.

> **姬子：**姬子终于不再严肃，笑了一声…《苍天航路绒绒号》，是吧？真没想到，你也会看漫画。

> **隆介：**没错，主角是个挥舞球棒的浣熊，是以那个孩子为原型的吧？

**Himeko:** As an artist, you do have a good eye. Come over here, (Trailblazer).

> **姬子：**作为画家，你的眼光还挺准的。开拓者，来这边。

**Himeko:** Thanks for giving us some time alone.

> **姬子：**谢谢你，给了我们俩一点独处的时间。

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, that's the first time I've seen you smile since I've seen you this time.

> **隆介：**姬子，刚才是咱们重逢后，你第一次露出笑脸。

**Ryusuke:** So, what brought you back home this time? What's the story behind our lucky meeting?

> **隆介：**所以，能和我聊聊吗，你们这次回到故乡是因为什么机缘巧合？

> **姬子：**就像我之前说的那样，我们为「幻月游戏」而来。

**Ryusuke:** ...So, the Phantasmoon Games 15 years ago were the reason you were able to overcome the Weathering Curse?

> **隆介：**…所以，十五年前你能克服风化诅咒，也是因为「幻月游戏」的缘故？

**Himeko:** Indeed.

> **姬子：**没错。

**Ryusuke:** I see. That makes sense. For those of us who haven't experienced the Aeonic game firsthand, it's just something from the history books, or even akin to a myth.

> **隆介：**原来如此，这就不奇怪了。对没有亲历过这星神游戏的人来说，它只是历史文献里的一段记录，甚至说它是神话也不为过。

**Himeko:** But I never imagined... that the Aeonic game would turn into a blood sacrifice overnight.

> **姬子：**但我从来没想到…这场星神的游戏会在一夜间演变成血祭。

**Himeko:** The Herald of Death... was a heartless madman. If I hadn't defeated him then, all of Duomension City would have become his slaughterhouse.

> **姬子：**告死魔…是个毫无人性的疯子。如果当时我没能击败他，也许整个二维市都会成为他的屠场。

**Himeko:** During the first few years of my journey, I relived that evening every single night in my dreams. The color of that twilight, painted with blood... So much blood, blended with terror and screams.

> **姬子：**踏上旅程的最初几年里，我每夜每夜都会梦回那个黄昏。那个黄昏的颜色，是用血…很多很多的血，还有恐惧和尖叫调出来的。

**Himeko:** The victims were twisted like clay into grotesque shapes, like paint tubes that had been snapped, one after the other. And that man... he reveled in the colors splattered everywhere, and wanted the whole world to be his canvas...

> **姬子：**受害者们像是被拧成各种奇怪形状的粘土，像一支又一支被折断的颜料管。而那个男人享受着遍地喷洒的颜色，他想把这个世界当作画布满……

- ▸ Stop... Himeko.
- ▸ Stop dwelling on those memories.

**Himeko:** ...Even if I could avenge his victims, in the end, it won't bring them back to life.

> **姬子：**…就算最后我能为那些受害者们报仇雪恨，他们也没法再活过来了。

**Himeko:** The entire thing is like a heavy piece of baggage. Only by letting it go can I travel the road ahead more easily.

> **姬子：**这整件事就像沉重的行李，只有抛下它，我才能在未来的旅途上走得更轻松一些。

**Ryusuke:** You did your best, and I'm glad to see you made it back here safe and sound.

> **隆介：**你尽力了,很高兴看到你能安然无恙地回到这里。

> **姬子：**我本以为自己不会回来的，但时局有了变化，幻月游戏又一次满足了举行条件——短短十五年，两次幻月游戏，这本就不寻常。

**Himeko:** Just like fifteen years ago, the IPC wants to control everything. However, a game woven by the most fickle of Aeons is destined to slip through everyone's grasp.

> **姬子：**和十五年前一样，公司想要操控这一切。只是，最善变的星神所编织的游戏，注定会脱离所有人的掌控。

**Himeko:** I received a report from the Department of Aberration Defense. I'm sure (Trailblazer) must also have heard. There's been a brutal incident in Dovebrook District. Someone massacred everyone at the headquarters of the Synwish Syndicate, leaving a signature that only the Herald of Death would use... the "Bloody Bloom."

> **姬子：**我收到「异常防御部」给的通报――开拓者想必也知道了，鸽川区发生了一起凶案，有人血洗了共愿帮的总部，并且留下了当年告死魔才会留下的杀人痕迹…「绽放血手」。

(Quick read)

Dovebrook Times News Clipping

**Mysterious Murder Case: Synwish Syndicate Members Found Dead in Pool of Blood!:** Recently, a major homicide occurred in Dovebrook District. A total of thirty-three members of the Synwish Syndicate, including boss Polwave and second-in-command Caustide, were found murdered at the Syndicate's headquarters. The Security Bureau suggests the case appears to be the result of a gang turf war. Crime Wave Strikes, Dovebrook District Gripped by Fear! Regarding these incidents, some residents are applauding the news, claiming gang activity has been seriously disrupting daily life in Dovebrook District. However, the vast majority of residents are deeply concerned, since anyone could be the next victim until the case is closed. As of press time, foot traffic on Dovebrook streets has plummeted, with many shops reporting declining sales due to the impact of these cases. Gang Infighting, or Something More Sinister? A shocking murder case has cast a bloody shadow over the newly opened Phantasmoon Games. Various rumors have added a suspiciously mysterious touch to the incident. Word has it that the Synwish Syndicate recently acquired a Supplicant mask. Could this be connected to the massacre that wiped them out? An IPC Worker also tragically perished in the incident, but why would an IPC employee be on Synwish Syndicate turf in the first place? Regarding the above questions, I've already contacted the Security Bureau for clarification. The receptionist stated: The case is still under investigation. Residents of Planarcadia are advised not to believe or spread rumors, and to await further official announcements.

> **姬子：**「异常防御部」的人告诉我，作为谒者的告死魔在接受审判后早已被处死了。就算复活，受规则的限制，同一人也无法两次成为谒者。

> **姬子：**我认为，无论这个告死魔二代是否与幻月游戏有关，击败他，会是开拓者向整个二相乐园展现无名客信念的好机会。

**Himeko:** Exactly.

> **姬子：**你说的没错。

> **姬子：**你可以把这句话理解为，星穹列车提供的安全建议。

> **姬子：**还有，由于开拓者已加入了幻月游戏，成为谒者，我会全力支持她的行动。我们家世代传承的欢愉假面，我想暂时交由她来保管。

### Return to the Parlor Car

> *Finally, a moment to catch your breath. But, the Phantasmoon Games waits for no one. Run forward or you'll be left behind.*

[Recap] Pearl invited the Supplicant of the Express to Pearluxe Tower to coordinate the next steps of the Phantasmoon Games, arranging for a private car to meet them at the giant gate next to the @ Plaza.

**Welt:** ...Has Mr. Ryusuke left?

> **瓦尔特：**…隆介先生走了？

> **姬子：**嗯，我让他先回家了，毕竟，「幻月游戏」的事情本就和他无关。

**Himeko:** I'm really sorry, everyone, for bringing my personal family matters onto the Express.

> **姬子：**真抱歉，各位，把家事带到了列车上来。

**March 7th:** Not at all! Mr. Ryusuke is your family, and that makes him part of our family too. And the most important thing for family is to be together, so he's welcome to drop by any time!

> **三月七：**没有的事儿，隆介叔叔是姬子姐的家人，也就是我们的家人了。一家人最重要的就是齐齐整整啦，我们很欢迎他时不时来列车玩玩。

**Welt:** Madam Pearl sent a message to the Express just now, inviting our Supplicant to a meeting. She'll have a private car waiting for us.

> **瓦尔特：**就在刚才，真珠女士发来信息，想邀请星穹列车的谒者见个面。她会派出专车在某个地方等候我们。

- ▸ Me?
- ▸ A meeting with Pearl?

**Welt:** Yes. The meeting won't be held in some stuffy formal reception room. She would like us to visit and tour the headquarters of a company under her name instead.

> **瓦尔特：**嗯，会面的地点不是什么气氛严肃的会客室。她希望我们去她名下企业的总部大楼做客参观。

**Welt:** Himeko, you know about Pearluxe Corp, right?

> **瓦尔特：**姬子，你知道「珠星财团」的吧？

> **姬子：**嗯，二相乐园最大的综合娱乐公司。

> **三月七：**娱乐公司？

> **姬子：**不错，在动画、漫画、游戏、玩具之类的娱乐消费品领域，珠星财团都能算得上独霸半壁江山的巨头。

> **三月七：**啊，「珠星」的「珠」，原来是这个意思啊。

> **瓦尔特：**说的不多，但她明确提到想和我们磋商「幻月游戏」的后续进展——还说可以为我们提供一些小小的帮助。

**Himeko:** Helping her opponents in the Aeonic game? Looks like she realizes we are all in the same boat. Perhaps she has also realized that the Phantasmoon Games cannot be artificially manipulated.

> **姬子：**为星神游戏里的对手提供帮助？看来她确实考虑过我们的「共同进退」。也许她应该意识到了，「幻月游戏」是无法被人为操控的。

**Welt:** We may be able to get a clearer picture of Pearl's intentions after (Trailblazer) meet with her.

> **瓦尔特：**在开拓者和她碰面之后，也许我们也能更清楚真珠的意图了。

**Himeko:** Think about it. You've already crossed paths with key figures from numerous factions, and even built rapport with some. You're already a capable Nameless who can more than hold your own!

> **姬子：**你好好想想，这一路来你和不少派系的重要人物都打过照面，有些甚至谈得上交情了。你可是个能独当一面的无名客了啊。

**Himeko:** Though she holds a high administrative position on this planet, do not forget she is also one of the Ten Stonehearts. You've already dealt with Aventurine on numerous occasions, so you should have ample experience handling members of the Ten Stonehearts.

> **姬子：**虽然真珠身居这颗星球管理者的高位，但她同时也是石心十人之一，你已经和砂金打过不少交道了，对于如何应对石心十人的成员应该颇有经验了。

**March 7th:** Hey, don't worry! If things do get messy, yours truly is here to save the day!

> **三月七：**别担心，真要出了岔子，还有机灵的本姑娘在！

**Welt:** Looks like March wants to visit too.

> **瓦尔特：**看来小三月也想一起去参观啊。

> **三月七：**我这不是担心开拓者嘛，就算局势有变，真珠总不能对咱们两个可可爱爱的小姑娘发火吧。

**Himeko:** Well then, I'll leave this meeting to you two.

> **姬子：**那么，这场会面就拜托你们了。

> **姬子：**我想和父亲谈谈，他不是要把家史里有关幻月游戏的记录交给我么…何况，这也是你们年轻人独当一面的好机会呀？

**Welt:** I understand. In that case, (Trailblazer), March, and I will be heading out now.

> **瓦尔特：**我明白了，那么，我和开拓者，还有小三月这就出发了。

> **三月七：**专车接送，好耶！那碰头的地方又在哪儿？

> **瓦尔特：**你们应该不陌生，就在@广场的大门下。

> **三月七：**那不就是我们第一次遇到火花的地方嘛？既然安排了专车接送，怎么不上门啊。

- ▸ Looks like we're not the only ones getting picked up, then.

> **三月七：**也好，我一直想顺路去漫画店买上一本《苍天航路绒绒号》呢。

### Head to Duomension City and wait for Pearluxe Corp's shuttle

> *Elation relishes surprises, coincidences, and randomness as its favorite treats. While wandering through Duomension City, you might just stumble upon a bit of fun in the randomness. Maybe a bizarre research society, or perhaps a potential opponent, a friend, or even a certain Supplicant who'd rather stay anonymous.*

*(Talk to March 7th, optional)*

**March 7th:** Destination, Pearluxe Headquarters! And let's grab a copy of "The Fluffy" while we're at it. This is just perfect!

> **三月七：**出发，珠星总部！顺路再买上一本绒绒号的漫画，计划完美！

*(Talk to Welt Yang, optional)*

**Welt:** (Trailblazer), we'll head out when you're ready.

> **瓦尔特：**开拓者，准备好的话，我们也出发吧。

*(Talk to Himeko, optional)*

**Himeko:** There may be some information in the family records that could help us... It's just that whenever I think about talking with my father, I can't help feeling uneasy.

> **姬子：**也许家史里会有些信息对我们有所帮助…只是一想到要和父亲谈话，难免有些浑身不自在

- ▸ That's not a Himeko we see very often.

**Himeko:** Haha... Even someone like me, who is always putting on a composed front, can end up giving everyone a laugh. When you think about it like that, (Trailblazer), the meeting with Pearl doesn't seem so scary, now does it?

> **姬子：**哈哈…平日里一直装作气定神闲的我，也会有让大家看笑话的一天。开拓者，这样一想，是不是去见真珠也没什么可怕的？

[Recap] While waiting for the car, March 7th hoped to grab a copy of the Fluffy Across the Blue from a nearby shop. Welt suggested they regroup under the gate in a few minutes.

*(Go to mission location)*

**March 7th:** Ah, look! There's a convenience store. Mind if I grab a copy of Fluffy Across the Blue while we're waiting?

> **三月七：**啊，这儿有家便利店。我想捎带买一本《苍天航路绒绒号》的漫画可以吗？

**Welt:** Is that the comic based on the Express's adventures? Haha, the author sure knows how to tap into what's trending.

> **瓦尔特：**就是那个以列车冒险故事为原型的漫画吗？哈哈，作者倒是挺会捕捉时事热点的。

**March 7th:** The best part is that the author drew all of us as adorable little animals! Totally fits our personas, don't you think?

> **三月七：**最讨巧的是，这位作者把咱们几个都画成了可可爱爱的小动物，很符合咱们的人设嘛！

- ▸ You're super adorable, March Bunny.

**March 7th:** Right back atcha, Baseball Raccoon!

> **三月七：**你也可爱，球棒浣熊！

- ▸ I'm THE protagonist, y'know!

**March 7th:** Hey, so am I! It's an ensemble comic!

> **三月七：**我也是，我也是啦！这是个群像漫画啊！

- ▸ The author is totally infringing on our IP!

**Welt:** Unfortunately, according to the intellectual property protection laws governing the Pan-Cosmic Trade System, using real-life experiences as creative material doesn't infringe on anyone's rights.

> **瓦尔特：**很遗憾，按泛银河贸易体系遵守的知识产权法规定，取用现实经历进行创作并不侵犯谁的权利。

**(Trailblazer):** Dang! There goes my payday!

> **开拓者：**可恶，痛失赔偿金。

**Welt:** In fact, it was recently announced that The Fluffy is getting an animated adaptation, and the copyright for it is currently held by Pearluxe Corp.

> **瓦尔特：**最近绒绒号还宣布了动画化的消息，动画版权如今就握在珠星手中。

**March 7th:** It's a super memorable comic! No way can I miss getting a copy while I'm here. I promise I won't be long, a couple of minutes, tops!

> **三月七：**这么值得纪念的漫画，看到了无论如何得捎带一本吧。等我几分钟哦，不会耽误太久的。

**Welt:** Looks like we got here on the early side. Hmm, since coming to this world, I still haven't had a chance to get a street-level view of the city. How about this: We'll meet up under the @ at the Plaza gate in a few minutes.

> **瓦尔特：**看情况，我们似乎也是到的早了些。打从来到这个世界，我还没好好看看这里的街景。不如这样，我们几分钟后在广场大门的@下面会合。

**March 7th:** Alright, sounds like a plan!

> **三月七：**嗯，就这么办。

### Wander around until the shuttle arrives

**(Trailblazer):** Might as well take a look around, then.

> **开拓者：**那就到处逛逛吧。

*(Approach Chatting Citizen, optional)*

**Chatting Citizen:** Absolutely insane! Did you see the post I shared?

> **闲聊的民众：**真是太疯狂了！你有看我发的帖吗？

*(Investigate Chatting Citizen, optional)*

**Chatting Citizen:** Hey, you hear the news? ...There was a Supplicant battle at Graphia Academy.

> **闲聊的民众：**你听说了吗…绘世学院那边的谒者大战？

**Waiting Citizen:** Yeah, I saw it. The online forums are already blowing up.

> **等车的民众：**看到了，网上的帖子早就聊了好几页了。

**Chatting Citizen:** The Supplicant called Sparxie... How terrifying! Actually using livestreams to spread memetic viruses! Aeons, that's just evil. I can't believe the Department of Aberration Defense is letting someone that dangerous run loose!

> **闲聊的民众：**那个叫火花的谒者…好可怕，她居然用直播散布模因病毒！哇，太歹毒了吧。这么危险的家伙，「异常防御部」也不管管！

**Waiting Citizen:** I heard her livestream got permabanned. Good thing that Nameless from the Astral Express acted so quickly.

> **等车的民众：**听说直播间已经被永久封禁了，得亏那个星穹列车的无名客动作迅速。

**Chatting Citizen:** I wonder what the next Supplicant will do?

> **闲聊的民众：**下一个出现的谒者又会做些什么啊？

*(Approach "The Fluffy" Fan, optional)*

**"The Fluffy" Fan:** Have you read the new chapter of Fluffy Across the Blue?

> **绒绒号粉丝：**《苍天航路绒绒号》最新话出了，你看了吗？

*(Investigate "The Fluffy" Fan, optional)*

**"The Fluffy" Fan:** Have you read the new chapter of Fluffy Across the Blue?

> **绒绒号粉丝：**《苍天航路绒绒号》最新话出了，你看了吗？

**"Daybreak Warrior" Fan:** Sorry, I'm not into comics for kids.

> **破晓战士粉丝：**不好意思，我不喜欢这么低龄向的作品。

**"The Fluffy" Fan:** They just released the official concept art for that giant Pom-Pom Robot!

> **绒绒号粉丝：**漫画里那个帕姆大机器人公开了官方设定图！

**"Daybreak Warrior" Fan:** That mech design is totally unrealistic... What's so great about it?

> **破晓战士粉丝：**这么没有现实感的机设到底有什么好看的？

**"The Fluffy" Fan:** Do you have the slightest clue how to talk to other people!? DO YOU!?

> **绒绒好粉丝：**你会不会和人聊天！你会不会和人聊天！

**"Daybreak Warrior" Fan:** Hey, relax! Now, if we were talking about a fusion between Daybreak Squadron and the raccoon, that would totally get my attention!

> **破晓战士粉丝：**别生气嘛，要是破晓战队和浣熊合体的话，我就感兴趣了！

**"The Fluffy" Fan:** Wow. You are unbelievably childish.

> **绒绒号粉丝：**你真的很幼稚。

*(Approach March 7th, optional)*

**Comic Reader:** Blurry_QR_Colt is the GOAT!

**Comic Reader:** What happened in the newest issue?

**Comic Reader:** No spoilers! If you've already read it, don't tell me a thing!

*(Talk to March 7th, optional)*

**March 7th:** I've tried buying a copy of "The Fluffy" from a couple of stores, but they were always sold out! I really hope this store has it in stock.

> **三月七：**我好几次想去店里买一本绒绒号的漫画，但发现都卖空啦。希望在这家店里能买到。

*(Approach Ashveil)*

**Recruiter:** Hello there! Would you like to learn more about the Research Society of Happy Smiles?

> **募新人员：**您好，想了解一下幸福微笑研究会吗？

**Ashveil:** So, uh... are there any charges or fees?

> **不死途：**所以，你们这个…收费吗？

**(Trailblazer):** Isn't that "Detective Passerby"? What's he doing here?

> **开拓者：**这不是「路过的名侦探」吗？他在这儿干什么？

[Recap] Ashveil spoke with some Smiles Society recruiters and found out about a seminar starting soon. You joined the talk out of curiosity and received one of their flyers.

*(Go to Ashveil)*

**Recruiter:** Is the weight of work and stress in your life crushing you? Check out the Research Society of Happy Smiles!

> **募新人员：**工作、生活压力沉重到压得人喘不过气来？请您务必了解一下「幸福微笑研究会」。

**Ashveil:** So, uh... this free?

> **不死途：**你们这个…免费吗？

**Recruiter:** Rest assured, the only thing joining our Research Society will cost you is a bit of your precious time.

> **募新人员：**请放心，加入研究会唯一需要的是您抽出一点宝贵时间就好。

**Recruiter:** We've set up a venue near Dovebrook, where we offer free psychological counseling and living assistance to all visitors. All our services are provided free of charge.

> **募新人员：**我们在鸽川附近设有宣讲会场，在那里我们会向所有来客提供免费的心理疏导和生活帮助一一所有的服务全都是免费的。

**Ashveil:** I... I had a friend who passed away, and he'd mentioned you before. He told me that if I ever ran into trouble, the kind people at the Research Society would help me...

> **不死途：**我…我有一位过世的朋友提到过你们。他曾跟我说过，要是遇到什么困难，可以求助有研究会的好心人——

**Recruiter:** Your friend passed away? How terribly sad. But the words he left you have illuminated your path in life.

> **募新人员：**您的朋友身故了？真令人悲伤啊。但他给您留下的遗言确实为您指明了人生道路。

**Ashveil:** He told me that if I joined the Research Society of Happy Smiles, I could get rid of my suffering forever, and be eternally happy. That true?

> **不死途：**他告诉我，加入幸福微笑研究会，就能永远消除痛苦，得到幸福的笑容。这是真的吗？

**Recruiter:** Of course it's true. Just look at the society we live in. Everyone's suspicious and dismissive of one another, and willing to take dangerous risks just for a little pleasure, for a thrill.

> **募新人员：**当然是真的。看看周围这个社会，人人彼此猜忌，相互漠视，为了一点点欢乐和刺激就铤而走险。

**Recruiter:** Did you hear about how a streamer took over Graphia Academy and was trying to spread a memetic virus through the internet? And over in Dovebrook, gang violence erupted, and left a pile of corpses in its wake. May the Master of Elation save us all.

> **募新人员：**您听说了吧，有个主播占据了绘世学院，想通过网络撒播模因病毒。而鸽川那边的帮派仇杀火并，死掉了好些人。愿欢愉之主普救众生。

**Recruiter:** The Research Society was founded with the sole purpose of healing humanity's incurable inner pain. Following Aha's creed, we aim to spread laughter across the mortal coil and eliminate all suffering.

> **募新人员：**研究会的成立初衷，正是为了治疗人类内心无法愈合的伤痛。它奉行阿哈的宗旨，要将笑声遍酒人间，消除一切痛苦。

- ▸ Eliminate all suffering... what's that mean?

**Recruiter:** Exactly what it says. As an eraser wipes away the doodles of a pencil, so too shall the pain be wiped away.

> **募新人员：**当然就是字面意义上的，就像是用橡皮将铅笔的涂鸦擦掉一样，抹掉痛苦。

- ▸ Eliminate all suffering... No way is that possible.

**Recruiter:** Of course it's possible! For this is a world blessed by the God of Elation, where Aha has granted us the right to happiness.

> **募新人员：**当然能做到，这里可是受到欢愉之神赐福的世界，阿哈向我们许诺了幸福的权利。

**Recruiter:** Have either of you heard about the tragedy of the "Bloodstained Games" fifteen years ago?

> **募新人员：**两位，你们是否听说过十五年前「血涂游戏」的悲剧呢？

- ▸ I've heard of it.

**Recruiter:** Why of course you have, you're the Astral Express's superstar! You've even dug up everything there is to know about something that happened over a decade ago!

> **募新人员：**不愧是星穹列车的大明星啊，连十五年前的旧事都打听得一清二楚。

- ▸ Nope, doesn't ring a bell.

**Recruiter:** That won't do at all! You're the Astral Express's superstar, there's a ton of things you need to look into before joining the Phantasmoon Games.

> **募新人员：**这可不行啊，星穹列车的大明星，要加入幻月游戏，许多事情可得打听清楚才行。

**Recruiter:** There was an awful accident during the last Phantasmoon Games, and many people lost their loved ones and children. It was as if a hole that could never be filled had been drilled into their hearts, leaving only sorrow to accompany them for the rest of their lives.

> **募新人员：**在上一次幻月游戏中，有许多人因为意外，失去了自己的亲人与子女。他们的内心仿佛被痛苦凿开了一个怎么也无法填满的空洞，余生只能和悲伤为伴。

**Recruiter:** To escape their suffering, these people who had lost their loved ones gathered together and established a mutual support association. This was the Research Society of Happy Smiles' predecessor.

> **募新人员：**为了能摆脱痛苦，这些失去挚爱至亲的人们聚集起来，成立了受害者互助协会。这就是幸福微笑研究会的前身。

**Recruiter:** It's now been well over a decade, and the Research Society continues to help more and more people emerge from the darkness in their lives and step into a brighter, happier future.

> **募新人员：**十多年过去了，研究会正在帮助越来越多的人走出生命中的黑暗，踏入更幸福光明的未来。

**Recruiter:** Come to think of it, it would be most wonderful if a distinguished person like yourself were also interested in our Research Society! Should you find yourself with a moment to spare, please come and visit our mutual aid society!

> **募新人员：**对了，如果您这样的知名人士也对研究会感兴趣的话，那可就太好了！之后闲暇时间，请务必来我们的互助会看看！

[Recap] Ashveil had planned to slip into the Smiles Society unnoticed, but you accidentally blew his cover. To honor a friend's memory, he bought the last copy of the The Fluffy from March 7th for a hundred times its value, advising you to watch your step.

**Ashveil:** Well, if it isn't the "Astral Express's superstar"... Of course it's you. Looks like we really were bound to run into each other.

> **不死途：**「星穹列车的大明星」…看来咱俩还真是冤家路窄啊。

- ▸ Ah, "Detective Passerby". What're you doing?
- ▸ What, here to catch me again?

**Ashveil:** Perish the thought! You're the hero who just saved Duomension City! Laying a hand on you would spark public outrage.

> **不死途：**怎么会，你可是刚刚拯救了二维市的英雄，对你下手可是会触犯众怒的。

**Ashveil:** I'm here to do my job. Problem is, you've gone and messed up my investigation!

> **不死途：**我出现在这里，当然是在干侦探该干的活儿。但是，你搞砸了我的调查！

- ▸ How'd I manage that?

**Ashveil:** Here I was, pretending to be a broke passerby to infiltrate this shady "Research Society." You should've ignored me, but no, you acted like some approachable celebrity waltzing into a supermarket, hijacking my conversation with their recruiter.

> **不死途：**当我装作一个经济拮据的路人，想要打入这个可疑研究会的当口，你最好对我视而不见，而不是像个平易近人的大明星走进超市一样，从推销员口中接过我的话头。

**Ashveil:** With a star like you here, I can't even test the waters anymore.

> **不死途：**你这样的名人一出现，我连探探口风都不成了。

- ▸ You're investigating some roadside charity group?

**Ashveil:** The "Research Society of Happy Smiles"... You think they're just some random charity outfit?

> **不死途：**「幸福微笑研究会」…这算什么路边的慈善团体。

**Ashveil:** You must've heard about the Synwish Syndicate killings in Dovebrook, right? The lead I'm following might be connected to this "Research Society of Happy Smiles." But I'll stop there. Not like you're going to pay for my intel, and none of this is any of your business anyway.

> **不死途：**你应该知道鸽川那边共愿帮的血案吧？我在查的事情也许和这个「幸福微笑研究会」有关。但多的我不想说了，横竖你不会掏钱买我的情报，而且这事也和你没什么关系。

- ▸ Someone I knew died in that incident.

**Ashveil:** ...Ah. Based on your social circle, you wouldn't be talking about the poor IPC sap who was unlucky enough to be there?

> **不死途：**…啊，从你的社交关系判断，你说的死者该不会是现场唯一的公司倒霉蛋吧？

**Ashveil:** Planning to avenge him?

> **不死途：**打算替他报仇吗？

- ▸ I am.

**Ashveil:** Sharp eyes, kid./missy. Anyone who messes with someone like you is in for a nasty surprise.

> **不死途：**小姑娘/小伙子，眼神不错啊。惹到你这样的人，对他们而言算是惹到个大麻烦了。

- ▸ Maybe?

**Ashveil:** I get it. I can see there's still a bit of doubt in your eyes.

> **不死途：**我理解。你的眼神里还藏着一点犹豫。

- ▸ I plan on defeating the Herald of Death. That counts as revenge.

**Ashveil:** Oh, so your next "miracle" will be... executing the Herald of Death? Can't say I'm surprised.

> **不死途：**喔，所以你的下一个「奇迹」是…「处决告死魔」？倒也不意外。

**(Trailblazer):** Be careful, the murderer is the Herald of Death. He's dangerous.

> **开拓者：**请小心，犯下这个案子的「告死魔」很危险。

**Ashveil:** Hahaha... Thanks for your concern. "Be careful"... Been a hot minute since anyone said that to me.

> **不死途：**哈哈哈…谢谢你的关心。「小心」…很久没人对我说这两个字了。

**Ashveil:** Even so, it's my duty to solve the case. Doesn't matter if nobody pays me, I'll still find the killer, and exact justice on the spot.

> **不死途：**不过，查清这桩案子是我的义务。哪怕没人为此掏钱，我也会查出凶手，将他就地正法。

- ▸ Why?

**Ashveil:** Because one of the victims happened to be someone I was on fairly good terms with... the fool.

> **不死途：**因为受害人中有一个和我私交还算不错的笨蛋。

- ▸ A friend of yours was among the victims?

**Ashveil:** Mm. A fool I happened to be on fairly good terms with.

> **不死途：**嗯，一个和我私交还算不错的笨蛋。

**Ashveil:** Revenge killings or turf wars... wouldn't be unusual for gang members to die like that. But dying at the hands of some psycho who kills for kicks? That, I can't accept.

> **不死途：**帮派分子死于仇杀、火并…这些都不意外。但死于某个疯子取乐般的虐杀，我不能接受。

**Ashveil:** No matter where you are in the cosmos, which era, regardless of how civilization evolves, some rules will just never change...

> **不死途：**无论身在银河中的哪个世界，哪个时代，无论文明如何变化，有些规则永远不会被改变

**Ashveil:** Like "an eye for an eye, a tooth for a tooth."

> **不死途：**比如「以牙还牙，以眼还眼」。

**March 7th:** Hey, (Trailblazer)! Guess which lucky girl just managed to snag the last copy in the whole store— Oh! Uh, who's this?

> **三月七：**哎嘿，开拓者！今天本姑娘真是好运！居然让我买到了便利店里最后一本漫画一一啊，这位是？

**Ashveil:** Allow me to introduce myself. I am Ashveil, the famous detective. You could say your friend and I are acquainted through past clashes.

> **不死途：**自我介绍一下，名侦探，不死途。和你的这位朋友可谓是不打不相识的关系。

- ▸ We didn't actually fight, though.

**Ashveil:** We did have a bit of a clash of opinions back at the tavern did we not?

> **不死途：**怎么说我们也是在「酒馆」打过照面的。

**(Trailblazer):** That's what you meant by "clash"? Fair enough.

> **开拓者：**原来是这么一个「打」啊？好吧。

- ▸ I—I don't really know this guy.

**Ashveil:** My, my, celebrities truly are heartless...

> **不死途：**大明星还真是无情啊……

**March 7th:** First time I've ever heard anyone introduce themselves as a "famous" detective...

> **三月七：**还是第一次听人介绍自己是名侦探的……

**March 7th:** Mr. Ashveil, why are you staring at me? Is there something weird on my face?

> **三月七：**不死途先生，你总盯着我干嘛？我脸上有什么奇怪的东西嘛？

**Ashveil:** Um... young lady, you're holding a copy of Fluffy Across the Blue, right? Would you mind giving it to me?

> **不死途：**那个…小妹妹，你怀里那本，是《苍天航路绒绒号》对吧？能不能请你把这本漫画让给我？

**March 7th:** Give it to you...? This was the last copy in the store, you know!

> **三月七：**让给你…可这是店里的最后一本漫画？

**Ashveil:** Ah, I see... Of course, I'm not asking you to give it away for free. I'll pay for it! The retail price is... 300 credits? I can pay you ten times sticker price... 3,000 credits, what do you say?

> **不死途：**这样啊，当然，我不是让你白给。我会付钱的！这本漫画的原价是…三百信用点？我可以十倍收购，三千信用点，怎么样、

**March 7th:** Hey, mister, this isn't about the money!

> **三月七：**大叔，这根本不是钱的事情！

**Ashveil:** Oh? Because it was the last one on the shelves? True, true. The last item in stock is like the final slice of cake on a plate, the last prey animal on the plains. It never fails to stir our urge to seize it before anyone else. So—

> **不死途：**喔？是因为这是店里的最后一本漫画？确实。最后一本漫画，就好像盘子里的最后一块蛋糕，原野上的最后一只猎物，总能激起人得之而后快的胜负欲，所以——

**Ashveil:** Th-thirty thousand credits, how about that? That's my final offer! I'm not exactly rolling in money here.

> **不死途：**三、三万信用点怎么样？不能再多了！我手头也不阔绰啊。

- ▸ ...Why are you fighting over a comic book with a kid?
- ▸ Aren't you a bit too old for stuff like this?

**Ashveil:** Truth is, I'm actually buying it for that foolish friend of mine, the one who died.

> **不死途：**其实，我是为那个死去的笨蛋朋友买的。

**Ashveil:** Today is his farewell ceremony. I thought I'd buy him his favorite comic to pay my respects.

> **不死途：**今天是他的告别仪式，我想着买一本他最喜欢的漫画去祭奠他。

**Ashveil:** Ha, he'd always be rambling on about some raccoon with a baseball bat, and how the journey of the Express would never end, that kinda stuff. I heard those stories so many times, it felt like my ears were gonna fall off.

> **不死途：**这家伙闲聊时总会跟我说什么浣熊和球棒，什么列车的旅程永远不会结束之类的，听得耳朵都起茧子了。

**Ashveil:** But... after all that fuss, I completely missed the chance to buy it! Every store around here is sold out!

> **不死途：**但是…刚刚那一番折腾，我居然错过了这附近店里的最后一本漫画！

**March 7th:** Aww, that's enough! My poor little heart can't handle sob stories like that. Here, Mr. Ashveil, take the comic!

> **三月七：**唉，好了好了！本姑娘最听不得这种让人心碎成八瓣的话。不死途先生，这本漫画你先拿走吧！

**Ashveil:** Thank you very much for the comic, young lady. Here's the 30,000 credits, as promised!

> **不死途：**谢谢你的漫画，小妹妹。这三万信用点，还请你收好！

**Ashveil:** Oh! I heard you're the real-life inspirations for the characters. Since you're here, could I perhaps trouble you to sign it?

> **不死途：**对了，听说你们就是这本漫画的角色原型，能请你们顺道帮我签个名吗？

**March 7th:** Unbelievable...

> **三月七：**真拿你没办法唉。

- ▸ (Sign) (Trailblazer)
- ▸ (Sign) Baseball Raccoon
- ▸ (Sign) May Your Journey Never End

**Ashveil:** Thank you both. If Caustide could see this from where he is now, I'm sure he'd be smiling down on us.

> **不死途：**谢谢二位。恶水这家伙要是泉下有知，他应该会很开心的。

**Ashveil:** As a token of my gratitude, I'd like to give you a friendly word of caution, (Trailblazer): I know you plan to target the Herald of Death and cement your heroic image.

> **不死途：**作为报答，开拓者，我想给你一些善意的提醒：我知道你打算将「告死魔」作为展示自己英雄形象的目标。

**Ashveil:** However, all eyes will be on you during the Phantasmoon Games. On such a public stage, discretion would be wise.

> **不死途：**不过，在「幻月游戏」的舞台上，当所有人的目光都注视着你的时候，还是谨慎行事为妙。

**Ashveil:** Because when the masses see your magnificent miracles and wield your extraordinary power, fear and jealousy could turn their "Wishpower" into "Wish-you-ill-power" at the drop of a hat. Forgive the expression, but you get what I mean.

> **不死途：**因为当你向那些普通人展示超然的奇迹和力量时，他们的「愿力」也随时可能会成为恐慌、嫉恨的「怨力」一一只是个文字游戏，你懂我意思就好。

**Ashveil:** So, whatever plans you might have for this Herald of Death, why not leave the whole messy affair of revenge to someone who couldn't care less about winning or losing, like me?

> **不死途：**所以，不管你对这位「告死魔」有什么想法，复仇这样容易脏手的事，还是交给我这样压根不在乎胜负的人，如何？

**Ashveil:** Just like this comic. I'm dead set on getting him.

### Meet up with Pearluxe Corp's host

[Recap] A specialist from Pearluxe Corp's mechatron division arrived, announcing that he was picking up Ashveil as well as the Express Crew. You all boarded a high-end shuttle to head to the tower.

**March 7th:** Well, we're here. So, where are they? I don't see anyone in IPC uniform.

**March 7th:** ...A mechatron?

> **三月七：**…机铠？

**???:** I am Guard number 100086, created by Pearluxe Corp, a security specialist from the Business Division. My designated human name is Feiron, said to derive from an ancient language meaning "iron." It's a pleasure to be of service.

> **？？？：**我是珠星造「卫」100086号机，商务科的特别安保专员，我被赋予的人类称呼是费伦，听说来自某个古老语言中的「铁」，很高兴为您效劳。

- ▸ Wow, a mechatron employee, how cool is that!

**Feiron:** I'm truly honored to hear you say so.

> **费伦：**能听到您这样说，我深感荣幸。

- ▸ Why a mechatron?

**Feiron:** At present, 16% of Pearluxe Corp's active workforce are mechatrons. In specific business and security contexts, they outperform human staff in protective capability and offer a stronger sense of spectacle and visual appeal.

> **费伦：**珠星目前在职员工中有16%的比例为机铠职员。在一些商务接待与安保场合，机铠能发挥比人类员工更好的保护作用，且更具惊异感和观赏性。

**Feiron:** The smiles on your faces and looks of surprise are the highest praise I could ask for.

> **费伦：**诸位脸上的笑容和惊讶，是对我最佳的褒扬。

- ▸ How long have you been waiting here?

**Feiron:** Please, do not concern yourselves with such things. My sole duty today is to ensure everyone reaches Pearluxe headquarters safely and happily.

> **费伦：**各位不必在意，今天我被安排的唯一工作就是确保大家顺利和愉快地到达珠星的本部大楼。

**March 7th:** So, what're we waiting for? Let's go!

> **三月七：**那咱们还等什么呢，这就走吧？

**Feiron:** According to the instructions given to me by Madam Pearl's secretary, there is still one more guest I need to pick up.

> **费伦：**按照真珠女士秘书的吩咐，还有一位客人要我顺道迎接。

**Ashveil:** Well, now how did I not see this coming... Looks like we're going the same way after all.

> **不死途：**我怎么就没想到呢…咱们竟是同路人。

> **费伦：**专车已经就位，请各位入座。

**Ashveil:** Whoa, now that's what I call a custom paint job.

> **不死途：**嚯，涂装还真有个性。

**Feiron:** Your ride isn't your run-of-the-mill business car, but a customized made-to-order vehicle, used especially for receiving guests.

> **费伦：**这并非一般商务接待车，而是我们为了迎接贵客而专门定制的车辆。

> **瓦尔特：**抱歉，虽然有些不合时宜，但我还是想问个问题一一这辆车的司机席，真的能坐下费伦先生吗？

## 177　Society of the Spectacle and Its Enemies　景观社会及其敌人

> **真珠：**最初的我，只是一台仿造人类画作的机器。对于智械来说，生成艺术品并非难事。

**Pearl:** Day after day, I studied. Day after day, I exhausted every possibility. I could instantly analyze the proportional structure and pigment composition of any painting. I could precisely reproduce every pixel of Graphia's masterpieces, yet I could not create as she did.

> **真珠：**我日复一日学习、日复一日穷举。我可以瞬间解析一切画作的比例结构与颜料成分，我可以精准复刻绘世画作的每一个像素，但我无法像她一样创造。

**Pearl:** Graphia's ultimate creation was establishing the "World in Canvas" at the end of the Phantasmoon Games. I want to understand her thoughts at that moment, and touch the essence of creation.

> **真珠：**绘世的至高创造，是在幻月游戏的尾声开辟了「画中世界」。我想洞察那一刻她的所思所想，触碰创造的真髓。

**Welt:** Is this your personal pursuit, or the IPC's demands on this world?

> **瓦尔特：**这是您个人的追求，还是公司对这个世界的诉求？

**Pearl:** Both. The IPC's point of view is far more pragmatic: The "World in Canvas" is a dimension that can shield us from the Antimatter Legion's warfare, and they've always been eager to master this technology.

> **真珠：**二者皆有。公司的视角更为功利：「画中世界」是一个足以躲开反物质军团战火的维度，他们也一直渴望掌握这项技术。

**Welt:** Unfortunately, it seems we can't convince each other to back out for now.

> **瓦尔特：**很遗憾，看来我们暂时无法说服彼此退让。

**Pearl:** Then, I have another proposal: Why don't we shift our perspective and turn our attention to those two Supplicants who haven't surfaced yet?

> **真珠：**那么，我有另一个提议：不妨改变一下视角，将目光投向尚未浮出水面的那两位谒者。

**Pearl:** These two individuals' objectives are unclear, and their abilities remain a mystery. If left unchecked, we might see a repeat of the Herald of Death tragedy. I have a cooperation proposal that could help us gather intelligence on them. And I'm willing to share this piece of information with you all.

> **真珠：**这两人目标不明、能力成谜，若放任不管，极有可能会重演当年告死魔的悲剧。我有一个合作方案，有助于判明他们的情报。同时，我也愿意和各位分享这份情报。

- ▸ I'm guessing that doesn't come for free, right?

> ▸ 不免费，对吧？

**Pearl:** That is correct. But all you need to do is sacrifice a tiny sliver of your reputation.

> **真珠：**不错。但各位需要付出的，也不过是一点名气罢了。

- ▸ Ok, but at what cost?

> ▸ 那么，代价是什么呢？

**Pearl:** A tiny sliver of your reputation.

> **真珠：**一点小小的名气罢了。

**Pearl:** Before we move on to the details of our cooperation, would you like to take a short break and have some tea to wet your whistles, or shall we continue?

> **真珠：**在谈合作细节前，诸位是想歇一歇，喝口茶润润嗓子？还是接着谈下去？

- ▸ Let's continue the discussion.

> ▸ 接着谈下去。

- ▸ Having some tea wouldn't be a bad idea.

> ▸ 喝口茶润润嗓子。

> **瓦尔特：**您想用这场展会引诱那两位身份不明的谒者现身？

> **真珠：**在「星铁FES」正式开启前，我还想拜托各位一件事：我想请列车组登门拜访，见一见《苍天航路绒绒号》的原作者，模糊二维马。

But if the real-life inspiration behind the story were to pay them a personal visit, it would surely be a great honor, and likely persuade them to attend a signing, stoking the excitement even further.

> **真珠：**自出道以来这位作者一直隐姓埋名，真容从不显露人前。但如果故事里的真人原型能到府上拜访，想必给足了他面子，也能说服他到场签售，再添一把火。

**Pearl:** By the way, (Trailblazer), before you leave, please accept this small gift as a memento of our first collaboration.

> **真珠：**对了，开拓者，在离开前，还请收下这件小小的礼物，作为你我第一次合作成立的纪念吧。

> **真珠：**我详细观摩了同僚们在匹诺康尼与合作伙伴往来的记录。其中砂金先生习惯赠送代表好运的小礼物这个技巧让我颇受启发。

**Pearl:** So, please keep this chip with you as a symbol of good fortune for our future cooperation. Take care, everyone.

> **真珠：**因此，请把这枚筹码当成是你我合作好运开始的象征带在身上。诸位，一路走好。
