> **瓦尔特：**那我们就告辞了。

[Recap] Ashveil was entrusted by Pearl to protect the Nameless at Star Rail FEST. Ashveil demanded from Pearl the truth behind the "Herald of Death" case, as well as immunity for revenge, as payment for taking on the mask.

**Ashveil:** As your private detective, I must warn you that a grand exhibition of unprecedented scale would make for a perfect opportunity to reenact the "Bloodstained Games."

> **不死途：**作为你的私家侦探，我有义务提醒你，一场盛况空前的展会，将会成为重演「血涂游戏」的绝佳时机。

**Pearl:** I understand. That's why I have a mission for you: Protect the Nameless at "Star Rail FEST."

> **真珠：**我明白。所以，我要交给你一个任务：在「星铁FES」上保护这些无名客。

**Ashveil:** You actually think they would need my protection with their strength? Don't tell me your extrapolations predict even bigger trouble ahead?

> **不死途：**以他们的实力，需要我来保护？难不成在你的推演里，会有更大的麻烦到来？

**Pearl:** Hidden beneath each extrapolation, tiny signals of unease keep bubbling up to my thought interface. I must be thoroughly prepared for whatever may come.

> **真珠：**潜藏在一次次推演之下，总有些小小的不安信号上浮到我的思考界面中。我必须为此做好万全的准备。

**Pearl:** Now that you've taken up the mask, let's talk about payment. What do you want? The same as always, money?

> **真珠：**既然你接下了面具，也该谈谈报酬了。你想要什么？老样子，钱？

**Ashveil:** Before we talk about payment, let's discuss the Herald of Death. Although the Department of Aberration Defense sealed and destroyed all records of the Bloodstained Games, I still managed to uncover some unusual leads...

> **不死途：**谈报酬前，先谈谈「告死魔」吧。虽然「异常防御部」封存、销毁了关于血涂游戏的资料。但我还是查到了一些不寻常的蛛丝马迹……

**Ashveil:** After the Herald of Death incident, the victims' families collectively received compensation so massive it could fill any gaping wound. So I figured, maybe we could find some leads by looking into official Planarcadia expenditures.

> **不死途：**告死魔事件结束后，受害者家属集体得到了一笔数字大到足以填满任何巨大创伤的赔偿金。所以我想，也许可以从二相乐园的官方支出里找找门道。

**Ashveil:** Here's the interesting part: This item was nowhere to be found in that year's public financial report. Care to guess whose books this money went through?

> **不死途：**有趣的是，当年公示的财报中却遍寻不着这个条目。你猜猜，这笔钱到底走了谁的帐？

**Pearl:** ...

> **真珠：**……

**Ashveil:** It was you, the omnipresent IPC. To be precise, it was the IPC's Technology Department that paid this astronomical compensation.

> **不死途：**是星际和平公司的「技术研发部」支付了这笔堪称天文数字的赔款。

**Ashveil:** Why would a lethal incident on some minor frontier planet cause IPC scientists to hemorrhage funding? Don't tell me they're moonlighting as philanthropists on top of their research work?

> **不死途：**为什么一颗小小边星上的死亡事件要让IPC科学家们的经费大出血？难道他们除了搞科研外，还心系慈善不成？

**Pearl:** Following the usual human drama tropes, am I supposed to give you a round of applause now? Being able to dig up all this information really shows your connections and capabilities, Detective Ashveil.

> **真珠：**按照人类戏剧的套路，这时候我是不是该来点掌声？能查到这些信息，足见名侦探不死途的人脉与实力。

**Ashveil:** In movies, a big shot like you would usually raise a hand right about now, revealing the dark muzzle of a gun.

> **不死途：**电影里你这样的大人物通常会把手抬起来，露出黑洞洞的枪口。

**Ashveil:** "I'm afraid you've learned too much." And BANG! That's when the gun goes off, and I, the poor sap who uncovered the truth, am supposed to bite the dust.

> **不死途：**「对不起，你知道的太多了。」砰！枪响，然后我这个翻出真相的倒霉蛋就该一命呜呼了。

**Pearl:** Sorry, my hand is on the checkbook and unavailable. Mr. Ashveil, tell me: How many zeros would you like to see on that check for our cooperation?

> **真珠：**抱歉，我的手正放在支票簿上，抽不开空。不死途先生，告诉我：这次合作的报酬数字，你想填几个零？

**Ashveil:** ...It's not about the money.

> **不死途：**…这不是钱的问题。

**Pearl:** Indeed, money isn't the issue. A hundred million credits?

> **真珠：**确实，钱不是问题。一亿信用点？

**Ashveil:** Are you trying to buy me off like some poor victim's family? Tell me, why did the Technology Department pay to sweep this under the rug?

> **不死途：**你把我当受害者家属收买？告诉我，为什么是「技术研发部」付钱抹平了这事？

**Pearl:** Three hundred million. I know you've always needed money: For cryosleep, supporting the families of fallen comrades, and for treating those who survived. Isn't this exactly what strategic investment is about? Putting money in the hands of those who need it?

**Ashveil:** I'm not your lackey, Intellitron! Tell me the answer!

> **不死途：**我不是你的打手，智械！告诉我答案！

**Pearl:** 500 million. I could pretend there's a glitch in my calculation process and make it a cool billion for you. Money or answer, your call.

> **真珠：**五个亿，我可以装作计数进程出错，给你填上十个亿。金钱或答案，二择一，你来选。

**Ashveil:** ...I want the answer.

> **不死途：**…我要答案。

**Pearl:** ...An unexpected choice. It defies my usual understanding of your decision tree. Note: I say this without sarcasm or ridicule.

> **真珠：**…出人意料的选择。颠覆了我对你决策树的一贯认知一一备注：这句话绝不含有嘲讽的语义。

**Pearl:** The answer is: I don't know much. Hard to believe, perhaps, but as CEO, my power isn't unlimited. Besides...

> **真珠：**答案是：我知道的不多。也许你很难相信，作为首席执行官，我的权力并不是无限的。何况……

**Pearl:** The Herald of Death affair lies fifteen years in the past.

> **真珠：**告死魔已是十五年前的往事了。

**Pearl:** This world is like an unfinished game of chess that's been dragging on for years. The hidden moves laid by the previous player keep becoming my challenges, even today.

> **真珠：**这个世界就像是多年未了的一局残棋，前任棋士埋下的暗手总是不断成为我今日的挑战。

**Ashveil:** But since you've invited me to finish this game together, sharing intel is a must.

> **不死途：**但既然你邀请我一起下完这局棋，那么共享情报是必须的。

**Pearl:** Reasonable. I'll share what I know about the answer.

> **真珠：**合理，我会分享我所知的「答案」。

**Pearl:** Marcus Antali, the Herald of Death, last member of the Intelligentsia Guild's "School of Evolutionary Perfection." Five years ago, his identity was confirmed, and he was executed. Note: Executed in every sense of the word.

> **真珠：**「告死魔」马库斯•安塔利，博识学会「完美进化学派」最后一位成员。五年前，他已被验明正身，执行死刑了——备注：各种意义上的「死刑」。

**Pearl:** Ms. Yabuli, head of the Technology Department, took possession of his belongings, and even I don't have the clearance to access those records.

> **真珠：**「技术研发部」的负责人亚婆离女士接手了他的遗物，相关的记录连我也无权阅读。

**Ashveil:** No wonder. So that guy was practically IPC personnel, give or take. That also explains why the Technology Department shelled out the compensation. Yet the method behind this latest killing is identical to the one from years ago, if the Herald of Death is truly dead...

> **不死途：**难怪，四舍五入，这家伙可以算得上公司的人。这也解释了「技术研发部」付钱的理由。但最近的这场凶案，手法和当年如出一辙，如果告死魔本尊已经死了……

**Pearl:** A serial killer coming back from the dead is the least of our worries. Even if a serial killer does come back to life, he's still just a serial killer who's been shut out of the Aeonic game.

> **真珠：**「一个连环杀手死而复生」，这是所有可能性中最不需要我们担忧的。连环杀手即便死而复生，也不过是一个被星神游戏排除在外的连环杀手。

**Pearl:** What's more concerning is this: Someone wants to exploit the legacy of the Herald of Death. Ms. Yabuli took away everything that man left behind, except for the fear he instilled in the people of this world.

> **真珠：**更值得警惕的是：有人想要利用「告死魔」的遗产。亚婆离女士带走了那家伙留下的一切，唯独没有带走这个世界居民对他的恐惧。

**Pearl:** And during the Phantasmoon Games, not only can miracles trigger Wishpower, but fear too. Based on my extrapolations, the appearance of this new Herald of Death might mark the beginning of the festival spiraling out of control.

> **真珠：**而在幻月游戏中，不仅奇迹可以激起愿力，恐惧也可以。按照我的推演，「新告死魔」的登场也许会成为游戏失控的开始。

**Ashveil:** No wonder you're in such a hurry to end the Games.

> **不死途：**难怪你如此急着结束它。

**Pearl:** Mr. Ashveil, I've always respected you, but right now, I need to rely on your strength even more.

> **真珠：**不死途先生，我向来敬重你，但眼下，我更需要借助你的实力。

**Ashveil:** "Respect"... When I was younger, what I really wanted was to make the IPC fear me. The passage of time's not exactly kind, is it?

> **不死途：**敬重…年轻时的我更想得到的是「公司的畏惧」，时间还真是无情啊。

**Ashveil:** Deal, Madam Pearl. But I will require one more form of compensation—

> **不死途：**成交，真珠女士。但我还要索取另一样报酬——

**Ashveil:** Whoever this new Herald of Death is, I must deliver justice with my own hands. This time, there won't be any lengthy interrogation.

> **不死途：**无论这个「新告死魔」是谁，必须由我亲手制裁。这一次，不会再有漫长的审讯。

**Pearl:** By the authority vested in me as CEO of Planarcadia, I pardon all crimes committed in the course of your revenge.

> **真珠：**以二相乐园首席执行官的权力，我赦免因你复仇而生的一切罪名。

**Pearl:** Go ahead and do what you must, Mr. Ashveil.

> **真珠：**放手去做吧，不死途先生。

Switching to Ashveil's POV...

While tracking the culprit, Ashveil stops by the bridge as memories of the past come flooding back...

## 178　Confessions of a Righteous Sinner　一个正义罪人的忏悔

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Duomension City　|　角色：Ashveil; Kuchiba; Fulwish; Magnus; Dan Heng; Blade; Silver Wolf; Sunday; Fugue　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Society of the Spectacle and Its Enemies'' completed

### Investigate the Smiles Society members at the scene

[Recap] Ashveil and Kuchiba were investigating the "New Herald of Death" and believed the culprit might be connected to the Research Society of Happy Smiles. The Society's sponsor, Fulwish, was then giving a speech in Dovebrook District, which was why Ashveil had come there.

Some time later, somewhere in Duomension City...

**Echo of the Past:** My friend, I don't blame you, but I'm done being a breathing dead weight. So do me a favor, and pay me back for that drink with this bullet.

> **过往的回声：**老朋友，我不怪你，可我不想再拖着这幅残躯，像个累赘似的活下去。所以，就用这颗子弹，来弥补你欠我的那杯酒吧。

**Echo of the Past:** Just do it, you coward! Let me die as a Galaxy Ranger before I turn into a monster of Abundance!

> **过往的回声：**动手啊！别当孬种！在我变成丰饶的怪物前，让我作为一个巡海游侠死掉！

**Echo of the Past:** Bana, shoot me. I, beg you.

> **过往的回声：**蕉，开枪，我，求你了。

**Ashveil:** By Skybow above, may you have a pleasant dream.

> **不死途：**天弓在上，愿你们能做个好梦。

**Kuchiba:** "Not knowing who you are, not knowing why you exist here... that isn't life."

> **朽叶：**「不知生为何人，不知为何存在于此，这不叫生命。」

**Kuchiba:** In ancient times, humanity's daily goal was to obtain two thousand calories of food. Humans back then knew they were just one member of the animal kingdom. They understood why they migrated, hunted, gathered...

> **朽叶：**在古老的年代里，人类每天的目标是获得两千大卡热量的食物。那时候的人类知道自己不过是动物的一员，明白自己为何迁徙、狩猎、采集……

**Kuchiba:** But now, in an era where you can get food delivered to your door with just a tap of your finger, effortlessly achieving that two-thousand-calorie goal, humanity has lost its way like never before.

> **朽叶：**而现在，在一个动动手指就有外卖送上门，两千大卡的目标轻松达成的时代里，人类前所未有地失去了方向。

**Kuchiba:** Tell me, will the Phantasmoon Games bring a new direction to this world, Ashveil?

> **朽叶：**你说,「幻月游戏」会给这个世界带来全新的方向吗，不死途先生？

**Ashveil:** What's wrong with getting food with just the tap of your finger? Don't tell me you want to go back to the days when everyone was starving and at the mercy of the harvest?

> **不死途：**动动手指就能得到食物，这不好吗？难不成你想退回那个人人食不果腹、看天吃饭的年代？

**Ashveil:** Two thousand calories are pointless but just for survival. It's the goals beyond two thousand calories that define humanity. And the Phantasmoon Games holds up a mirror to humanity, allowing them to see their truest selves.

> **不死途：**两千卡路里并不是意义，只是生存罢了。两千卡路里以外的目标才能定义人类。而幻月游戏给了人类一面镜子，让他们照见自己最真实的模样。

**Ashveil:** I'm just a detective. Ask me such a profound question again next time, and I'll charge extra.

> **不死途：**我只是个侦探，下次再问我这么深奥的问题，我要额外收费。

**Kuchiba:** My apologies. I just heard you joined the Phantasmoon Games and couldn't resist asking for your thoughts.

> **朽叶：**抱歉，只是听说你加入了「幻月游戏」，忍不住好奇，问问你的感想。

**Kuchiba:** You've met Madam Pearl, huh?

> **朽叶：**你见过真珠女士了？

**Ashveil:** She dumped this hot potato of the "Herald of Death" on me.

> **不死途：**她把「新告死魔」这个烫手山芋丢给了我。

**Kuchiba:** On us. Judging by your expression, it seems less like a hot potato and more like a sweet fruit.

> **朽叶：**是我们。看你的表情，这不像是个烫手山芋，倒像是颗甘甜的果实。

**Ashveil:** Because... I have a lead now. I think the key lies in the word: "copycat."

> **不死途：**因为，我有眉目了。我认为关键就在「模仿犯」三个字上。

**Ashveil:** To prevent copycats, you kept the Herald of Death's modus operandi from fifteen years ago under wraps. Which means...

> **不死途：**为了预防模仿犯出现，你们对十五年前告死魔的作案特征进行了严格保密，所以……

**Kuchiba:** There's only a few people who could reproduce this killing method with such precision: the investigators from the Security Bureau and the Department of Aberration Defense at the time, and those directly involved in the Herald of Death case.

> **朽叶：**能精确复现这杀人手法的只有两种人——时任治安局和异常防御部的办案人员，以及告死魔事件的当事人。

**Ashveil:** Detective Kuchiba, I've heard that you've devoted considerable time to researching the crimes from the Phantasmoon Games...

> **不死途：**朽叶小姐，我听说你一直在研究「幻月游戏」中的各种罪案……

**Kuchiba:** So, I'm the prime suspect now, is that it?

> **朽叶：**所以，我成首要疑犯了是吗？

**Ashveil:** Just a bit of minor deduction. After all, murderers always love to return to the scene of the crime. Isn't it coincidental that you happen to be standing right before my eyes?

> **不死途：**一点小小的推理罢了。毕竟，凶手总喜欢回到现场。这不是…你碰巧就在我的眼前吗？

**Kuchiba:** That's not deduction. It's a conjecture at best. As for investigating those two categories of suspects, we've been doing that for a long time already.

> **朽叶：**这算什么推理，顶多是个猜想。关于上述两类人嫌疑的调查，我们早就在进行了。

**Kuchiba:** It's been fifteen years. The relevant people and the victims' families have all gone their separate ways. Tracking them down now won't be easy.

> **朽叶：**只是十五年过去了，相关人员与受害者家属的信息都多有变迁，一时间也很难收束目标。

**Ashveil:** Yeah. After losing their loved ones in such a cruel way, their hearts were shattered. Many couldn't bear to stay in Dovebrook District and chose to move away.

> **不死途：**是啊，经历了亲人与子女离世的残酷命运，备受折磨的心灵无处安放，他们中不少人都选择搬离鸽川区这个伤心地。

**Ashveil:** I remember seeing that in the case files you sent me. They even formed a mutual aid society for the victims.

> **不死途：**我在你给的卷宗里翻到过，这些人还组成了受害者互助会。

**Kuchiba:** I wonder... after all these years, have they managed to heal the wounds in their hearts?

> **朽叶：**我不知道十五年过去，他们是否治愈了内心的创伤……

**Ashveil:** Maybe they have. After all, they go by a new name now: the Research Society of Happy Smiles.

> **不死途：**多半是翻篇了吧。毕竟，他们现在的名字是，「幸福微笑研究会」。

**Kuchiba:** Oh, you mean that charity with the slogan, "Healing Smiles, Healing Arcadias."

> **朽叶：**嗯，你是说那个「治愈笑容，治愈乐园」的慈善团体。

**Ashveil:** Before he died, Caustide mentioned that group to me. He said quite a few young people in the gang left without looking back just to join them. So I thought... maybe we should start by looking into their rivals.

> **不死途：**恶水在死前曾和我谈起过这个幸研会。他说，帮派里有不少年轻人为了加入那边，头也不回地离开了。所以我想，也许可以从竞争对手这条线查起。

**Kuchiba:** The Research Society of Happy Smiles, huh? A charity that helps people escape their pain and find happiness, offering free counseling, medical care, all that good stuff.

> **朽叶：**「幸福微笑研究会」，宣扬逃离生活的痛苦、寻找快乐，提供包括但不限于心理、医疗等公益服务的慈善团体。

**Kuchiba:** Sounds like they've turned over a new leaf. How could you call them rivals to the underworld?

> **朽叶：**投奔那边都可以算是迷途知返了吧，这样的组织怎么还被你说成是帮派的竞争对手？

**Ashveil:** I dug a little deeper into them... and found something interesting.

> **不死途：**我顺着幸研会这条线，查到了一些有趣的信息……

**Kuchiba:** You mean your monkey dug up something interesting, right?

> **朽叶：**是你的猴子查到了有趣的信息吧？

**Ashveil:** Okay, okay... Mister N dug up something interesting. Have you heard the name of the sponsor behind the Research Society? Her name's Fulwish.

> **不死途：**好吧、好吧，是老白查到了一些有趣的信息。你听过幸研会背后金主的名字吗？她叫满愿。

**Kuchiba:** That name rings a bell. Wait, isn't she the boss of Fulwish TV? I think she was one of the survivors of Graphia Academy, too.

> **朽叶：**好耳熟的名字啊，等等，这不是满愿电视台的老板吗？我记得她同样是绘世学院幸存的学生之一。

**Kuchiba:** This is getting a little too coincidental. Just like every case you've accidentally cracked before... Mr. Ashveil, should I compliment your keen instincts or your incredible luck?

> **朽叶：**这一切确实有点太过巧合了。就像过去每一次误打误撞被你破获的案子一样…不死途先生，我是该夸你嗅觉很敏锐呢，还是该夸你运气好啊？

**Ashveil:** Ahem, luck's a part of a detective's toolkit too.

> **不死途：**咳，运气也是名侦探实力的一部分。

**Ashveil:** Anyway, whether Fulwish is guilty or not, we won't know until we look into her. That's why I asked you here.

> **不死途：**总之，满愿到底有没有嫌疑，要调查了才知道，今天我就是为这事约你来的。

(Quick read)

Research Society of Happy Smiles Mutual Aid Seminar Notice

Dovebrook District New Life Project: Public Recruitment and Mental Healing Seminar at the Research Society of Happy SmilesConfused about the future? Don't know where to turn? Is the burden of life suffocating you? You crash onto your bed after another grueling day, drifting aimlessly through the tide of memes OrchTok feeds you.But when the screen fades to black, the reflection staring back reveals the truth... You aren't smiling. You can't quite recall when it began, but you seem to have lost your reason to smile.You try to force the corners of your mouth up when facing the mirror, but all you feel is a sense of alienation. When was the last time.. you really smiled? Worry not no more! The Research Society of Happy Smiles is here for you!Amidst the endless violence and spates of grisly crimes plaguing Dovebrook District...The Research Society of Happy Smiles is hosting a week-long mental wellness seminar.We are here to ease your burdens, answer your prayers, and help us all weather this storm together! Once the star of the smash hit Please Be Happy and the people's beloved "daughter,"now the president of the Research Society of Happy Smiles and director of Fulwish TV... Fulwish will also be making a special appearance soon! Together, we'll banish the gloom and rediscover the joy we've been missing! No appointment needed! Just walk right in! The Research Society of Happy Smiles is always right by your side.

**Kuchiba:** "Dovebrook District New Life Project: Public Recruitment and Mental Healing Seminar at the Research Society of Happy Smiles"... and she's the speaker?

> **朽叶：**「鸽川区新生计划：幸福微笑研究会公开招募暨心灵疗愈宣讲会」…主讲人是她？

**Kuchiba:** Holding a seminar at a place meant to bury past trauma, the very site of two bloody incidents... Ms. Fulwish's audacity is certainly impossible to ignore.

> **朽叶：**在试图忘却伤痛的地方，在两场血案发生的地方举行一次宣讲…这位满愿女士的行动真是大胆到让人难以忽视。

**Ashveil:** So what are we waiting for? It's time to drag Dovebrook District's old skeletons out into the light.

> **不死途：**还等什么呢？鸽川区的旧账，是时候该翻开来晾一晾了。

**Foolish Passerby:** Aha, are you talking? Yeah, I'm almost there.

> **痴言痴语的路人：**阿哈，你在说话吗？是的，我快到了。

**Passerby Talking to Themselves:** I want more happiness...

> **自言自语的路人：**我想要更多的幸福……

[Recap] Kuchiba was curious about Fulwish's wish, while Ashveil believed it was to stop the tragedy from fifteen years ago from ever happening. Kuchiba pressed Ashveil about his goal in the Phantasmoon Games, but Ashveil did not answer.

*(Go to mission location)*

**Ashveil:** This place is buzzing. Are they all here waiting for Fulwish to grant their wishes?

> **不死途：**这儿还挺热闹。他们都是在这等待满愿来满足他们愿望的吗？

**Kuchiba:** A rich person like Fulwish can make most people's wildest dreams come true with the snap of her fingers. I'm less curious about who she'll help than what she wants for herself.

> **朽叶：**像满愿这样的有钱人拍拍手就能实现大部分人想也不敢想的愿望。比起她能实现谁的愿望，我更好奇她的愿望又会是什么？

**Ashveil:** If money really could accomplish everything, the IPC wouldn't be eyeing this world. My bet? I'm sure that Fulwish wishes the tragedy fifteen years ago never happened.

> **不死途：**金钱要真是如此万能，公司也不会盯上这个世界了。至于满愿想要些什么，闭着眼我也能猜到，多半是想让十五年前的悲剧从没发生过。

**Ashveil:** Erasing history? Can The Laughter really pull it off?

> **不死途：**抹去历史，那位乐子神真的能做到吗？

**Kuchiba:** In past victory records, there have been countless and often bizarre instances where the victor used the Elation's authority to change reality...

> **朽叶：**过往的胜利记录中，胜者以欢愉权能改变现实的例子千奇百怪，无所不有……

**Kuchiba:** You have to understand: The Phantasmoon Games isn't just some wish-granting lamp. What makes it irresistible is that it turns the one who wishes into the genie.

> **朽叶：**但你要明白，「幻月游戏」可不是简简单单一盏许愿神灯。它最诱人的地方，莫过于让许愿者成为灯神本身。

**Ashveil:** That's quite alluring. Have you heard about the Noblesse Worm Aha made godlike? It became the happiest creature in existence, happy enough to burst apart, leaving no intact body behind.

> **不死途：**确实诱人，你听说过吗？曾经有一只被阿哈赐予神力的诺布莱斯蠕虫，它一定是世上最幸福的生物——幸福到四分五裂，死无全尸了。

**Kuchiba:** Your knowledge of these unofficial records is enough to rival ours. Since you see things so clearly, Mr. Ashveil, why sign up for the games?

> **朽叶：**你知道的野史不比我们少嘛。既然看得这么明白，不死途先生，你又是为什么要加入这场游戏？

**Ashveil:** Miss Kuchiba, is that a rhetorical question, or are you fishing for a confession?

> **不死途：**朽叶小姐，这是明知故问，还是想钓鱼执法？

**Kuchiba:** Fishing? Don't be afraid. Now that you're a Supplicant, public officials like me no longer have any authority over you.

> **朽叶：**钓鱼执法？别害怕，既然你成了谒者，我这样的公职人员对你也就毫无约束力了。

**Kuchiba:** I'm guessing what you want goes beyond a straightforward act of revenge, beyond a vigilante execution carried out in public.

> **朽叶：**我猜，你想要的不止是一场光明正大的复仇，一次公开执行的私刑。

**Kuchiba:** If you were only out to avenge friends, with or without the Phantasmoon Games, someone like you would toss laws and decorum aside and do what needs to be done.

> **朽叶：**如果只是想为死去的朋友雪恨，不管有没有幻月游戏，你这样的人都会无视一切法律和公序良俗的阻碍，做你想做的事。

**Kuchiba:** So tell me, Mr. Ashveil. What do you actually hope to get from the games?

> **朽叶：**不死途先生，我真的很好奇，你到底想从游戏中得到什么？

**Ashveil:** Consider it a mystery. If I make it to the end, you'll know the answer before Aha does.

> **不死途：**就把这个愿望当成悬念好了——如果有幸走到终盘，你会比阿哈先一步知道我的想法。

**Kuchiba:** Well then, I'd be honored. Just don't let your wish ruin our lives here.

> **朽叶：**那我还真是荣幸啊。只希望，你的愿望不会摧毁我们的日常。

**Ashveil:** Daily life here fits like a tailored prison uniform. Wear it too long, and you forget what your bones should feel like. It's time to break free. Look at these prisoners of routine before us...

> **不死途：**这里的日常生活是一件合身的囚服，穿久了会让人忘记骨头本该有的形状，也是时候该挣脱它了。看看眼前这些日常的囚犯……

**Ashveil:** Fulwish hasn't shown up yet. Let's talk with the members of the Research Society. Maybe we can coax something useful out of them.

> **不死途：**满愿还没到场，不如和幸研会的人聊聊，运气好还能从他们嘴里撬出来点东西。

### Listen to what the Smiles Society members are talking about

[Recap] Ashveil investigated members of the Smiles Society and discovered that they viewed everything as "happiness," even going so far as to consider the Dovebrook murder case a good thing for ridding the city of its scum.

*(Try to communicate)*

**Happy Office Worker:** I got called into another "chat" with the boss today. Never thought he'd care about me this much.

> **幸福的上班族：**又被领导找去谈话了，我以前根本想不到，他会这么关心我。

**Happy Office Worker:** Everyone is smiling at me. The office feels downright cheerful.

> **幸福的上班族：**同事们都对着我笑，办公室充满了欢愉的气氛。

**Happy Office Worker:** These days, I go home, change my baby boy's diaper, help my daughter with her homework, prepare tomorrow's lunch, and clean up the house... It's hard to describe how satisfying that feels.

> **幸福的上班族：**现在每天回到家，给小儿子换尿布，给大女儿指导作业，准备明天的午餐，再打扫好卫生，别提多有成就感了。

**Happy Office Worker:** When Ema left me, she must've left the kids to me for my own happiness.

> **幸福的上班族：**爱玛离开我一定也是为了我的幸福，才把孩子们留给我的。

**Happy Office Worker:** When I look back, I realize I was someone who had it good and I never understood how lucky I was.

> **幸福的上班族：**回想起来，以前的我真是个身在福中不知福的混账。

**Happy Patient:** Right, right? All thanks to Ms. Fulwish.

> **幸福的患者：**对吧对吧，多亏了满愿老师。

**Happy Patient:** Yesterday, during my tumor removal, the anesthesia wore off halfway through. I squinted and watched the doctors pull all kinds of things out of my stomach. There was this lump, still twitching when they cut it out!

> **幸福的患者：**昨天切除肿瘤的时候，麻药失效了，我眯着眼睛偷偷看着医生们把各种东西从我的肚子里取出来，有块肉团，它被割下来的时候还在动！

**Happy Patient:** I used to be too weak to even get out of bed, but after the Society's treatment, I'm almost ready to walk out of the hospital.

> **幸福的患者：**本来我已经是床都没法下的状态，自从接受了幸研会的治疗，都快康复出院了。

**Happy Office Worker:** Isn't that amazing? I kinda wanna see someone slice me open too. It should be fun.

> **幸福的上班族：**真好啊，我也想看看有人把我切开的样子，应该挺有意思的。

**Ashveil:** Hey, did you two hear about the Synwish Syndicate?

> **不死途：**两位，你们听说过共愿帮的事吗？

**Happy Office Worker:** You've come to the right person. My neighbor got a visit from their debt collectors. You should've heard the racket!

> **幸福的上班族：**这你问对人了，我邻居就被他们上门讨过债，那嗓门儿别提有多大了。

**Happy Office Worker:** I saw the news too. Those parasites who ruined the peace, who kept people from being happy. Gone! Wiped clean! Praise Aha!

> **幸福的上班族：**这几天的新闻我也看到了。夺走城市安宁的渣滓，阻碍别人得到幸福的家伙彻底消失了！真好啊，是阿哈保佑！

**Happy Office Worker:** Wait, they extorted you, too?

> **幸福的上班族：**怎么，你也被他们敲诈过？

**Ashveil:** Something like that. Still owed their Underboss tens of thousands of credits.

> **不死途：**差不多吧，我还欠他们二当家几万信用点呢。

**Happy Office Worker:** Ha ha, so your debt's wiped clean. Must feel pretty great, huh?

> **幸福的上班族：**哈哈，债务一笔勾销了，那你现在应该很幸福吧。

[Recap] Members of the Smiles Society claimed that Aha was speaking to them. If Ashveil and Kuchiba could not hear it, it was because they were not happy enough yet.

*(Try to communicate)*

**Happy Youth:** We meet again! Hey, Lady Mechatron, how've you been?

> **幸福的少年：**又见面了！机铠大姐姐，最近过得还好吗？

**Happy Mechatron:** Good! Since I joined the Research Society, I've made so many new friends.

> **幸福的机铠：**嗯，加入研究会后，我多了好多新的朋友。

**Happy Mechatron:** In the past, I struggled with mobility, I always had to work three or four times harder than the other mechatrons just to keep up.

> **幸福的机铠：**以前，我的行动能力很弱，总要花上比其他机铠多几倍的时间，才能跟上他们。

**Happy Mechatron:** A lot of imagenae think factory-made types like me don't count as their own, and humans look at us and see soulless machines.

> **幸福的机铠：**很多幻造种觉得我们这些工厂制造的和他们不是同类，人类也常常因为外形把我们看做没有感情的机器。

**Happy Mechatron:** If every sentient being has a home, then even the scrapyard is closed to me. I'm not even considered recyclable waste.

> **幸福的机铠：**如果每个智慧生命都有能回的家，对我来说，就连报废流水线都不属于我，因为我甚至不是可回收垃圾。

**Happy Mechatron:** But here in the Society, no one cares what I am or what I can do. They just see me as a normal person.

> **幸福的机铠：**可在研究会里，大家都不会关心我的身份，我的能力，我只是一个普普通通的「人」。

**Happy Youth:** Congrats. You've found your home.

> **幸福的少年：**恭喜，你找到自己的家了。

**Happy Mechatron:** Mm, Aha said the same. THEY and everyone here are my family.

> **幸福的机铠：**嗯，阿哈也这么说，祂和这里的每个人都是我的家人。

**Kuchiba:** Aha talks to you?

> **朽叶：**阿哈在对你们说话？

**Happy Youth:** Of course! You mean you can't hear THEM? Guess you're not happy enough yet.

> **幸福的少年：**对呀，难道你们听不见？一定是你们还不够幸福。

**Happy Mechatron:** Don't worry. Once you join the Society, you will. No pain, no tears, just pure joy. The Society makes everyone happy.

> **幸福的机铠：**没关系，只要加入研究会，你们会听见的。没有痛苦，没有眼泪，只有快乐，研究会让所有人都幸福。

### Wait for Fulwish's presentation to start

[Recap] Fulwish delivered a speech to the crowd, rallying them to oppose the IPC's governance of Planarcadia and join the Smiles Society in pursuit of true elation and happiness to rebuild Planarcadia into a place where smiles never fade.

*(Go to mission location)*

**Ashveil:** Looks like it's about time. If the star of the show doesn't show up soon, the crowd's gonna burst with anticipation.

> **不死途：**时间差不多了，主演再不登场，就有点吊人胃口了。

**Shout From Nearby:** Everyone, please make some room, for Ms. Fulwish.

> **不远处的喊声：**大家站开点，请给满愿女士留个位置。

**Fulwish:** By the Master of Elation, may THEY bless all souls and grant them smiles. Are you all happy today?

> **满愿：**欢愉之主在上，愿池普救世人，赐予微笑。大家今天过得幸福吗？

**Happy Crowd:** Very happy!

> **幸福的人们：**非常幸福！

**Fulwish:** Thank you for your smiles. They're like lamps in the dark, so precious.

> **满愿：**谢谢你们的笑容。它们就像黑暗中的灯火，如此珍贵。

**Fulwish:** But before we celebrate the Elation, let me share a memory drenched in tears. It once dragged me into the abyss, yet it also taught me what the Elation really means.

> **满愿：**然而，在我们赞颂欢愉之前，请允许我分享一段浸透泪水的回忆。它曾让我坠入深渊，却也最终让我理解了何谓「欢愉」。

**Fulwish:** As you all know, fifteen years ago, during the Phantasmoon Games, the shadow cast by the Herald of Death swallowed this place whole.

> **满愿：**众所周知，十五年前的幻月游戏中，告死魔的阴影吞噬了此地。

**Fulwish:** That day, I lost my parents, my classmates, and the others... The morning greetings, the little daily chatter... All went silent under that crimson dusk.

> **满愿：**在那一天，我失去了父母、同学、失去了很多人…那些出门前的问候、日常琐碎的唠叨，都在那个血红的黄昏里陷入沉寂。

**Fulwish:** After that, for a very, very long time...

> **满愿：**那之后，很长很长一段时间……

**Fulwish:** ...All that remained of my world: a door shut tight, a room steeped in silence, and a wound in my heart that would never heal.

> **满愿：**…我的世界只剩下一扇紧闭的门，一个死寂的房间，还有心底永不愈合的伤口。

**Fulwish:** Just hearing the words "Herald of Death" made me shake uncontrollably. I even...

> **满愿：**仅是听到「告死魔」这三个字，都会让我止不住发抖，我甚至……

**Fulwish:** ...I even once thought the only escape was to follow the dead and leave with them.

> **满愿：**…甚至一度以为，只有随那些离世的人一起离开，才是唯一的解脱。

**Fulwish:** Back then, I was just an empty shell, breathing, beating, but lifeless inside. And just as my soul was about to fade away, an old lady from the mutual aid society knocked on my door.

> **满愿：**那个时候，我活成了一具只剩呼吸和心跳的空壳。就在灵魂即将彻底熄灭的那一刻，是互助会一位慈祥的婆婆，敲响了我的门。

**Fulwish:** She didn't offer empty words of comfort. She just looked me in the eyes and asked—

> **满愿：**那位婆婆没说太多安慰的空话，她只是看着我的眼睛,问我——

**Fulwish:** "Child, would you... try stepping outside this door? Out there are many people, broken by pain just like you. But together, we can start picking up the pieces... One by one."

> **满愿：**「孩子，你愿不愿意…试试走出这扇门？这里有许多和你一样，被痛苦捏碎了心的人。我们在一起，试着…一点点把碎片捡起来。」

**Fulwish:** She smiled at me. A warm, gentle smile. And in that smile, I felt the Master of Elation grant me a reason to live.

> **满愿：**她给了我一个温暖的微笑，我明白，那一刻欢愉之主借她的笑容，给了我活下去的理由。

**Fulwish:** At the mutual aid society, I saw countless souls scarred by fate. Some bore wounds beyond healing, and some had lost their loved ones. Each of us bore a different wound, but every wound cut deep.

> **满愿：**在互助会里，我看到无数被命运深深伤害的灵魂。有人遭受无法治愈的伤残、有人失去了挚爱，我们的伤痛各不相同，但每个人的伤痛都深可见骨。

**Fulwish:** And yet, what truly moved me wasn't the pain. It was how, beneath those scars, they still reached out to one another, offering a smile to a child who'd stopped crying, lending a guiding hand to someone lost.

> **满愿：**但令我感到震撼的，并非残忍的痛苦，而是在这样的伤痕之下，他们依然在努力互相搀扶，为一个擦干眼泪的孩子露出笑容，为一个迷途者伸出援手。

**Fulwish:** They taught me something precious: Pain can destroy a person, but the wish to bring Elation to others can spark the fire of rebirth. That was when Aha's voice echoed again in the silence of my heart.

> **满愿：**他们教会了我，痛苦可以摧毁一个人，但对他人欢愉的期盼，也能点燃重生的火种。就在那时，阿哈的声音才重新在我死寂的心中响起。

**Fulwish:** The smiles passed from one another through mutual aid is Aha's most precious blessing. It does not belong only in the small room of a support group, it yearns to illuminate hearts lost in darkness.

> **满愿：**这抹由互助传递的微笑，是阿哈赐予我们最珍贵的礼物。它不应只停留在互助会的小小房间里，它渴望照亮更多被阴霾笼罩的心灵。

**Fulwish:** Each time we share a true smile, it's as if we hear Aha's voice. And when that voice rings out, all suffering fades, all pain is healed, and everyone knows what true happiness feels like.

> **满愿：**每当送出一个发自内心的笑容，我们就仿佛听到了阿哈的声音，而它的声音响起，一切苦难都成为过去，一切伤痛都被弭平，每一个人都体会到了何为真正的幸福。

**Fulwish:** That's why the Research Society of Happy Smiles was founded! I will share this divine smile with all of you!

> **满愿：**为此「幸福微笑研究会」成立了！我要将这抹来自神明的微笑传递给你们。

**Cheering Audience:** (We support you, Ms. Fulwish! Thank you, Ms. Fulwish! Please bring us your smile!)

> **欢呼的听众：**我们支持你！满愿小姐！——谢谢你！满愿小姐！——请带给我们微笑！

**Fulwish:** Everyone, I have questions for you. When was the last time you truly smiled? How long has it been since you felt happy from the bottom of your heart?

> **满愿：**诸位，我想要问问你们，上一次露出发自内心的微笑是什么时候？距离你们上次感受到幸福，又过去了多久？

**Fulwish:** Minado, do you ever feel like your days are looping endlessly, like an old silent film, repeating until it suffocates you? And when you realize that loop might never end, does despair flood in like the tide?

> **满愿：**米纳多先生，你是不是常常觉得日子如同循环播放的默片，重复得令人窒息？一想到这样的循环可能永无止境，绝望便如潮水般涌上心头？

**Numb Audience:** ...You're right.

> **麻木的听众：**…您说的没错。

**Fulwish:** Miss Belle Woods, you left school full of dreams, only to have your résumé rejected again and again. You told your parents you were "busy in Seafeld City," hiding your heart-breaking loneliness behind that lie.

> **满愿：**贝尔伍德小姐，你怀揣着梦想走出校门，现实却将你的简历一次次退回。为了不让远方的父母担忧，你不得不编织一个在海原市忙碌的谎言，独自咽下苦涩的孤独？

**Distressed Audience:** ...Please help me!

> **忧愁的听众：**…请帮帮我！

**Fulwish:** And Tyjah... Did the cold exclusion at school, and the indifference of the adults who should have cared, build a wall around your heart, one that made you afraid to open yourself up to the world again?

> **满愿：**还有泰加同学，校园里冰冷的排挤，师长视若无睹的冷漠，是否在你心底筑起了一堵高墙，让你害怕再次向世界敞开自己？

**Cowardly Audience:** Y—Yes...

> **懦弱的听众：**是、是的。

**Fulwish:** When we were born, no one had to teach us how to smile. But who would've thought that one day, even smiling would become so hard?

> **满愿：**我们来到这世上，无需任何人教导，都会对他人露出笑容。那时的我们可曾想过，有一天连这样简简单单的微笑会都变得勉强。

**Fulwish:** Once, our world was blessed by Aha's grace, a gentle World in Canvas, painted in Elation. For centuries, humans and imagenaes lived freely together in that eternal haven—

> **满愿：**我们的世界曾因受阿哈的恩典，被温柔地描摹于画卷中。在那个由欢愉主宰的画中世界，人类与幻造生灵不受拘束地在那片幻想田园中和平共度了无数个百年——

**Fulwish:** Until the Interastral Peace Corporation violently tore us away from that sanctuary! They brought us their so-called interastral civilization, imposed their so-called standards of happiness... and told us that life's ultimate joy lies in self-focus, self-indulgence, and self-satisfaction!

> **满愿：**直到星际和平公司将我们粗暴地拖离了那片净土！他们为我们带来所谓的星际文明，他们为我们制定了所谓的幸福标准…他们告诉我们，人生的终极欢愉是自我关注、自我享受和自我满足！

**Fulwish:** They flooded us with dazzling material desires, blinding our hearts. They fed arrogance and prejudice into our minds through the network, making us chase pleasure for ourselves, and forget that true Elation is found when we smile together.

> **满愿：**他们打造五光十色的物质洪流，蒙蔽了我们的心灵，用网络向每个人的内心灌输傲慢和偏见，让我们竭尽所能取悦自己，却忘掉了真正的欢愉是和身旁的人一起微笑。

**Fulwish:** Wishpower has turned into fuel. Credits jingle where heartfelt laughter used to ring. We drown in anxiety, competition, and an endless sense of not being enough. And in all that noise, we can no longer hear Aha's gentle voice!

> **满愿：**愿力沦为了能源，信用点叮当作响，取代了发自肺腑的笑声。我们沉溺在焦虑、攀比和永无止境的匮乏感中，再也听不见阿哈温柔的呼唤！

**Fulwish:** Elation, the truest light of life itself, is being eaten away, and replaced by the external!

> **满愿：**欢愉，这生命最本真的光芒，正在被外物所侵蚀、取代！

**Unhappy Audience:** You said it! I work myself to death for scraps, and my boss doesn't even notice!

> **不幸福的人：**说的没错！我成天为那点可怜的工资拼了老命，还换不来上司一点肯定！

**Unhappy Audience:** And those useless loudmouths online. They do nothing with their lives, yet constantly judge everyone else!

> **不幸福的人：**我也受不了，网络上那帮傻瓜明明一事无成却整天评头论足指手画脚！

**Unhappy Audience:** The IPC is nothing but a nest of vampires! They're feeding off of us!

> **不幸福的人：**我不喜欢公司，公司就是一群吸血鬼！只知道骑在我们头上作威作福！

**Unhappy Crowd:** Ms. Fulwish! Please tell us how to be happy!

> **不幸福的人们：**满愿女士！请告诉我们怎么才能获得幸福！

**Fulwish:** Look around you! This land was once blessed by the Master of Elation, and yet now, it's overflowing with misery. Everyone's chasing their own happiness, but in doing so, they wound others without even noticing.

> **满愿：**看！在这片曾被欢愉之主深深祝福的土地上，竟充斥着如此多的不快乐。每个人明明都在追求属于自我的幸福，却让他人受尽伤害。

**Fulwish:** Do you remember the moment I found happiness again? It was when someone, someone who was suffering just as I was, smiled at me.

> **满愿：**诸位，还记得我重获幸福的那个瞬间吗？那个瞬间，我得到了来自他人的微笑。

**Fulwish:** Even through their pain, they gave me that smile. A smile filled with blessings. That smile saved my life.

> **满愿：**明明自己饱受苦难，却想要用微笑让我幸福哪怕一瞬间，我就是被这样含着祝福的微笑拯救的！

**Fulwish:** So listen well. Only by opening your heart and sharing your smile with another can you truly grasp what happiness means. It isn't just giving a smile, it's embracing the power that Aha bestowed upon us to smile.

> **满愿：**诸位，我想告诉你们，只有敞开心扉，将微笑给予他人，我们才能体会到幸福的真谛。这不仅仅是送出一个微笑，而是拥抱了阿哈赐予我们的，送出微笑的能力。

**Fulwish:** Join the Research Society of Happy Smiles! I'll help you rediscover the smile buried within yourself, and Aha will grant your life a transformation!

> **满愿：**加入幸福微笑研究会，我会帮助各位找到自己生命中的微笑，阿哈会赐予你们生命的转变！

**Fulwish:** Our mission doesn't end with finding our own smiles. We must bring them to the world, and spread true happiness across all existence.

> **满愿：**我们的使命绝不仅限于找到微笑，我们还要把微笑带给这个世界，给予这个世界真正的幸福。

**Fulwish:** While others pray to the Aeons for their own desires, we, the true followers of Aha, carry a higher calling: to fulfill THEIR wish!

> **满愿：**当世人向星神祈求满足自己的「愿」时，我们—―阿哈真正的追随者，将肩负起更神圣的使命：我们将去实现祂的「愿」！

**Ashveil:** ...To fulfill Aha's wish?

> **不死途：**…实现「阿哈的愿望」？

**Fulwish:** Yes, to still every cry of sorrow. To wipe away every bitter tear. We will build, no... rebuild a world where smiles never fade, and Elation never dies!

> **满愿：**抚平世间每一声哀伤的哭啼，拭去每一滴苦涩的泪水！我们将亲手创造一—不，是重建——一个让笑容永不凋零,欢愉永不枯竭的世界！

**Happy Crowd:** Eternal happiness!

> **幸福的人们：**永远幸福！

**Happy Crowd:** Eternal laughter!

> **幸福的人们：**永远欢笑！

**Happy Crowd:** HAHAHAHAHAHAHAHAHAHAH...

> **幸福的人们：**哈哈哈哈哈哈哈……

### Try to find out more about Fulwish

**Kuchiba:** The speech is over. What's your take, Detective?

> **朽叶：**演讲结束了。名侦探，你怎么看？

**Ashveil:** She's so far gone it's hard to make sense of it.

> **不死途：**疯得太过离谱，无法判断。

**Kuchiba:** Then let's not overthink it. Let's talk to her directly.

> **朽叶：**那就直接了当和她谈谈吧。

[Recap] Ashveil and Kuchiba interrogated Fulwish about the Dovebrook murder case. Fulwish seemed well-prepared and voluntarily revealed the existence of the "New Herald of Death" to the crowd, triggering panic among those present.

*(Approach Fulwish)*

**Kuchiba:** Ms. Fulwish, I'm Kuchiba from the Department of Aberration Defense. I'd like to have a chat with you.

> **朽叶：**满愿女士，我是「异常防御部」的朽叶，想占用您一点时间和您聊聊。

**Fulwish:** I see. You're here about the Synwish Syndicate case, aren't you?

> **满愿：**我明白了。你们是为共愿帮的案子来的吧？

**Ashveil:** So you were expecting us.

> **不死途：**看来你早有准备啊。

**Fulwish:** I heard the news. The entire Synwish Syndicate... massacred in Dovebrook District. Such a cursed place...

> **满愿：**我听说了，鸽川区的共愿帮全员被暴徒谋害了。真是片多灾多难的地方。

**Fulwish:** They may not have been saints, but I still pray for them. May their souls rest in peace.

> **满愿：**虽然他们算不上什么守法公民，但我还是会为这些人祈祷的，愿他们的灵魂早日安息。

**Ashveil:** Ah, how merciful of you.

> **不死途：**哦,您还真是慈悲为怀啊。

**Ashveil:** Though, rumor has it the Research Society of Happy Smiles had a few run-ins with them? Regarding recruitments, donations, turf... Sounds like plenty of reasons for conflict, don't you think?

> **不死途：**我怎么听说，幸研会和共愿帮在鸽川区有几次摩擦呢？招募人员、捐献和地盘…光想想，你们两方的冲突就有不少吧？

**Fulwish:** Are you suggesting... we had the Synwish Syndicate killed? For conflicted interests?

> **满愿：**您是在暗示…幸研会的人因为利益冲突对共愿帮的人下了毒手？

**Ashveil:** Wait, that's not what I said.

> **不死途：**等会儿，我可什么都没说。

**Fulwish:** You know nothing about our finances. Every expense is covered by a charitable foundation established with the compensation from fifteen years ago, we don't need a single cent from our members.

> **满愿：**你对幸研会的资金状况一无所知，组织的所有开销全都来自十五年前那笔赔偿所建立的慈善基金。我们根本用不着会众捐献一分一毫。

**Fulwish:** When I heard about this tragedy, I personally allocated relief money for the victims' families, to keep order and peace in Dovebrook.

> **满愿：**听闻这件血案发生后，我已拨了一笔钱，用于安抚死难者的家属，维护鸽川的秩序。

**Fulwish:** Tell me, Miss Kuchiba... Someone copied the Herald of Death and slaughtered dozens. What will your Department of Aberration Defense do this time?

> **满愿：**我倒是很好奇，朽叶小姐，有人模仿告死魔犯下如此可怕的血案，这一次「异常防御部」又打算做些什么呢？

**Kuchiba:** We never released that detail. How do you know someone's imitating the Herald of Death?

> **朽叶：**我们从没对外放出过这场凶案的具体细节，您是怎么知道「有人模仿告死魔犯案」的？

**Fulwish:** Isn't that what you've been waiting for? For the killer to slip up, so you can swoop in and tie everything up nicely?

> **满愿：**你们不就在等这一刻吗？犯人露出破绽，好当场拿下，让一切平安无事。

**Fulwish:** Fifteen years have passed. You don't seriously believe we're still living in an age where information can be sealed off at will, do you? From the very day the murders occurred, the news has long since spread online.

> **满愿：**十五年过去了，你们不会天真地以为自己还身处于一个可以随便封锁消息的年代吧？打从凶案发生那天起，消息在网络上早已传开了。

**Fulwish:** The Synwish Syndicate paid a fortune for some ancient mask, and the next thing you know, they're all dead. The scene painted red with traces of the Bloody Bloom.

> **满愿：**听说共愿帮的人花重金购入了一张古老的面具。消息刚走漏，就落得个满门皆亡的下场。案发现场到处是「绽放血手」的痕迹。

**Kuchiba:** Please pay no attention to baseless rumors...

> **朽叶：**请不要随意听信无关的谣言……

**Fulwish:** Rumors? And yet you're here. That alone proves one thing: The rumors are true... and the New Herald of Death really does exist.

> **满愿：**怎么会？你们的到来至少证实了一件事：那些消息是真的…「新告死魔」，真的存在。

**Fulwish:** Otherwise, why do you suspect me? "Only someone who knows the original case could recreate it so perfectly," right?

> **满愿：**不然，你们为何会将我列为嫌疑人？难道不是因为「只有当事人才能复现当年的凶案细节」吗？

**Fulwish:** Everyone! You heard our conversation, didn't you?

> **满愿：**各位！你们都听到了我和这两位的对话了吗？

**Fulwish:** The Herald of Death has returned! He's here in Dovebrook! He's slain the entire Synwish Syndicate! He's back to relive the carnage of the Phantasmoon Games!

> **满愿：**告死魔回来了！就在鸽川，他杀害了数十名共愿帮成员！他回来了！他要再次重演幻月游戏的腥风血雨！

**Panicked Newbie:** The Herald of Death? Didn't the officials declare him executed? How has he appeared again?

> **恐慌的新人：**告死魔？官方不是宣布已经把它处刑了吗？为什么他又出现了？

**Frightened Newbie:** He's immortal!? A demon!?

> **畏惧的新人：**这家伙真的是不死的恶鬼吗？！

**Flustered Newbie:** He's back for revenge! He'll take revenge on everyone! We're doomed!

> **失措的新人：**他一定是回来复仇的！向我们所有人复仇！我们完了！

**Happy Mechatron:** Aren't gang members the very people who steal smiles from others? And isn't the best way to make bad people "happy" to erase them from the world? Wouldn't that be doing something good?

> **幸福的机铠：**帮派分子，不是夺走笑容的坏人吗？让坏人幸福的最好方法，就是让他们从世界上消失，这不是做了件好事吗？

**Happy Youth:** Yeah, Sister Mechatron is right! Those thugs deserved it!

> **幸福的少年：**机铠大姐姐说得对，这些帮派份子死有余辜罢了。

**Happy Office Worker:** Back from the dead? Lies! The IPC kept him alive, to wield him as their weapon!

> **幸福的上班族：**死人复活？骗鬼呢！我看是公司当年根本就没杀他！留着当黑手套吧？

**Angry Passerby:** You useless officials! Fifteen years ago, you failed and let him get away, and that's why he's back again now!

> **愤怒的路人：**都是你们这些当官的无能！十五年前抓不住，现在又让他冒出来了！

**Fulwish:** Everyone, there's no need to panic!

> **满愿：**诸位——大家不必慌张！

[Recap] Fulwish reassured the crowd, declaring that the Smiles Society would protect this world and the happy would never die. The crowd erupted into a frenzy, chanting "Happiness! Happiness!"

**Fulwish:** Remember who you are! You are those who live beneath Aha's brilliance, who have claimed happiness and cast off the shackles of suffering!

> **满愿：**记住你们是谁！你们是沐浴在阿哈光辉下，已经寻获幸福、不再被痛苦束缚的人！

**Fulwish:** Once, the Herald of Death fed on our fear and tears. That was the source of his power!

> **满愿：**曾经，告死魔以我们的恐惧和泪水为食粮，那是他力量的源泉！

**Fulwish:** Look at the smiles on the faces of those around you! There is no fear left in our hearts, no tears left in our eyes! All that remains is resolve, happiness, and faith in the Elation!

> **满愿：**但如今，看看你们自己！看看周围同伴的笑脸！我们心中已无恐惧，眼中已无泪水！我们只有坚定、只有幸福、只有对欢愉的信仰！

**Fulwish:** The Research Society of Happy Smiles will protect this world!

> **满愿：**幸福微笑研究会会保护好这个世界的！

**Fulwish:** Etch this into your hearts: The happy do not die. For even death cannot touch a heart filled with happiness!

> **满愿：**牢牢记住我的话——幸福的人是不会死的！因为我们的心，连死亡都无法侵蚀！

**Ashveil:** What's going on? There's something very wrong about this woman.

> **不死途：**怎么回事？这个女人…很不对劲。

**Panicked Newbie:** Yes! Yes! As long as we're happy enough, there's nothing to fear! We won't die!

> **恐慌的新人：**对！对！只要足够幸福，就什么都不怕！就不会死！

**Happy Patient:** Happiness! Happiness is our weapon! Happiness will destroy the Herald of Death!

> **幸福的患者：**幸福！幸福是我们的武器！幸福能杀死告死魔！

**Happy Mechatron:** Happiness will save this world! Operation: Spread Happiness!

> **幸福的机铠：**幸福能拯救这个世界。执行方案：传播幸福！

**Joyful Youth:** Let happiness infect everyone, and spread to every corner of the sky! Make the Phantasmoon smile! Make Lord Aha laugh until THEY cry for us!

[Recap] Fulwish said she could not provide Ashveil and Kuchiba with any useful information, but she would work with the Smiles Society to do everything she could to protect Planarcadia's future and the smiles of its citizens. Kuchiba thanked Fulwish for cooperating with the investigation.

**Happy Crowd:** Infect the Phantasmoon with happiness! Happiness! Happiness!

> **幸福的人们：**用幸福感染幻月！幸福！幸福！

**Kuchiba:** Silence!

> **朽叶：**——肃静！

**Fulwish:** My apologies, officers. I can't provide anything useful for now, but rest assured. I'll cooperate with any investigation from the Department of Aberration Defense or the Security Bureau. And of course, I'll fulfill my civic duty...

> **满愿：**两位，很抱歉，虽然我暂时没法提供什么有用信息，但请放心…我愿意接受来自异常防御部或治安局的—切调查。同时我也会尽我的公民义务……

**Fulwish:** Whether it's the newly emerged Herald of Death, or the various upheavals surrounding the Phantasmoon Games, the Research Society of Happy Smiles will handle them appropriately. What we protect is the future, and the smiles of Planarcadia.

> **满愿：**无论是这个新出现的告死魔，还是幻月游戏的种种变故，幸福微笑研究会都会妥善处理。我们守护的，是二相乐园的未来和笑容。

**Fulwish:** Or perhaps... after all this, you too have felt that yearning within your hearts? Would you like to join us, to embrace eternal happiness?

> **满愿：**还是说——经历这一切，你们也感受到了内心的渴求？想要加入我们，拥抱这份永恒的幸福？

**Ashveil:** I'll pass. But I've got a feeling... we'll meet again.

> **不死途：**不了。我有预感，我们之后还会再见的。

**Kuchiba:** Ms. Fulwish, thank you for your cooperation. Hopefully, next time we meet, it won't be like this.

> **朽叶：**满愿女士，感谢您配合我们的调查，希望…下一次，我们不会再以这种方式见面。

**Fulwish:** May the Master of Elation watch over all, and bless you with THEIR everlasting smile.

> **满愿：**欢愉之主在上，愿祂普救世人，赐予两位微笑。

[Recap] Ashveil sensed something was wrong with Fulwish and suggested working with Kuchiba to learn more about the Smiles Society from Cinderborne.

Ashveil and Kuchiba left the gathering...

**Distorted Echo:** Let go of your obsession. Let go of the shadow in your arms. Let go of that false sense of responsibility.

> **扭曲的回声：**放下你的偏执吧，放下手臂里的影子，放下那虚伪的责任感。

**Distorted Echo:** You're the one who should be dead, aren't you?

> **扭曲的回声：**明明你才是最该死掉的那个，不是吗？

**Distorted Echo:** Come, join your brothers. Let the gears grind to a halt, let the hands fall still, and end that life of yours that was never meant to be.

> **扭曲的回声：**来吧，和兄弟们一起。让齿轮停转，指针落下，停滞你那不该存续的生命。

**Ashveil:** Sorry. That day will come, but for now, there are more important things I must do.

> **不死途：**抱歉，那一天会来的。但现在，还有更重要的事。

**Kuchiba:** What did you say...?

> **朽叶：**你说什么……？

**Ashveil:** Nothing, never mind. Looks like Fulwish is a much slyer fish than expected.

> **不死途：**不，没事。一时间走了神。看来满愿这条鱼，比想象中狡猾得多。

**Ashveil:** The bait was there, but she ignored it, and instead she stirred up a whirlpool.

> **不死途：**咱们丢下了饵，她却不咬钩，反而激起了一池聒噪的水花。

**Kuchiba:** Quite the opposite, actually. Not only did she take our bait, but she also flat out told us she couldn't care less about the hook.

> **朽叶：**恰恰相反。鱼儿不仅咬了饵，还大大方方地告诉我们，她根本不在乎我们的钩子。

**Ashveil:** Oh? Was there something I missed?

> **不死途：**哦？有什么我漏掉的细节吗？

**Kuchiba:** The Bloody Bloom mark from the Synwish Syndicate massacre. How did she know that detail, when we specifically obfuscated it with "special mark" even in our internal reports?

> **朽叶：**共愿帮灭门案中的「绽放血手」的印记，她准确说出了这个细节。但这一次，连我们内部下发的简报都刻意用了「特殊标记」去代称，她是怎么知道得一清二楚的？

**Kuchiba:** She broadcasted this information word for word, and even used it to stir up public sentiment, scattering it across the place like tossing blood-soaked feed into a fishpond. Now stopping the rumors from spreading will be even harder.

> **朽叶：**不仅精准地说了出来，这个女人还把这条消息当作煽动群情的道具，抛得满场都是，就像在养鱼的池子里撒了一把带血的鱼食。这下要阻止谣言飞传可就更麻烦了。

**Ashveil:** Well, maybe she has her connections, or maybe she made a lucky guess as one of the victims. Regardless, she's right about one thing: Trying to stop something from spreading today sure is a lot more difficult than fifteen years ago.

> **不死途：**也许是人家路子野，也许她作为当年的受害者蒙对了。有一点这个女人没说错，眼下不是十五年前，封锁消息的难度不可同日而语。

**Kuchiba:** So, Mr. Ashveil, did your "lucky" radar start blaring when it came to Fulwish

> **朽叶：**说说吧，不死途先生，你的「幸运」雷达对满愿有没有警报大作？

**Ashveil:** There's something very wrong about that woman.

> **不死途：**这个女人，很不对劲。

**Ashveil:** You saw the way she played the crowd? I guarantee you that it had nothing to do with her public speaking skills. She could very well be a Supplicant in disguise.

> **不死途：**瞧见刚才她煽动群众的手法了吗？我敢打包票，那绝不是什么演讲技巧。也许她是个尚未揭露身份的谒者也说不定。

**Ashveil:** Still, this whole run has been smooth to an unbelievable degree. We got our bearings in no time at all, so let's keep our prized fish in the pond for now. Fulwish is not going anywhere.

> **不死途：**无论如何，这一路简直顺利得不像话了。咱们一下子就找到了方向，就先让满愿这条大鱼在池塘里呆着好了，她跑不了的。

**Kuchiba:** You're right. It's too risky to make a direct move against a well-respected philanthropist and media tycoon. Maybe we should start with the people around her.

> **朽叶：**嗯，贸然对一位备受爱戴的慈善家和媒体大亨动手实在风险太高，也许可以先从她的周遭动手。

**Ashveil:** There were people from all walks of life at the gathering just now. Workers, suits, students, the elderly, and even mechatrons...

**Ashveil:** Judging by their crazed enthusiasm, I highly doubt they'd be willing to testify against Fulwish.

> **不死途：**你瞧见刚才集会上的人，从哪个椅角杳晃冒出来的都有：穿工装的、穿西装的、学生、老人、甚至还有机铠……

**Ashveil:** But... I have my ways.

**Kuchiba:** Mr. Ashveil, coercive interrogation is strictly against the rules. Please refrain from doing it somewhere I can—

**Ashveil:** Don't get the wrong idea. I meant an old friend that I spotted at the gathering. By the way, ever watch Daybreak Squadron? It's on Sunday nights at eight.

**Kuchiba:** I thought you weren't a TV person?

> **朽叶：**不死途先生，刑讯逼供是明令禁止的，请你在我看不到的地方……

**Ashveil:** Not ME! Mister N! He watches it!

**Kuchiba:** ...Your monkey's quite the child at heart.

> **朽叶：**你不是不看电视节目吗？

**Ashveil:** Ugh, anyway. Come on, let's go get an "autograph" from our heroic film star.

> **不死途：**跟我无关！是老白！老白会准时收看啊！

### Find that special "friend"

*(If you try to leave the area)*

**Kuchiba:** Time to get to work, detective.

> **朽叶：**…你的猴子还真有童心啊。

**Ashveil:** Hmph...

*(Approach Cinderborne)*

**Kuchiba:** Is that him?

> **朽叶：**就是签名那个年轻人？

**Ashveil:** Shh, don't interrupt.

> **不死途：**嘘，别打岔。

### Follow Cinderborne's footsteps

*(Follow into the alley)*

**Kuchiba:** What, you lost him?

> **朽叶：**怎么，跟丢了？

**Ashveil:** Not even close.

> **不死途：**没那回事。

[Recap] Cinderborne claimed that the Smiles Society helped him move past the tragedy from fifteen years ago, and he would not reveal anything about it. When more members of the Smiles Society appeared, Ashveil decided to teach them a lesson.

*(Go to mission location)*

**Cinderborne:** Why are you two... following me?

> **灰烬：**你们俩…为什么一路跟着我？

**Ashveil:** Oh~ We're just two grown-up fans of the Daybreak Squadron trying to get an autograph from big brother Magnus.

> **不死途：**哎呀，只是两个破晓战队的成年粉丝想问真弘大哥哥你讨个签名。

**Cinderborne:** Mister... Ashveil! Don't prank me like that. You really scared me.

> **灰烬：**不死途…先生！你开什么玩笑，可把我吓坏了。

**Ashveil:** Really? Cinderborne... the peacekeeper of our city, vanquisher of evil fiends, is scared of his own fans?

> **不死途：**不会吧，守护城市和平，直面邪恶爪牙的「灰烬战士」居然会被自己的粉丝吓一跳。

**Ashveil:** Long time no see, Magnus.

> **不死途：**好久不见了，真弘。

**Cinderborne:** You're not here for autographs, are you? I saw you two at the gathering. I don't understand. Why are you investigating Miss Fulwish?

> **灰烬：**你们不是来要签名的，对吧?我在集会现场看到你们俩了。我不明白，为什么要调查满愿小姐？

**Cinderborne:** She's been nothing but a kind, lawful person who spreads happiness to others.

> **灰烬：**她一向是个遵纪守法、传播幸福的大好人。

**Ashveil:** You heard about what happened with Synwish Syndicate, I assume?

> **不死途：**你知道共愿帮的事情了，对吧？

**Cinderborne:** Then... was Miss Fulwish telling the truth?

> **灰烬：**所以，满愿小姐说的是真的吗？

**Ashveil:** The perpetrator left the same signature as the Herald of Death did fifteen years ago.

> **不死途：**杀死他们的凶手留下了与十五年前告死魔一样的痕迹。

**Cinderborne:** So... did that monster really kill Mr. Polwave and Caustide!?

> **灰烬：**所以，池波先生和恶水大哥…真的是死在那个怪物手里的？！

**Cinderborne:** Fifteen years and he's back... This demon... How many more lives must he take?

> **灰烬：**十五年过去了，他又回来了…这个恶魔，到底还想夺走多少人的性命？

**Kuchiba:** Mr. Magnus, you're one of the survivors of the incident, right?

> **朽叶：**真弘先生，你也是十五年前那场悲剧的幸存者之一？

**Cinderborne:** ...Yes. That monster took my parents that day.

> **灰烬：**嗯…就在那一天我的父母，都被那个混蛋夺走了。

**Cinderborne:** But... what's the Synwish Syndicate case got to do with Miss Fulwish?

> **回家：**但是…共愿帮的案件和满愿小姐又有什么关系？

**Ashveil:** Because that Herald of Death was executed fifteen years ago. That's for certain. The Synwish Syndicate case, on the other hand, has copycat written all over it — and Fulwish is a suspect.

> **不死途：**因为十五年前那个告死魔已经被处决了，千真万确。而共愿帮的案子，更像是模仿犯的手笔——满愿，有嫌疑。

**Cinderborne:** So just because we're survivors, we automatically become suspects? What, are you going to interrogate me too?

> **灰烬：**幸存者就该被怀疑？那我岂不也该受你们审问？

**Ashveil:** If that's what you want, Miss Kuchiba can arrange for it later. For now, I just want to ask you a few questions about the Smiles Society.

> **不死途：**如果你这么想，朽叶小姐之后可以问讯你。现在，我只想向你了解一些幸研会的事情。

**Cinderborne:** It doesn't matter if you have a few or a hundred questions. I have nothing to say to you.

> **灰烬：**无论你们想问什么，我都无可奉告。

**Cinderborne:** Fifteen years ago, I was left all alone in this world... The so-called relatives closed in like a pack of hyenas, their eyes fixed solely on the compensation smeared with my parents' blood.

> **灰烬：**十五年前的那天，我被孤零零地…抛弃在这个世界上了。那些…所谓的亲戚像闻到了血腥味的鬣狗一样围了上来,他们眼里只有那笔沾着我父母血的赔偿金！

**Cinderborne:** Abuse, deception, expulsion... to them, I was nothing more than a walking money bag, an eyesore, a "jinx."

> **灰烬：**打骂、欺骗、驱赶…我在这些人眼里，不过是个会走路的钱袋，一个碍眼的「扫把星」。

**Cinderborne:** It was the Society that gave me the chance to live the life I have now.

> **灰烬：**是幸研会给了我一个机会，让我能过上现在的生活。

**Ashveil:** What about the Synwish Syndicate, then? If I recall correctly, they took care of you once. Old Polwave helped you deal with those relatives.

> **不死途：**那共愿帮呢？我没记错的话，他们照顾过你的生活。是池波这老家伙帮你摆平了那些亲戚。

**Cinderborne:** Listen, Mr. Ashveil. The debt I owe the Synwish Syndicate will be paid. I will avenge them as a member of the Smiles Society as well as the Daybreak Squadron. I'll rip the Herald of Death out from the shadows in their name and see justice served. That is my duty!

> **灰烬：**不死途先生，共愿帮的仇，我会替他们报的。我会以幸研会一员、以破晓战队一员的名义，把新告死魔从阴影中揪出来,将他绳之以法！这是我的责任！

**Ashveil:** You're in over your head.

> **不死途：**你做不到。

**Ashveil:** The new Herald of Death is very dangerous. I doubt a handful of actors like you can achieve anything substantial.

> **不死途：**新告死魔实在很危险，我不觉得你们这些演员能对他做什么。

**Cinderborne:** You think we're just a couple of kids playing superheroes with masks, right? Well, you're wrong. My squad mates and I aren't the easy pushovers we used to be, Mr. Ashveil.

> **灰烬：**你以为我们是过家家扮超级英雄的孩子，对吗？你太小看我们了，不死途先生。我和破晓战队的伙伴早就不是当年那些任人欺凌的小孩子了……

**Cinderborne:** I haven't had my transformation surgery yet, but I can already power a mechatron warrior in battle! WE are real heroes here!

> **灰烬：**虽然我还没接受手术转变，但我已经可以操纵机铠战士战斗了！我们，是真正的英雄！

**Kuchiba:** Boys, let's stay on topic, shall we? I just wanted to have a few words about the Society...

> **朽叶：**偏题了，大男孩们！我只想聊几句关于幸研会的问题……

**Cinderborne:** I have nothing to say to you either, Miss Kuchiba. Neither of you are getting a word out of me on this.

> **灰烬：**我和你没话可说，朽叶小姐。我不会透露任何事情，一个字也不会——

**???:** Well done, Magnus... Your loyalty to the Smiles Society warms our hearts.

> **？？？：**干得好啊，真弘…你没有背叛幸研会，这让我们大家都很欣慰。

**Cinderborne:** You're... following me?

> **灰烬：**你们…跟踪我？

**Smiles Society Member:** Not by choice. These IPC dogs made it clear that they intend to frame Miss Fulwish. We're not going to just let this slide.

> **幸研会成员：**没办法，谁叫这两条公司狗一看就是想咬着满愿小姐栽赃陷害呢？我们可不打算随便揭过这事儿。

**Kuchiba:** Would you look at that, the hunters become the hunted.

> **朽叶：**真是螳螂捕蝉，黄雀在后。

**Ashveil:** What does it matter? If someone's looking for a beating, we'll gladly give it to them.

> **不死途：**有什么关系，有人找上门讨打，赏他一顿打不就好了？

**Kuchiba:** You have five minutes for questioning, so make it count, Mr. Ashveil. They're all yours.

> **朽叶：**我会给你五分钟时间，想问什么尽情问吧。有劳了，不死途先生。

**Ashveil:** Never expected my first "miracle" as a Supplicant to be teaching thugs... I mean, lawful citizens, a lesson on the streets.

> **不死途：**真没想到，成为谒者后展示的第一个「奇迹」居然是在街边巷口教训流氓…啊不，守法公民。

**Ashveil:** Let's get this over with, then! And if you want to join them too, Magnus, be my guest.

> **不死途：**放马过来吧！至于你，真弘，想和他们一起动手，请便。

### Defeat the Smiles Society members

*(Enter battle)*

Wave 1 Assistant Graffiti: Demander ×1, SD Civilian Mechatron ×2, Assistant Graffiti: Slacker ×1

**Ashveil:** Fair warning: I'm out of practice. If you get hurt, that's your problem.

> **不死途：**很久没动手了，死伤概不负责。

**Smiles Society Member:** Hehe, your fists don't hurt at all.

> **幸研会成员：**嘻嘻，你的拳脚一点也不痛啊。

**Cinderborne:** Mr. Ashveil, stop!

> **灰烬：**不死途先生！住手！

Wave 2 SD Civilian Mechatron ×2, Daybreak Squadron: Cinderborne ×1

**Cinderborne:** I'm sorry, Mr. Ashveil. I have to stop you.

> **灰烬：**抱歉，不死途先生，我必须阻止你。

**Smiles Society Member:** Hooray, Cinderborne! The bringer of justice!

> **幸研会成员：**好样的，灰烬战士！正义的使者！

**Cinderborne:** Shut up!

> **灰烬：**给我闭嘴！

*(Battle ends)*

[Recap] The defeated Smiles Society members seemed to feel no pain whatsoever. Ashveil suspected this was related to the "conversion surgery" Cinderborne mentioned, but Cinderborne refused to respond to the matter.

**Smiles Society Member:** It hurts... a little. But I'm... happy...

> **幸研会成员：**一点点，痛。但，很快乐……

**Ashveil:** There's something seriously wrong with you all.

> **不死途：**你们这些家伙…真的很不对劲。

**Cinderborne:** E—Enough! You... Are you really going to kill them!?

> **灰烬：**够了！你…你真要把他们杀了不成？

**Ashveil:** What's the matter, defender of the city? Are you scared?

> **不死途：**怎么，守护城市的英雄——灰烬战士，你害怕了？

**Ashveil:** This is the real Phantasmoon Games, Magnus! This is the bloody truth under the mask of the Elation's game.

> **不死途：**真弘，这就是幻月游戏！这就是剥开「欢愉」的外壳，这个游戏底下最真实的部分。

**Ashveil:** Fifteen years ago, at dusk in this city, the Herald of Death tore people apart one by one! Did he ever stop to ask even once, "Is it enough!?"

> **不死途：**十五年前，告死魔在这座城市的黄昏里，把人们一个接一个地撕碎！他有没有过问过一句「够不够」？！

**Ashveil:** I know you want to make something out of yourself like the Trailblazer of the Astral Express, and I know you want to catch the new Herald of Death and crush him like a bug, using the same methods he used against the Synwish Syndicate.

> **不死途：**我知道你在想什么，你想和星穹列车的开拓者一样做成些大事。你想找出这个新的告死魔，用他对付共愿帮的方法把他像虫子一样碾碎。

**Ashveil:** But here's the deal: It's not your duty! And the Aeonic Game sure isn't a TV show where you get to play the hero and save the day.

> **不死途：**但我告诉你，这不是你的责任！星神的游戏也不是你扮演光明英雄的电视剧。

**Ashveil:** You mentioned a "conversion surgery" earlier... Are you saying that all Society members underwent some kind of unknown modification?

> **不死途：**你刚才说什么…「手术转变」，莫非幸研会的人都接受过什么不知来历的改造？

**Ashveil:** That figures. I was wondering why these people looked like they felt no pain at all. Humming along merrily even as they bled... Does this have anything to do with the "surgery"?

> **不死途：**难怪，刚才冲我来的这几个家伙，好像完全察觉不到痛苦一样，头破血流也只会快乐地哼哼。这和你刚才说的「手术」有关吗？

**Cinderborne:** ...

> **灰烬：**……

**Ashveil:** Nothing to say? Fine by me. Three minutes left, that's long enough for me to question them.

> **不死途：**不说话？也好，还剩三分钟，足够我问他们问题了。

### Investigate the unconscious Smiles Society members (0/3)

**Ashveil:** Time to hurry and get some answers from these people while they're still on the ground.

> **不死途：**赶紧从这几个躺倒到家伙口中问问题吧。

*(Talk to Kuchiba, optional)*

**Kuchiba:** You have five minutes for questioning, so make it count, Mr. Ashveil. They're all yours.

*(Talk to Smiles Society Member)*

**Ashveil:** Hey lackey, wake up!

> **不死途：**喂，杂鱼，给我醒醒！

**Collapsed Society Follower:** Pain... should not exist.

> **倒地的会众：**痛苦…不应存在。

*(Floating text)*

Pain should not exist.

*(Talk to Smiles Society Member)*

**Collapsed Society Follower:** Smile... please, smile!

> **倒地的会众：**笑…笑一笑吧！

*(Floating text)*

Please smile!

*(Talk to Smiles Society Member)*

**Collapsed Society Follower:** The laughter... don't let it stop.

> **倒地的会众：**笑…不要让笑声停下来。

### Ask Cinderborne what's going on here

*(Floating text)*

Don't let the laughter stop.

Pain should not exist.

(Repeated many times)

Smile

Happiness

Pain should not exist.

Everlasting, blissful, HAPPINESS!

Everlasting, blissful, HAPPINESS!

No one should have to suffer.

Everyone will be happy!

[Recap] Cinderborne confessed that anyone who officially joined the Smiles Society had to undergo "Happiness Surgery," but Cinderborne was not ready for it. Ashveil told Cinderborne to remember those feelings of hesitation and terror, then convinced him to leave the scene.

*(Try to communicate)*

**Collapsed Society Follower:** (Crowd cheering)

> **倒地的会众：**（众人的欢呼声）

**Ashveil:** What the heck... happened to these people?

> **不死途：**这些人到底是…怎么回事？

**Cinderborne:** This is... the small procedure that official members receive. They call it the "Happiness Surgery."

> **灰烬：**这是…正式入会成员接受的小手术。他们管这个叫「幸福手术」。

**Cinderborne:** With a prick of the needle no more than a mosquito's bite, the doctor puts this small thing inside your eyelids. Even smaller than a Synesthesia Beacon, and more convenient...

> **灰烬：**只要轻轻扎个针，就像被蚊子叮一下。然后，医生会在你的眼睑内侧放一个小东西，很小很小，比联觉信标还小，还方便……

**Cinderborne:** People who have had the surgery say that it soothes all your pain and suffering. Past or present.

> **灰烬：**听做完手术的人说，它会抚平你所有的伤痛，不管旧的还是新的。

**Cinderborne:** Even your body becomes stronger, and your mind... clearer, and more focused. A state where you can truly receive "happiness."

> **灰烬：**连身体都会变得更强壮，精神也更加纯粹、专注…真正体察到了「幸福」。

**Ashveil:** So... you haven't had this "Happiness Surgery" yet, right?

> **不死途：**所以，你没有接受这个…「幸福手术」吧？

**Cinderborne:** I... I'm not ready yet! I've been thinking it over.

> **灰烬：**我…我还没准备好！我一直在犹豫。

**Cinderborne:** But the rest of the Squadron had it, and they've all become... happier.

> **灰烬：**但是，队友们都做了啊。他们都变得…更快乐了。

**Cinderborne:** They say it's the necessary step to becoming a true Superhero that safeguards everyone else's happiness. If I don't do it, then... do I still deserve the name "Cinderborne"?

> **灰烬：**他们说这是成为真正的超级英雄，守护大家幸福必需的一步。我不做的话…我、我还有资格成为「灰烬战士」吗？

**Cinderborne:** Mr. Ashveil... What should I do...?

> **灰烬：**不死途先生，我到底…该怎么办…？

**Ashveil:** The surgery won't make you stronger, nor will it help you get your revenge. These guys on the floor are proof enough.

> **不死途：**这手术根本给不了你真正的力量，也无法帮助你复仇，倒在地上的那些家伙就是证据。

**Ashveil:** Remember the hesitation and fear you feel in this moment, Magnus. If people simply erase their perception of fear and pain instead of overcoming them, then those people will never find true strength.

> **不死途：**真弘，记住你这一刻的「犹豫」和「恐惧」。如果人只是抹去了对恐惧和痛苦的知觉，而不是战胜它们，那这样的人永远不可能拥有力量。

**Ashveil:** Go now. I have some cleaning up to do.

> **不死途：**你走吧，我要善后了。

### Talk to Kuchiba about the investigation results

[Recap] Ashveil concluded that the Smiles Society had somehow eliminated its members' ability to feel pain and theorized that Star Rail FEST would be the perfect venue for the "New Herald of Death" to incite panic for a second time.

*(Approach Kuchiba)*

**Kuchiba:** So, did you pry the answers you were looking for out of them?

> **朽叶：**从他们嘴里撬出你想知道的事情了？

**Ashveil:** These people have all been "processed" by the Smiles Society.

> **不死途：**这些家伙都被幸研会「处理」过了。

**Ashveil:** Heck, who knows what method they used. It completely neutralized their ability to feel suffering and despair, and gave them bodies like steel, incapable of feeling pain...

> **不死途：**鬼知道他们用了什么手法，把会众感知痛苦、绝望的那根筋给抽了，往人家脑袋里塞进了某种东西，给这些会众一副不怕疼痛的钢筋铁骨……

**Ashveil:** Say, is this the kind of treatment that the Medical Ethics Committee would approve?

> **不死途：**我说，这是医疗伦理委员会能盖章放行的那种医疗方案吗？

**Kuchiba:** As if. I doubt they even bothered to submit an application.

> **朽叶：**怎么可能，怕是连申请都没提交过吧？

**Ashveil:** Which means Fulwish has far more dirty laundry than we initially expected.

> **不死途：**所以，满愿这个女人，比我们预估的要邪门得多。

**Ashveil:** The idea was to find the Herald of Death with the leads we had, but here we are, going deeper down an increasingly scarier rabbit hole...

> **不死途：**咱们本来是想顺着线索挖出告死魔，没成想，一铲子下去，却无意中挖出了更可怕的东西……

**Kuchiba:** Judging by their voices, these followers don't seem to have any ill intentions. In fact, they sound a lot more humane than most humans.

> **朽叶：**仅听这些会众口中发出的声音，他们的内心似乎并没什么恶意，不如说，那声音比绝大多数人类更有人情味。

**Kuchiba:** If all this has nothing to do with the Herald of Death, then it matters little to our investigation.

> **朽叶：**如果这一切和告死魔无关，那也和我们眼下的行动无关。

**Kuchiba:** After all, how they pursue happiness is their own choice. Wouldn't you agree?

> **朽叶：**毕竟，幸研会的会众如何追求幸福，这是他们自己的选择，不是吗？

**Ashveil:** Are you serious, Kuchiba?

> **不死途：**朽叶，你说这话是认真的？

**Kuchiba:** It's only human instinct to avoid pain, right? Such avoidance doesn't go against the laws of nature, nor does it legally constitute a crime when the party involved was a voluntary participant.

> **朽叶：**逃避痛苦，这是生命刻在骨子里的本能，不是吗？它既不违背自然规律，从狭隘的法律条文上看，只要当事人「自愿」，我们也难以定罪。

**Ashveil:** Not a crime? Well my instinct right now is telling me that this definitely has something to do with the Herald of Death.

> **不死途：**难以定罪？我的直觉告诉我，这事儿和告死魔，绝对脱不了干系。

**Kuchiba:** Unfortunately, Mr. Ashveil, we need evidence to solve a case.

> **朽叶：**很遗憾，不死途先生，办案要的是铁证。

**Ashveil:** I'm here to follow my senses and dig up whatever's at the bottom of this. As for the evidence, that's your headache.

> **不死途：**我只负责凭我的嗅觉把东西挖出来，至于找到铁证，那是你该头疼的事情。

**Ashveil:** Besides, I don't think we need to turn over every stone to figure it out. If the new Herald of Death and Fulwish are connected, we'll be seeing another move soon enough. If I were her, I'd choose the brightest stage possible, so this weapon could spread maximum panic.

> **不死途：**何况我觉得用不着费心找——如果新告死魔和满愿有关，很快他就会有第二次行动。如果我是满愿，我会找个最闪亮的舞台,让这件兵器造成最大的恐慌……

**Kuchiba:** ...You mean the Star Rail FEST?

> **朽叶：**…你是说即将举办的「星铁FES」展会？

**Kuchiba:** Madam Pearl promised that top-level security would be deployed during the FEST, as well as... a contingency plan robust enough to quell any crisis.

> **朽叶：**真珠女士承诺过，FES期间会有最高级别的安保，以及…足以镇压一切危机的对策方案。

**Ashveil:** And you are staring right at him.

> **不死途：**她说的对策——就是我呀。

[Recap] Dan Heng tracked down Blade and revealed that Yao Guang foresaw Blade would be plagued by his violent tendencies, and he demanded to know his purpose there. Blade explained his visit concerned "Death," but there was another power of the Abundance existing within Planarcadia, which left him restless and agitated. Until everything was settled, Dan Heng would keep a watchful eye on Blade to ensure the Stellaron Hunters' arrival would not endanger his companions or this planet's future.

**Dan Heng:** According to Sunday, the crow should lead me to Blade's whereabouts. Since it stopped here, that means...

> **丹恒：**照星期日所言，乌鸦会带我找到刃的去处，既然它停留在这里，就说明……

**Blade:** I wasn't even looking for you, yet here you are, throwing yourself into the fray. Consider me surprised.

> **刃：**我没去找你，你却主动来送死，挺意外的。

**Silver Wolf:** We haven't even settled the score over the mask you stole from the Synwish Syndicate, and now you're skulking around and following us. What's your deal?

> **银狼：**从共愿帮那儿抢走我们的面具，这事儿都还没找你们算账，现在又鬼鬼祟祟地跟在我们后面做什么？

**Blade:** What do you, or should I say, all of you, want?

> **刃：**你，或者说，你们，作何打算？

**Dan Heng:** General Yao Guang of the Yuque has come to this world and is looking for you.

> **丹恒：**玉阙的爻光将军来了这个世界，她正在追踪你的下落。

**Dan Heng:** "Dragons clash in the wilderness, their ichor dark and yellow." In her divination, you will become a slave to your own violence, bringing a catastrophic end to all. I am in that future as well.

> **丹恒：**「龙战于野，其血玄黄」，在她的卜算中，你或将被自己的凶性所困，为所有人带来灭顶之灾，而我，也在那个未来当中。

**Dan Heng:** The only violence within you that comes to my mind is Shuhu.

> **丹恒：**潜藏在你体内的「凶性」，我能想到的，只有——倏忽。

**Dan Heng:** The Emanator of Abundance who resurrected time and time again. The monster who turned planets into living beings and merged billions of people into flesh spheres.

> **丹恒：**那个复活了一次又一次的丰饶令使，让行星化作活体，令百亿人融成肉球的怪物。

**Dan Heng:** The Xianzhou fears that he is using your body... to resurrect himself.

> **丹恒：**仙舟担心的恐怕是…他在你的身上借尸还魂。

**Blade:** So, what's your plan? Kill me again?

> **刃：**所以，你准备怎么做？再杀死我一次？

**Silver Wolf:** Oh my, things are getting a little heated. Should I get you both slushies?

> **银狼：**哎呀，好大的火气啊，要不我给你俩买杯冰沙？

**Dan Heng:** Thank you.

> **丹恒：**谢谢。

**Blade:** I'll pass.

> **刃：**不用。

**Dan Heng:** While General Yao Guang's divination may foretell a death battle between us, that doesn't necessarily mean it's the only interpretation.

> **丹恒：**爻光将军的占卜或昭示着你我的死战，却未必没有其他的解读。

**Dan Heng:** My experiences on the Express have shown me time and time again that even fates set in stone aren't entirely immovable.

> **丹恒：**列车的经历已无数次告诉我，即便命数已定，也未尝无可撼动。

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Dan Heng:** I have no intention of getting into a quarrel with you, but I need to make sure that the presence of the Stellaron Hunters won't endanger my companions, or the future of this planet.

> **丹恒：**我无意与你纠缠，但我需要确认，星核猎手的到来不会危及我的同伴，以及这颗星球的未来。

**Dan Heng:** Thus, I must know your purpose here, Blade. What has Destiny's Slave written in your script?

> **丹恒：**我必须知道你们此行的目的，刃，命运的奴隶给你的剧本上都写了什么？

**Blade:** He stated this trip would be connected to "Death."

> **刃：**他说，此行会和「死亡」有关。

**Dan Heng:** ...Should I congratulate you?

> **丹恒：**…该说恭喜吗？

**Blade:** Much obliged. In my experience, it's best not to get your hopes up too soon. Prophecies rarely unfold the way you expect.

> **刃：**多谢。我的经验之谈是，不要高兴太早，预言多半不会以你想的样子兑现。

**Blade:** However, the General's divination isn't entirely baseless. Ever since my arrival here, I have been feeling restless.

> **刃：**不过，那位将军的卜算并非无稽之谈，自从来到这儿，我就时常躁动不安。

**Blade:** There's another power here beckoning me.

> **刃：**这里有另一股力量，在呼唤我。

**Dan Heng:** Is there another form of Abundance in the world besides you?

> **丹恒：**除你之外，这个世界还有另一股丰饶的力量？

**Blade:** Perhaps there is more than one.

> **刃：**或许不止一个。

**Dan Heng:** Are you referring to the Disciples of Sanctus Medicus or the Elixir Seekers?

> **丹恒：**药王秘传还是求药使？

**Blade:** Something far more dangerous.

> **刃：**比那更危险。

**Dan Heng:** Do they have support from other factions?

> **丹恒：**有其他势力的协助吗？

**Blade:** Unclear.

> **刃：**不清楚。

**Dan Heng:** As such, I'll keep an eye on you until everything settles, just like you did in the past.

> **丹恒：**那么，在一切尘埃落定之前，我会死死地盯着你，就像以前的你那样。

**Dan Heng:** That's as far as I'll go.

> **丹恒：**仅此而已。

**Blade:** Hah.

> **刃：**哈。

**Silver Wolf:** Did you two just agree to some weird collaboration before me?

> **银狼：**你们两个，是不是当着我的面，达成了什么奇怪的合作？

**Blade:** I'll leave that matter in your hands, Silver Wolf.

> **刃：**银狼，那边的事，就拜托你了。

**Silver Wolf:** I don't get it. That good-for-nothing detective didn't even blink an eye before selling us out to Pearl, and you still plan on working with him?

> **银狼：**我不明白，那倒霉侦探可是一点犹豫都没有，转头就把我们卖给了真珠，你还打算找他办事？

**Blade:** There are things only he can get done.

> **刃：**有些事，只有他能办到。

[Recap] Through tuning, Sunday detected a unified "laughter of happiness" echoing through the minds of the Smiles Society members. At the same time, Yao Guang vanished from Sunday's senses.

Meanwhile, in Dovebrook District...

**Happy Resident:** Hey, you two. Why the long faces? On land blessed by the Master of Elation, frowning is bad manners~

> **快乐的居民：**两位，在欢愉之主赐福的土地上，愁眉苦脸可不好哟~

**Smiling Resident:** Come on, smile! Whether you're hungry or full, poor or rich, The Laughter is with you! Would you be interested in learning about our Research Society of Happy Smiles?

> **微笑的居民：**笑一笑吧，无论饥饿还是饱腹，贫穷或是富裕，乐子神都会庇佑我们的！有兴趣了解一下「幸福微笑研究会」吗？

**Sunday:** Thank you, but I'll pass.

> **星期日：**不必了，谢谢。

**Sunday:** Another one. How many people have been talking to us about "happiness" today?

> **星期日：**又一个。这是今天第几个向我们谈论「幸福」的路人了？

**Tingyun:** Well, six, exactly.

> **停云：**我有数喔，不多不少有六个。

**Tingyun:** Locals say ever since the Synwish Syndicate case, this Research Society of Happy Smiles has exploded in size.

> **停云：**听路人说，打从共愿帮的案件发生后，这个叫「幸福微笑研究会」的团体突然人多了起来。

**Sunday:** That gang's fall left a power vacuum, and the Research Society of Happy Smiles filled it like a fast-growing fungus. Even those who were once resistant or indifferent got swept up in it.

> **星期日：**那个帮派的消失留下了真空，幸福微笑研究会像最有效率的菌群，迅速填补了这片混乱。许多原本对他们抵触或漠不关心的人，也被卷了进来。

**Tingyun:** Hm, I think I'm starting to get a better picture now. Is this, by any chance, tied to what you're preparing to do in the Phantasmoon Games?

> **停云：**啊，小女子算是听出些苗头来了。莫非这和您在幻月游戏中打算做的事情有关?

**Sunday:** Not only me. It might also be tied to the "Ominous Fate" General Yao Guang warned about... The one looming over this world.

> **星期日：**不仅和我有关，也可能和爻光将军提到的这个世界所面临的「凶险命数」有关。

**Sunday:** A few days ago, when I used the tuning power to help her intervene at SparxiCon, I caught a strange sound echoing in people's minds.

> **星期日：**几日前我以调律之力帮助爻光将军介入「火花大会」时，意外在人群脑海中聆听到了一个奇怪的声音。停云……奇怪的声音？

**Tingyun:** ...A strange sound?

**Sunday:** Yes, cheerful, happy laughter echoing in their heads.

> **星期日：**嗯，这些人的脑海中统统回荡着一阵「幸福的笑声」。

**Happy Thoughts:** (Heh heh... Without those gangsters, this street's much cleaner now, hahaha!)

> **幸福的心声：**（嘿嘿，没了那些帮派渣滓，这条街干净了许多，哈哈哈哈！）

**Happy Thoughts:** (Hahahahaha—)

> **幸福的心声：**（哈哈哈哈哈——）

**Tingyun:** The so-called "voice of Aha" those society recruiters claim to hear? Could the Joyseeker truly be so passionate about universal redemption?

> **停云：**这是…那些招募我们的研究会成员说的，他们能听到的「阿哈的声音」？常乐天君，真有这般普世救赎的心肠？

**Sunday:** Whatever that is, it's not Aha's laughter.

> **星期日：**无论那是什么，那绝不会是来自欢笑之神的声音。

**Sunday:** From the moment I set foot on this street, I've been using my tuning ability to sense the residents' minds. Miss Tingyun, allow me to correct you... those connected to the Research Society of Happy Smiles number not six, but forty-two.

> **星期日：**从踏入这条街开始，我就在使用调律能力感知居民们的意识波动。停云小姐，容我纠正，与幸福微笑研究会有关的，不是六人，而是四十二人。

**Sunday:** Each of them carries the same sound: identical laughter, looping endlessly like a broken record.

> **星期日：**这些人脑中，都回荡着一道极其相似、如同重复播放的劣质唱片般的笑声。

**Tingyun:** Mind control? Or... something worse?

> **停云：**这是精神控制，还是……？

**Sunday:** Are you trying to say that this is tied to the Harmony?

> **星期日：**您想说，这是不是来自「同谐」的力量？

**Tingyun:** My apologies. I dare not make such wild assumptions.

> **停云：**失礼了，小女子不敢妄加判断。

**Sunday:** No. This isn't the work of any Tuner. It feels like a barrier, born from the human mind itself, to numb the unbearable pain it cannot endure.

> **星期日：**不，这声音…并非来自任何调弦者施加的影响。它更像是人为了逃避无法承受的痛苦，由意识自身分裂出来、用于自我麻痹和自我欺骗的屏障。

**Sunday:** Like a worn out tape recorder hidden deep in the subconscious, repeating the same thing over and over: "I am happy. I am happy."

> **星期日：**打个比方，一个不断重复「我很幸福」的、生锈的录音机，藏在每个人脑海深处。

**Tingyun:** So... whose divine handiwork is this supposed to be now?

> **停云：**这又是何方神圣的手笔啊？

**Sunday:** Wherever this laughter comes from, causing so many people to self-deceive in perfect unison while enduring pain is well beyond the capabilities of a mere charitable group.

> **星期日：**不管这笑声来自何处，能让如此多的人在痛苦中整齐划一地自我欺骗，这远超出一个慈善团体所能做到的极限了。

**Sunday:** Beneath these calm waters, the undercurrents are far more turbulent than anyone imagines.

> **星期日：**这片平和的水面之下，暗流汹涌远超想象。

**Tingyun:** The bad news just keeps coming. Dan Heng's gone off on his own, and Madam Yao's completely vanished. Can you still sense her?

> **停云：**坏消息真是一条接着一条。丹恒先生单枪匹马地行动，爻老板也音讯全无。你能感应到她的存在吗？

**Sunday:** The bird I entrusted to General Yao Guang has seemingly gone silent. I can't share its vision anymore. I can't even feel her thoughts through it.

> **星期日：**不知为何，我交给爻光将军的那只鸟儿像是断线了一般…我既无法分享它的视野，也无法透过它感知其思绪。

**Sunday:** But I know it hasn't faded. That means she's still in this world somewhere.

> **星期日：**唯一能确认的是，这只鸟儿并未消散，爻光将军本人应该还在这个世界的某处。

**Tingyun:** Let's pray she returns soon, with some news to pierce through all this fog. Otherwise, this arcadia built on happiness will crumble fast and fall into the abyss...

> **停云：**只盼她能平安归来，带回些能拨开迷雾的消息吧。否则，我们脚下这片看似幸福的乐园，要不了多久就该滑向深渊了……

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 179　The Bum's as Holy as the Seraphim　浪子与六翼天使一般神圣

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Dovebrook District　|　角色：March 7th; Trailblazer; Aeon ★ Aha; Paraprince; Furbulitzer; Furli; Joolyon; Himeko; Ryusuke; Asat Pramad　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Confessions of a Righteous Sinner'' completed

### Find the comic author of The Fluffy

> *Blurry_QR_Colt... is not actually a colt, right?*

[Recap] Heading to the address given by a Pearluxe staff, you and March 7th arrived at the building housing the Furbobo Weekly headquarters. Ms. Blurry_QR_Colt lived right there.

After some time, you and March pay a visit to the author of The Fluffy...

**(Trailblazer):** Blurry_QR_Colt... That's an unusual name.

> **开拓者：**模糊二维马…真是好奇怪一个名字。

**March 7th:** Why would the author of Fluffy Across the Blue have such a pen name?

> **三月七：**《苍天航路绒绒号》的作者为什么要起这样一个笔名？

- ▸ Maybe the author's eyes went blurry after too much drawing.

> ▸ 画漫画画到视线模糊了。

**March 7th:** You're taking it a bit too literally. But... it's not impossible!

> **三月七：**你这完全是望文生义吧。但…也很有可能！

- ▸ He's a Duomension City horse!

> ▸ 他是二维市的一匹马！

**March 7th:** Really? A pen name often says a lot about its creator...

> **三月七：**真的假的？唔，通常来说笔名代表作者的个性……

**March 7th:** Someone who can draw something that cute must have a warm, gentle heart.

> **三月七：**嗨呀，能画出这么可爱的漫画，肯定是位性格温柔的作者。

**March 7th:** I heard The Fluffy's been running for half a year in a magazine. At first, it wasn't very popular in Planarcadia.

> **三月七：**我听说这位老师在杂志上连载这漫画也有小半年了。一开始，绒绒号在二相乐园算不上什么大热作品。

**March 7th:** But after that Memory Bubble movie Holy Grail/stay night Old Oti produced became a massive hit, the Astral Express's adventure stories spread far and wide, and The Fluffy started gaining popularity.

> **三月七：**自从老奥帝先生投拍的忆泡电影《圣杯/今晚留下来》大卖后，星穹列车的冒险故事就越来越广为人知了，绒绒号也突然得到关注，大火了起来。

**March 7th:** Now the latest chapter, The Sacred Flame's Return: Amphoreus's Creation, is sold out everywhere!

> **三月七：**据说最新连载的《圣火归还之章•翁法罗斯创世》都卖到脱销了。

**March 7th:** This Colt sure knows a lot about our journey. Must be a galactic fan of the Express Crew.

> **三月七：**这位马老师，对咱们列车一路的冒险过程了解得还真不少，一定是列车组的超银河级大粉丝。

- ▸ Another villain, perhaps?

> ▸ 指不定是什么新反派…

**March 7th:** Come on, what ever happened to trust?

> **三月七：**人与人之间的信任呢？

- ▸ We just gained another loyal fan!

> ▸ 又收获新的铁粉一枚。

**March 7th:** Yes, yes. You've told us the story of Cinderborne ten times since we got back from Pearluxe Tower.

> **三月七：**是是是，从珠星大厦回来后，你已经把那位灰烬小弟的故事给咱们讲了十遍了。

- ▸ I still think we deserve royalties though.

> ▸ 我希望他多少给点赔偿。

**March 7th:** Royalties? You're being way too practical. It's a comic where we're the protagonists, someone's giving us free publicity! Can't you cut them a little slack?

> **三月七：**你也太现实了，那可是以咱们为主角的漫画欸，人家平白给咱们宣传，你就不能对人家网开一面吗？

**March 7th:** According to the address the Pearluxe employee gave us, it looks like we've arrived at a familiar place...

> **三月七：**按照珠星工作人员给的地址，咱们似乎来到一个熟悉的地方……

**(Trailblazer):** For such a small place, Dovebrook District is really full of hidden talents.

> **开拓者：**小小一个鸽川区，真是卧虎藏龙。

**March 7th:** Turn left at the first intersection, then turn right. Go straight for two hundred meters. Wait, this is...

> **三月七：**在第一个路口左走再右转，再往前直走两百米，这是……

**March 7th:** The Furbobo Weekly newspaper!?

> **三月七：**《狸狸周刊》报社？！

**March 7th:** Don't tell me the author of The Fluffy is actually a furbo! No wonder he rarely shows his face.

> **三月七：**难道，绒绒号的作者本人是只狸猫？难怪他几乎不怎么露面？

**March 7th:** (Trailblazer), come on, let's go upstairs!

> **三月七：**开拓者，走，咱们上楼去！

*(Unlock Achievement The Pursuit of Happyness)*

> **三月七：**绒绒号，好绒绒，小帕姆，毛茸茸。

*(Approach the office door)*

> **普狸策：**黑心社长！

**???:** Pay us!

**Nihilux:** Ahem. Please come in.

**Nihilux:** Let me guess... Your next line is—

> **虚照：**「你为什么在这儿？！ 」

- ▸ It's you!

> ▸ 是你！

- ▸ Why are you here!?

> ▸ 你为什么在这儿？！
