> ▸ 哎，我就不说。

**March 7th:** Miss Nihilux, what are you doing here!?

> **三月七：**虚照小姐，你为什么在这儿？！

**Nihilux:** What's the problem? I'm the magazine's founder, its president, and its editor-in-chief. It's only logical that I'd be sitting here.

> **虚照：**怎么啦？！为这家杂志社的创始人兼社长兼总编，我坐在这里，完全合情合理、合乎逻辑。

**Furbulitzer:** What? A million!? President Nihilux has already been behind on my... I mean, behind on everyone's wages for months! If it weren't for the money... who'd risk their neck being a tabloid journalist, sneaking photos of the bigwigs on the Astral Express?

> **普狸策：**吓？一百万？！虚照社长已经拖欠本狸…不是，拖欠全体兄弟好几个月工资哩！要不是为了钱…谁会铤而走险来干八卦记者，偷拍星穹列车的大人物哩？

It finally occurs to you why the name "Nihilux" always felt vaguely familiar. The former president of Furbobo Weekly, that deadbeat boss who skipped town to dodge her debts, went by that exact name.

**Furbulitzer:** A boss who doesn't pay wages has no right to sit here, they belong in the underworld!

> **普狸策：**不发工资的社长，没资格坐在这里，该下地狱哩！

**Nihilux:** Idiots. Without me, you'd all have been locked up in some Department of Aberration Defense painting prison long ago. And you still have the gall to demand wages from your savior?

> **虚照：**笨蛋，如果没有我，你们早就不知道被「异常防御部」关到哪个画中监狱去了。居然还敢腆着脸向庇护你们的恩人索要工资这种东西。

**Nihilux:** (Trailblazer), have you ever heard of the sensational serial thief case "Phantom Furbomorph"? It happened in the year 1996 of the Arcadian Calendar—

> **虚照：**开拓者，你听说过轰动一时的社会新闻「怪盗变化狸」连环失窃案吗？话说在乐园历1996年——

**Furbulitzer:** I'm sorry! I was wrong! I shouldn't have talked back! I'm sorry for being born a furbo!

> **普狸策：**对不起，我错了，我不该顶撞您，生而为狸猫，我很抱歉！

**Paraprince:** Beep-boop! Even if you've got dirt on us, making us work for free is way too ridiculous!

> **幻太子：**哗坡，就算我们有把柄捏在你手里，但让我们打白工也太狸了个大谱嘞！

**Furbulitzer:** We've had enough! We've overthrown the boss! We've got a new President now! (Trailblazer), please stand up for us!

> **普狸策：**我们已经成功下克上了！我们有了新社长哩！开拓者社长，请为我们主持公道哩！

**Nihilux:** Welcome, traveler! Furbo hotpot requires a reservation, rumors are 10,000 credits a piece, and if you're looking to hire a hitman, just head right down the hall to the storeroom. Oh, or maybe—

> **虚照：**你们好啊，无名客！狸肉火锅请提前预约，小道消息一万信用点一条，买凶杀人往里直走右拐，那个杂物间里住的家伙就是干这个的。又或者说——

**Nihilux:** You came to meet the brilliant, charming, and universally adored Blurry_QR_Colt? Well then... truth be told, that person is actually—

> ▸ 三月，你背着我做漫画家？

**March 7th:** Ugh... Even more nonexistent memories. What are you even talking about!?

> **三月七：**嘶…不存在的记忆又增加了。说什么呢你！

**(Trailblazer):** Once you rule out all the wrong answers, only one truth remains—

> **开拓者：**排除所有错误选项后，真相只有一个——

> **开拓者：**这里唯一会画画的人，虚照，你就是模糊二维马！

[Recap] Blurry_QR_Colt was Nihilux's alias. You convinced Nihilux to attend Star Rail FEST by threatening to sue for portrait licensing fees. Before the exhibition, Nihilux wanted you to get familiar with The Fluffy comic.

**Nihilux:** ...

> **虚照：**……

> **虚照：**哎呀，被看穿了呢。作为识破我伪装的奖励，想要签名还是合照呢？

**Nihilux:** Allow me to reintroduce myself. I'm Nihilux, you can also call me by my pen name, Blurry_QR_Colt.

> **虚照：**重新自我介绍一下，在下虚照，你们也可以称呼我的笔名，模糊二维马。

> **三月七：**难怪你在珠星大厦的时候老跟着我们，还对我们这么熟悉，这就不奇怪了，等等……

**March 7th:** This "Blurry_QR_Colt" persona isn't a fake, is it?

> **三月七：**「模糊二维马」这个身份，不会也是冒名顶替的吧？

**Nihilux:** Identity is just a mask and a body. Real or fake, it's all in how you see it.

> **虚照：**所谓身份，不过是一张面具和一副皮囊，是真是假，只取决于看待它的角度。

**Nihilux:** At the very least, for now, there's no other Blurry_QR_Colt around who could show up at Star Rail FEST for a live signing and hype up the Astral Express, right?

> **虚照：**至少对现在的你们而言，眼下这个地方也没有第二个模糊二维马能到「星铁FES」做现场签售，为星穹列车造势了吧？

**March 7th:** So you already know why we're here?

> **三月七：**我们的来意，你都知道了？

**Nihilux:** Just a little deduction, that's all.

**Nihilux:** The news about "Star Rail FEST" is already everywhere online. Knowing how that perfectionist Madam Pearl operates, she's definitely pulling strings to make your collaboration a success.

> **卒中：**关于「星铁FES」铺天盖地的宣传都开始在网络上刷屏了。以那位真珠女士万事都谋求尽善尽美的办事态度，她一定会为你们之间的合作创造更多有利条件。

**Nihilux:** Naturally, having me step in was part of her plan.

**Nihilux:** That's not a signing event, but a public execution, right?

**March 7th:** Yeah, that does sound pretty horrifying.

> **三月七：**确实，有点恐怖。

**Nihilux:** Could be tomorrow, could be next month, but definitely not today.

> ▸ 所以，我什么时候有新形态？

**Nihilux:** How should I know!? It depends on what trouble you get yourselves into... Uh, I mean, on where you go and on which villain you heroically defeat!

> **虚照：**这我哪儿能知道啊！不都得看你们又去哪儿捅了哪个马蜂窝…咳咳，我是说，不都得看各位去哪儿行侠仗义击败了哪个反派吗？

- ▸ You've made quite a fortune off our adventures, haven't you?

> ▸ 你用我们的故事挣了不少吧？

**(Trailblazer):** So tell me, Miss Nihilux, do you really have the right to refuse us? You've used the Crew's likenesses and adventure experiences without permission... Surely you owe us some form of compensation.

> **开拓者：**你真的有立场拒绝我们吗？虚照老师，你未经许可使用了列车组的肖像权和冒险经历——不掏点什么慰问咱们，说不过去吧。

**Nihilux:** Wh—Why are we talking about this all of a sudden?

> **虚照：**…怎怎怎么突然说起这茬来了？

> **开拓者：**现在，我代表星穹列车乘员组全体向你索赔肖像授权金、精神损失费、误工费共计…

**(Trailblazer):** 1.6 billion credits!

> **开拓者：**十六亿信用点！

**March 7th:** How did you work that out?

> **三月七：**这个金额到底怎么算出来的？

**March 7th:** That's impossible! Just your royalties alone should've made you rich—

> **三月七：**不可能吧！光漫画的版税就该让老师你财富自由了——

**Nihilux:** Due to... uh, "unknown circumstances," my luck with money hasn't been great. But I get what this is really about. You're not only after cash, are you~

> **虚照：**因为一些不为人知的原因，我这个人的财运嘛…一直不太行。但是，我看得很明白，你们也不是非要我掏钱不可嘛~

**Nihilux:** I'll show up at "Star Rail FEST." Is that good enough for you?

> **虚照：**我会出席「星铁FES」的！这样总行了吧！

**Nihilux:** Time to attend Miss Nihilux's class. Please follow me.

### Join Nihilux's Author Workshop

> *Ding! Ding! Ding! Time for Nihilux's Workshop! Eyes off this, my dear student. Exit and listen to my class carefully.*

[Recap] Nihilux introduced the comic characters: Baseball Raccoon, Woof Woof Dan, March Bunny, Himekat, Mr. Yang, and Pom Pom. Six adorable fluffballs boarded the Express known as "The Fluffy" and embarked on a Trailblaze journey.

*(Talk to Nihilux)*

**Nihilux:** Ahem—ahem! Miss Nihilux's mini-class is officially in session. If you have any questions, feel free to ask at any time.

> **虚照：**咳咳，马老师的小课堂开课了，有问题的同学记得随时提问。

**March 7th:** Miss Colt, why did you pick the Astral Express as the theme for your comic?

> **三月七：**马老师，为什么选星穹列车做漫画主题？

**Nihilux:** Well, I'm all about realism, but sadly, my previous works like Acheron's Godslay, Galaxy Rangers' Saga, and dozens of others all got canceled.

> **虚照：**因为我是现实取材派，但遗憾的是，我之前画的《黄泉弑神斩》、《巡海游侠战记》还有其余几十本漫画都腰斩了。

**Nihilux:** If you hadn't beaten Irontomb and boosted its popularity, this one would've been canceled too. Let's not ask sad questions, alright?

> **虚照：**如果不是你们打败了铁墓，热度飙升，这本也要腰斩。下次不要问这么悲伤的问题。

**March 7th:** S—Sorry!

> **三月七：**对、对不起！

**Nihilux:** Alright, pay attention. The cute little critter in the center of the image is Baseball Raccoon, the main character of Fluffy Across the Blue. Just recently, March Bunny and Woof Woof Dan found him/her in a trashcan.

> **虚照：**注意看，画面中间的小动物叫球棒浣熊，是《苍天航路绒绒号》的主角。不久前，三月兔和汪汪丹在一个垃圾桶里找到了她。

**Nihilux:** At that moment, a terrifying monster is causing havoc on the space station. It tries to spit fire at Baseball Raccoon, but the flames don't burn through the trashcan. Instead, it sets the trash on fire, and the stench knocks the monster out.

> **虚照：**此时，一只恐怖的怪兽正在空间站肆虐，它向球棒浣熊喷出火焰，但火焰没能穿透垃圾桶，反而点燃了垃圾，弥漫的臭味熏倒了怪兽。

**March 7th:** Miss Colt, why is he/she the protagonist?

> **三月七：**马老师！为什么她/他是主角？

**Nihilux:** Readers today value immersion, and a character who has no memory of their past is, of course, the best choice.

> **虚照：**现在的读者都讲究一个代入感，这种对过去一无所知的角色，当然是最好的人选。

**March 7th:** I don't know anything about my past either, so why didn't you pick me?

> **三月七：**我、我也对过去一无所知，怎么不选我？

> **虚照：**虽然开拓者上车最晚，但她在各个世界留下的段子太多了，就连罗浮的监狱里都有她殴打外星生物的传言。

**Nihilux:** With material this crazy, not making it the main character? I might disagree, but Aha absolutely wouldn't

> **虚照：**这么神经的素材不拿来做主角，我同意，阿哈都不同意。

- ▸ March, I'll give you the protagonist role for a day.

> ▸ 三月，我把主角让给你一天。

**March 7th:** Stick to being the Baseball Raccoon okay? I don't need special treatment.

> **三月七：**当好你的球棒浣熊吧，本姑娘用不着谁来让。

- ▸ Miss Colt, well said!

> ▸ 马老师，你做得好啊！

**March 7th:** Wow, that's a smug face!

> **三月七：**哇，这幅得意忘形的嘴脸。

- ▸ Well, that's the reality of casting for you.

> ▸ 好现实的选角标准。

**March 7th:** So every time you land somewhere, you just run around to get more screen time, huh!?

> **三月七：**原来你每次一落地就到处乱跑，是为了提高自己的出镜率！

**Nihilux:** March, The Fluffy is just a comic. Outside of this, the author and the readers, every character in this world, aren't we all the protagonists of our own lives?

> **虚照：**三月小姐，绒绒号只是本漫画。而在此之外，每个角色、作者和读者，我们不都是自己人生的主角吗？

**March 7th:** That sounds so passionate, Miss Colt! I totally get it now.

> **三月七：**太热血了，马老师，我完全明白了。

**Nihilux:** And so, the unruly Baseball Raccoon, the quiet Woof Woof Dan, and the lively March Bunny formed a trio everyone loves.

> **虚照：**于是，脱线的球棒浣熊，寡言的汪汪丹，以及活泼可爱的三月兔，组成了大家喜闻乐见的三人小队。

**(Trailblazer):** Actually, I have a question, too.

> **开拓者：**其实我也有个问题。

- ▸ Why are they all small animals?

> ▸ 为什么大家都是小动物？

**Nihilux:** Ever since I got punched in the face by sales numbers, I've grasped a universal truth: In today's world, fluffy cute animals are always a crowd pleaser.

> **虚照：**自从吃了销量的铁拳，我就参透了一个宇宙至理——在现代社会，还得是毛茸茸的萌物最吃香。

**March 7th:** Seriously, even I feel like I can't compete with Pom-Pom's cuteness...

> **三月七：**的确，帕姆的可爱程度，连我都自愧不如

**Nihilux:** Designing the adult versions took some work. After defeating the monster, Baseball Raccoon accepted an invitation from the other fluffy animals and boarded The Fluffy.

> **虚照：**为了设计成年组的形象，我花了不少功夫。在打败怪兽后，球棒浣熊接受了其他小动物的邀请，登上了全是毛茸茸的绒绒号。

**Nihilux:** Here, the protagonist meets three big animals with distinct personalities. First, the adorable and universally loved conductor, Pom-Pom.

> **虚照：**在这里，主角见到了三只性格迥异的大动物。首先，是读者们敬爱的列车长，宇宙无敌可爱的帕姆。

**Nihilux:** Beside them is the elegant Navigator, Miss Himekat.

> **虚照：**在它的身旁，是优雅的领航员，姬奇猫小姐。

**March 7th:** I know, that's Himekat. Her original inspiration is Himeko. Phew, (Trailblazer), please don't tell her!

> **三月七：**我知道，这是「姬奇猫」，原型是姬子姐一一噗，开拓者，你可别告诉姬子姐啊。

- ▸ Pfft.

> ▸ 噗。

- ▸ Awesome!

> ▸ 太酷了。

- ▸ Your taste in naming is...

> ▸ 你的起名品味…

**Nihilux:** And the big brown dog on the other side, see? His name will shock you! That's right, he's—

> **虚照：**至于另一边这只棕色的大狗，说出他的名字，怕吓你们一跳！没错，他就是——

**March 7th:** Mr. Yang!

> **三月七：**杨叔！

- ▸ Mr. Yang?

> ▸ 杨叔？

- ▸ No fictionalized name for him?

> ▸ 没其他名字？

**Nihilux:** Just Mr. Yang.

> **虚照：**就是杨叔。

**March 7th:** Your taste in naming is indeed... extraordinary.

> **三月七：**老师的起名品味确实…非同一般。

**Nihilux:** Anyway, the six fluffy little creatures boarded The Fluffy Express and set off on their journey.

> **虚照：**总之，六只毛茸茸的小萌物就这样搭乘列车绒绒号，踏上了旅途。

**Nihilux:** As for the plot, it's pretty much the same as what you've all experienced, just with a bit of artistic tweaking to suit Planarcadia's audience.

> **虚照：**剧情方面应该用不着我介绍，和你们经历的大差不差，无非是针对二相乐园的受众，进行了一点点艺术加工。

- ▸ A bit?

> ▸ 一点点？

**Nihilux:** A tiny bit.

> **虚照：**亿点点。

- ▸ Artistic tweaking?

> ▸ 艺术加工？

**Nihilux:** If you don't stitch in a few local elements, no one's going to be interested.

> **虚照：**缝合点本地元素，大家才乐意看嘛。

**Nihilux:** In the comic world, which is even more cutthroat than the Phantasmoon Games, if you don't cater to the readers' tastes, it's nearly impossible to survive.

> **虚照：**在比幻月游戏还要残酷的漫画界，不投读者所好是很难活下来的。

**Nihilux:** Once upon a time, great comic artists had the luxury of a hundred chapters to build things up. Now, if you don't hook readers within a hundred pages, you're done.

> **虚照：**很早以前，漫画大师们可以用一百章的内容做铺垫。但放到现在，要不了一百页，作品就会被腰斩。

**Nihilux:** Readers today are hard to please, and authors have to come up with ever more shocking and sensational material. Doesn't this remind you of the Phantasmoon Games?

> **虚照：**读者愈发难满足，作者不得不创作出更刺激、更有噱头的内容。你们不觉得，这和幻月游戏有异曲同工之妙？

**Nihilux:** Looks like you're all familiar with the characters from The Fluffy. Now, let me talk about some of the creative challenges I'm facing.

> **虚照：**看来你们已经完全了解绒绒号的角色了。那么接下来和你们谈谈我目前面临的创作问题吧。

[Recap] Nihilux pointed out that while a heroic identity brought popularity to a character, it also became a set of shackles that bound them. If the protagonist became predictable or stopped getting stronger, the readers would quickly grow weary of them.

*(Talk to Nihilux)*

**Nihilux:** So, what do you see on these The Fluffy stands?

> **虚照：**在这批绒绒号的立牌上，你们看到了什么？

**March 7th:** It's (Trailblazer) turned into a cute little animal.

> **三月七：**变成可爱小动物的开拓者。

- ▸ A little raccoon.

> ▸ 一只小浣熊。

- ▸ A pirated version of me.

> ▸ 盗版的我。

- ▸ A furry version of me.

> ▸ 毛茸茸的我。

**Nihilux:** Yes, this is the character, and you're the inspiration behind him./her.

> **虚照：**没错，这是角色。而你是她的原型。

**Nihilux:** But even for someone as interesting as you, there are many backstories that readers don't care about, don't want to know, or don't feel obliged to understand.

> **虚照：**但即便是你这么有趣的原型，也一定会有许许多多读者们不关心、不想也没有义务去了解的故事。

**Nihilux:** A comic artist's job is to capture the most interesting parts and present them to the readers.

> **虚照：**漫画家的工作就是把值得看的部分抓出来，呈现给读者。

**Nihilux:** The cute and clever one is March Bunny. The unruly but reliable one is Baseball Raccoon. This is what we call a concrete archetype paired with an abstract character.

> **虚照：**可爱又机灵的，就是三月兔。脱线但靠谱的，是球棒浣熊。这叫做，具体的原型和抽象的角色。

**Nihilux:** And the abstract side of a character will only be further simplified in the eyes of the readers.

> **虚照：**而角色抽象的一面，又会在读者那里被进一步简化。

**Nihilux:** So, how do you think the readers across the universe view you Nameless?

> **虚照：**你们觉得，全宇宙的读者是怎么看待你们这些无名客的？

- ▸ Passersby.

> ▸ 过客。

**Nihilux:** Don't underestimate your influence.

> **虚照：**可不要小瞧了你们的影响力哦。

- ▸ Bullies.

> ▸ 恶霸。

**Nihilux:** That must be Oswaldo, right?

> **虚照：**那是奥斯瓦尔多吧。

- ▸ Heroes.

> ▸ 英雄。

**Nihilux:** Well said.

> **虚照：**说得不错。

**Nihilux:** Your heroic deeds and accomplishments drew my attention, and your demeanor gave me inspiration for the character in The Fluffy.

> **虚照：**因为英雄的壮举和事迹，所以关注你们；也因为你们展现出的气质，绒绒号里的角色才找到了灵感。

**Nihilux:** But while the hero identity boosts the character's popularity, it also becomes a limitation. That's why the little animals in The Fluffy have to defeat even more powerful villains and keep upping the ante to get more applause from the audience.

> **虚照：**但英雄的身份为角色带来人气的同时，也成为了束缚角色的枷锁。所以，绒绒号的小动物们必须打倒更多强大的反派，上演更精彩的故事，才能博得观众们的满堂彩。

**Nihilux:** In turn, the readers' perceptions and expectations constrain the character's growth. If he/she acts out of line, the popularity will crash.

> **虚照：**这样一来，读者的认知和期待也约束了角色的发展，要是她做出了违背形象的行为，人气很快就会一落千丈。

**Nihilux:** If he/she can't come up with new ideas or grow stronger, the audience will soon lose interest in the comic!

> **虚照：**要是她不能贡献出出人意料的点子，或是不能变得更强，观众们很快就会厌倦这部漫画的！

**Nihilux:** Unfortunately, the creation of Fluffy Across the Blue is now stuck at this very awkward bottleneck.

> **虚照：**很遗憾，《苍天航路绒绒号》的创作，正处于这样一个尴尬的瓶颈。

**Nihilux:** Honestly, after drawing comics for so long, I've pretty much run out of ideas.

> **虚照：**实不相瞒，画了这么久的漫画，我肚子里的货也差不多见底了。

**Nihilux:** Hundreds of Amber Eras ago, people would laugh out loud just from seeing a cat do a backflip.

> **虚照：**几百个琥珀纪前，人们会因为看到一只后空翻的猫开怀大笑。

**Nihilux:** Now, to get the same kind of enthusiastic reaction, you'd probably need to have that cat fight a mechatron in a one-on-one showdown in Pearluxe Tower.

> **虚照：**现在，想激起同样热烈的笑声，恐怕得让这只猫在珠星大厦和机铠一对一死斗。

**Nihilux:** Although The Fluffy specials are selling like crazy, doing side stories without progressing the main plot isn't a long-term solution.

> **虚照：**虽然绒绒号的日常特刊大卖特卖，但一直画番外篇，不推进主线也不是办法。

**Nihilux:** Ever since all the gossip Memokeepers I hired got wiped out in Amphoreus, my source of intel's been drying up fast.

> **虚照：**自从雇佣的八卦忆者全部折在翁法罗斯，我的素材来源就越来越少。

**Nihilux:** But if someone from the Astral Express could quietly feed me a few juicy insider secrets from the Phantasmoon Games, imagine the readers' reaction when the next chapter drops—

> **虚照：**但是嘛，如果有星穹列车的内部人员，悄悄地为我提供一些幻月游戏内部的秘辛，让读者在看最新话的时候，忍不住惊叹——

**Nihilux:** "Whoa! Does Blurry_QR_Colt have a time machine or what!?"

> **虚照：**「天哪！难道模糊二维马老师有时光机吗？」

**Nihilux:** That'd totally save The Fluffy's future sales.

> **虚照：**这样一来，绒绒号未来的销量就有救了。

**Nihilux:** After all, my little furbo crew already works for (Trailblazer). Just have them slip me a copy of the Phantasmoon Games press release. Let's call it buying your exclusive adaptation rights.

> **虚照：**反正我手下的狸猫崽子已经是开拓者的员工，让它们把幻月游戏的新闻稿顺便给我一份，就当是买下了你们的独家改编权。

**Nihilux:** We'll do a fifty-fifty split.

> **虚照：**到时候，我们五五分成。

- ▸ Deal.

> ▸ 成交。

**March 7th:** Heh, I've already figured out how to spend the royalties. First, I'll buy Pom-Pom ten new outfits, then get Himeko a brand-new coffee machine, and after that...

> **三月七：**嘿嘿，我都想好版权费怎么花了。首先给帕姆买十件新衣服，给姬子买台新的咖啡机，然后……

- ▸ I'll have to think about it.

> ▸ 我再考虑考虑。

**Nihilux:** What about a thirty-seventy split!?

> **虚照：**三七分成！

**(Trailblazer):** Deal.

> **开拓者：**成交。

**Nihilux:** Seventy for me, thirty for you!

> **虚照：**是你三我七喔！

**March 7th:** Wow, you change your face faster than Sparkle.

> **三月七：**哇，你变脸变得比花火还快。

**Nihilux:** Alright, since we've got the profit split sorted, looks like my creative block won't last much longer. Just wait for my appearance at "Star Rail FEST"!

> **虚照：**好了，既然大家把分成都谈妥了，创作瓶颈的解决也指日可待。就尽情期待「星铁FES」虚照老师真人登场吧!

**Nihilux:** "Star Rail FEST," a grand event that gathers millions of eyes and an inordinate amount of Wishpower. And under all that attention, the dark forces coveting the Master of Elation's blessing are bound to surface.

> **虚照：**「星铁FES」，一场汇聚百万双眼睛，聚集百万愿力的盛会。盛邀之下，觊觎欢愉之主赐福的种种黑暗力量，也必然会浮出水面。

**Nihilux:** Hehe, heroes and devils dancing together in Planarcadia. Tell me, is there a better source of inspiration than that?

> **虚照：**嘿嘿，英雄和魔鬼在乐园共舞，此时此刻，世界上还有比这更好的创作素材吗？

**Nihilux:** My hero, don't let us down.

> **虚照：**我的英雄，可别让大家失望哦。

### Head to Graphia Academy and continue your adventure

> *Our eyes have never met the ancient moon, yet this same moon once shone upon the people of the ancient times. If the Phantasmoon could feel, it would surely hold the memory of every being bathed in its light.*

[Recap] Himeko got her hands on some family notes about Phantasmoon Games that she believed might help you out. Ryusuke knew she's hiding something from him, but he didn't mind. As long as Himeko's alive, he's willing to wait for the day she's ready to share the real story behind her miraculous recovery from the Weathering Curse. Ryusuke opened up about the origins of their family name, Murata, and gave Himeko his full blessing to chart the world as she sees it, one Trailblazing step at a time.

Meanwhile, at the Graphia Academy...

**Himeko:** "A Supplicant is the master of Wishpower, but Wishpower can also become the shackles that bind the Supplicant, forcing them to fulfill its desires."

> **姬子：**「谒者是愿力的主人，但愿力也会成为束缚谒者的枷锁，迫使他们满足其愿望。」

**Himeko:** "Love and hatred are alike. Dependence resembles fear. What commands Wishpower isn't only love, but also hatred."

> **姬子：**喜爱与厌恶是相似的，依赖与恐惧是相似的，操控愿力的不仅可以是爱，也可以是恨。

**Himeko:** Thank you... These notes will really be a big help to (Trailblazer).

> **姬子：**谢谢…这些笔记应该能给开拓者提供不少帮助。

**Ryusuke:** Good. I'm so glad to be of help.

> **隆介：**那就好，能帮到你们就好。

**Ryusuke:** For generations, many members of our family have attempted to enter the Phantasmoon Games, hoping to rely on the Aeon's immense power to lift the curse afflicting the Graphia bloodline.

> **隆介：**我们家族历代有不少人都尝试过加入幻月游戏，想要依仗星神的伟力解除困扰绘世血脉的诅咒。

**Ryusuke:** Some came close to the Mask, but could only watch as history passed them by. Others wore it, only to fail in the end.

> **隆介：**他们有的与面具失之交臂，只能作为观测者见证历史。有的佩戴上面具，却最终功败垂成。

**Ryusuke:** That was, until four hundred years ago when your great-great-grandfather, Hiro, won the Phantasmoon Games and successfully gained an audience with Aha.

> **隆介：**直到四百年前，你的曾曾外祖父，弘赢得了幻月游戏的胜利，成功谒见了阿哈。

**Ryusuke:** But the family's curse was not broken. In his notes, he wrote—

> **隆介：**但，家族的诅咒并未断绝。他的笔记中这样写道——

**Ryusuke:** "Just as the God of Destruction cannot cradle a bouquet, so too is the God of Elation powerless against a curse, such is the cycle of cause and effect."

> **隆介：**「正如毁灭之神无法手捧花束，欢愉之神亦对诅咒无计可施，此为因果循环。」

**Himeko:** Father... are you wondering what it was that healed me?

> **姬子：**父亲，你是不是想问，是什么医好了我……

**Ryusuke:** I know you're keeping something from me. But I don't care what cured you fifteen years ago. As long as you're alive, I can wait until the day you're ready to tell me.

> **隆介：**我知道你有秘密瞒着我，但我不在乎十五年前是什么治好了你，只要你活着，我可以等到你愿意告诉我的那天。

**Ryusuke:** Still, as a father, there are things I must say, even if you don't want to hear them.

> **隆介：**但有些话，即使你会厌烦，身为父亲，我也不得不说。

**Ryusuke:** As an artist, I'm fascinated by fleeting lives: how they struggle, suffer, and in doing so, reveal a brilliance worthy of time's memory.

> **隆介：**身为艺术家，我钟情于见证，终将消逝的生命经由历练，展露出足以铭刻在时间上的光辉瞬间。

**Ryusuke:** But as a father, I can't stand by and watch my daughter walk into tragedy. "I bless you to be a hero, to welcome the same end as Akivili." Those are words I could never speak.

> **隆介：**作为不称职的父亲，我不想眼睁睁看着女儿走向悲剧。「我祝福你成为英雄，迎接和阿基维利一样的结局」，这样的话我说不出口。

**Himeko:** The road ahead is shrouded by uncertainty, and that's why someone must take the first step, to trailblaze a path others can follow.

> **姬子：**正因前路难测，才需要有人第一个迈出脚步，开拓出能让更多人走下去的道路。

**Ryusuke:** ...

> **隆介：**……

**Ryusuke:** I can't stop you, even though every part of me wishes you'd stay. But this is your choice, my daughter's choice. All I can do is stand by you quietly and pray you'll come back safely.

> **隆介：**所以我没法阻止你，即使我无比希望你能留下来。这是我女儿的选择，我只能默默支持她，祈祷她平安归来。

**Ryusuke:** Do you still remember the bedtime story I used to tell?

> **隆介：**还记得我常讲的那个睡前故事吗？

**Ryusuke:** They say there was once a princess in the sky. Day and night, she painted in her celestial garden, untouched by the joys and sorrows of the mortal world.

> **隆介：**传说天上有一位公主，她每日每夜在花园中作画，不知人间的喜悲。

**Ryusuke:** One night, a thunderous sound echoed from her painting. Curious, she stepped inside, through seas of flowers, across mountains, until she reached the world's edge.

> **隆介：**一天夜里，画里传来一阵巨响，疑惑的她走入画中，穿过花海与群山，直至世界边缘。

**Ryusuke:** There, stood an ancient tower, so tall it pierced the heavens, so vast it seemed to hold the universe itself.

> **隆介：**那里有一座古老的高塔，它贯穿天地，仿佛容纳了整个宇宙。

**Himeko:** Climbing the endless staircase, the princess reached the summit, and from there, she saw the world beyond her garden. In the distance, she heard cries, the voices of people, burning in raging fire.

> **姬子：**沿着阶梯攀援，在高塔顶端，公主看见了花园外的世界。有哭声从远方传来，那是燃烧着熊熊大火的人间。

**Himeko:** From that "Tower of Infinity" descended the world-painter, to walk the mortal realm. With a single stroke of her brush, she brought forth a thousand imagenae, turning the tide against Destruction.

> **姬子：**从「无限的高塔」中降生的绘世踏入尘世，她挥动画笔创造出千百的幻造种，为对抗毁灭的战场带来了转机。

**Himeko:** To go where there are cries, to go where there are flames. That is the origin of our family name, Murata.

> **姬子：**去有人哭喊的地方，去有火燃烧的地方，这便是我们家族的姓氏「无量塔」的由来。

**Ryusuke:** But beyond the family name, my interpretation of our ancestor's name, "Graphia," is also the hope I place in you.

> **隆介：**但除却姓氏外，对先祖名讳「绘世」的解读，也是我送给你的期许。

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, whatever choice you make... if this is truly what you want, then go, use those steps blazed upon trails to paint the world as you see it.

> **隆介：**姬子，无论你做出什么选择…如果这就是你想做的，那就去「绘世」吧，去用开拓的脚步描绘你眼中的世界吧。

**Himeko:** Thank you, Dad.

> **姬子：**谢谢你，爸爸。

**Himeko:** By the way, how's your... health? You haven't picked up any bad habits like smoking or drinking while I was away, have you?

> **姬子：**对了，你的身体状况…还好吗？我不在的这些年里，没有沾上烟酒之类的毛病吧？

**Ryusuke:** Hah, as the dean of Graphia Academy, I still have to set a good example for my students. It's just... my tendinitis keeps acting up, my herniated disc is getting worse.

> **隆介：**堂堂绘世学院的校长，还是要给学生们做表率的。就是最近腱鞘炎老是复发，腰间盘突出也越来越严重了。

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, how long will you be staying?

> **隆介：**姬子，你会在这里停留多久？

**Himeko:** At least until the lunar cycle shifts, I'll stay here in Planarcadia until then.

> **姬子：**至少在月相变化之前，我暂时不会离开二相乐园的。

**Ryusuke:** I see. If only the Phantasmoon could stay full forever...

> **隆介：**是吗？是幻月永远满盈就好了……

**Ryusuke:** Himeko... safe travels on the trails ahead.

> **隆介：**姬子，祝你旅途顺遂。

## 180　Simulacra and Simulation, or Even the Just　拟像与仿真，或向正义追索

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Parlor Car　|　角色：Blazeweaver; Bullet Wyrm Express; Evanescia; March 7th; Aeon ★ Aha; Trailblazer; Welt; Himeko; Poofy; Snowie; Sparxie; Azurewing; Magnus; Dawnlance; Ashveil; Kuchiba; Serval; Feld　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''The Bum's as Holy as the Seraphim'' completed

### Head to the Parlor Train and continue your adventure

> *Legend has it that the rails connect to gateways leading to other worlds. For you all, what you're about to witness might as well be one of those other worlds.*

[Recap] According to the news, the construction of the Star Rail FEST venue was officially completed. On the first day, three exhibition areas were opened to the public: Belobog area, the Xianzhou Luofu area, and Penacony area. Several members of the Astral Express Crew were invited to visit.

**Poofy:** Hello everyone, welcome to Fulwish TV's special news segment, Arcadian Morning Report! I'm your host, Poofy!

> **噗噗：**大家好，欢迎收看满愿电视台《乐园早知道》特别新闻栏目！我是主持人噗噗！

**Snowie:** Hi everyone, I'm your host, Snowie!

> **雪雪：**大家好，我是主持人雪雪！

**Snowie:** The "Star Rail Fest" exhibition venue, funded by Pearluxe Corp and constructed by Turbogen Heavy Industries, has officially been completed.

> **雪雪：**由珠星财团出资，速建重工承接建设的「星铁FES」展会现场正式竣工。

**Poofy:** Thanks to modern imagenesis technology, it took only a day for the skyline's Lookout Cloud to take on a completely new look.

> **噗噗：**凭借现代化的幻造技术，仅耗时一日，天际线观览云岛上就展现一派全新气象。

**Snowie:** Starting today, the Lookout Cloud Line is officially opening up a stop for the Star Rail Fest exhibition areas.

> **雪雪：**从今天开始，「观览云岛线」正式开通「星铁FES」展区站点。

**Snowie:** The exhibition areas opening on day one include the Belobog Area, Xianzhou Luofu Area, and Penacony Area, showcasing the legendary adventures of the Astral Express. The Amphoreus Area, which is currently under construction, will open in due course.

> **雪雪：**首日开放的展区包含「贝洛伯格区」、「仙舟罗浮区」、「匹诺康尼区」三处，为您呈现星穹列车的传奇冒险之旅。在建中的「翁法罗斯区」也将随后续计划开放。

**Poofy:** For this event, Pearluxe cordially invites several crew members of the Astral Express to visit and be interviewed, including (Trailblazer), who recently joined the Phantasmoon Games as a Supplicant.

> **噗噗：**在本次活动中，珠星诚邀星穹列车的多位乘员莅临参观、接受采访——其中包括最近加入「幻月游戏」，成为谒者的开拓者。

**Snowie:** That is certainly something to look forward to! I heard Pearluxe has also invited Blurry_QR_Colt, the author of Fluffy Across the Blue, for a signing session. It'll be the first time the author reveals their true face in public...

> **雪雪：**那可真是太令人期待了！听说珠星还邀请到了《苍天航路绒绒号》的漫画作者模糊二维马老师到场签售，这也会是作者本人首次以真面目亮相……

[Recap] An exhibition gathering Wishpower would indeed benefit both Pearluxe Corp and the Astral Express. However, Himeko was worried that Star Rail FEST might become a target for the new Herald of Death, and she reminded you to stay vigilant.

**Himeko:** An exhibition to gather Wishpower, now that's an interesting scheme. Leave it to Pearl to come up with such a bizarre idea.

> **姬子：**一场敛聚愿力的展会，确实是个有趣的企划。得亏真珠能想出这个奇怪的点子。

- ▸ Well, that is Pearluxe's forte.

> ▸ 毕竟这是珠星的优势。

**Himeko:** Pearluxe excels at creating dreams for both children and adults, and the Astral Express is exactly the kind of otherworldly dream that fascinates the people of this world.

> **姬子：**珠星擅长为孩子和成年人营造梦想，而星穹列车正是个令这个世界倍感新奇的天外之梦。

- ▸ This arrangement works out well for both parties.

> ▸ 这样做倒是对双方都有利。

**March 7th:** Mm-hmm, Pearl is also mobilizing Pearluxe's resources to promote us.

> **三月七：**唔，真珠也在动用珠星的资源为我们进行宣传。

**Himeko:** When I talked with Pearl, I shared with her the idea of becoming a sail, advancing and retreating together. This collaboration could truly bring out the best of our respective strengths.

> **姬子：**在和真珠对谈时，我向她提过「成为风帆，共同进退」的理念。这一场合作，确实发挥出了我们各自的优势。

**Welt:** Sometimes I wonder if Jade invited us here with the intention of giving her colleagues a "winning edge" in the Phantasmoon Games...

> **瓦尔特：**我有时候也会猜想，翡翠将我们邀请到这里，是否有为自己的同僚送上「幻月游戏胜利砝码」的意图……

**Himeko:** For Pearl, this Aeonic game is probably also a good opportunity to observe and study the way we operate.

> **姬子：**对真珠而言，这场星神游戏恐怕也是学习和观察我们行动风格的好机会。

**Welt:** Though, compared to our usual opponents, this Intellitron executive is a surprisingly rational decision-maker. Her proposal for cooperation seems more trustworthy than most.

> **瓦尔特：**不过，比起咱们平日里打交道的对手，这位智械执行官倒是难得的理性决策者，她的合作提议相对更值得信任。

**Himeko:** It's not rationality that concerns me. It's the "irrational" factors...

> **姬子：**比起理性，我更担心「非理性」的因素……

> ▸ 你是说…新告死魔？

> **姬子：**没错。鸽川血案的消息已经在网上传开了，虽然「异常防御部」的人尽力压制了各种流言传播的势头，但许多人还是把它和十五年前的告死魔联系在了一起。

**Himeko:** With such a large crowd gathering there, this exhibition could very well become a target for the new Herald of Death.

> **姬子：**这场汇集人数众多的展会很有可能成为目标，被新告死魔利用。

**March 7th:** Pearl must have contingency plans for situations like this, right?

> **三月七：**真珠对这样的情况，应该不至于毫无准备吧？

- ▸ Maybe we're the ace up her sleeve.

> ▸ 也许我们就是暗手。

> ▸ 可能是那位路过的侦探。

**March 7th:** That old guy? No way!

> **三月七：**那个大叔？不会吧！

**Himeko:** Whatever Pearl has planned, we must consider the possibility that the Herald of Death might attack the venue. We won't just be there to showcase the Astral Express to the people of this world. We'll also need to stay on high alert for any suspicious activity.

> **姬子：**无论真珠有什么准备，我们都要将告死魔袭击现场的可能性考虑在内。这次出席，我们除了向这个世界的民众展示星穹列车的形象外，还要随时警惕任何异动。

**Welt:** It's about time. You should get going.

> **瓦尔特：**时间差不多了，你们该出发了。

> **瓦尔特：**列车上总得有人留守，陪陪列车长，也好随时响应突发事件啊。

**Welt:** You guys have fun out there, but stay safe. Contact me right away if anything comes up.

> **瓦尔特：**你们几个，游玩时也要注意安全，有任何情况随时联络我。

> ▸ 丹恒和星期日呢？

**Himeko:** They're both out on a mission with General Yao Guang. Dan Heng is tracking Blade's whereabouts, while Sunday is working with Miss Tingyun. Both of them are tied up. Looks like they won't be able to make it to the exhibition this time.

> **姬子：**他们俩都随爻光将军行动去了。丹恒在追踪刃的下落，星期日与停云小姐一起行动，都脱不开身。这一次的展会应该没法到场了。

> ▸ 那咱们走咯。

> **三月七：**得，分享一路见闻的重任又落在小三月的肩膀上咯。那我多拍几张照片，回来后好跟你们好好讲讲展会的事情。

**March 7th:** Star Rail FEST, here we come!

> **三月七：**出发，「星铁FES」！

*(Talk to Hostess)*

### Complete the stage performance at the Xianzhou Venue

> *All the world's a stage, and until you're thrust into the spotlight, you can't be sure what character you'll play, or how this performance will end.*

[Recap] The four Daybreak Warriors, who had been invited to play the High-Cloud Squadron, made their entrance and declared that Planarcadians' happiness should be under the protection of local heroes. In particular, their leader, Blazeweaver, announced: As Supplicants, the Daybreak Squadron challenges you.

**Hostess:** Welcome, dear visitors and spectators! At this moment, you've arrived along with the Astral Express at the ancient and mysterious... Xianzhou!

> **主持人：**各位游客、各位观众你们好！各位此刻正跟随星穹列车抵达古老神秘的——仙舟！

> **认真的观众：**是仙舟「罗浮」！

> **主持人：**没错——仙舟「罗浮」的鳞渊境！让我们感谢星穹列车的英雄开拓者，她不但拯救了罗浮仙舟，还顺带在今日拯救了我们这座小小的展馆！

**Hostess:** And now, it's showtime! Adapted from the true adventures of the Astral Express, we present: Battle at Scalegorge Waterscape! High-Cloud Squadron Assembles!

> **主持人：**接下来，我们将进入演出环节，根据星穹列车真实冒险旅程改编的《血战鳞渊境！云五战队集结！》

**Hostess:** Thanks to the support of Fulwish TV, we invited the four main stars of the action hero series Daybreak Squadron to perform in this show!

> **主持人：**感谢满愿电视台的支持——我们请来了英雄剧《破晓战队》的四位主角们担纲饰演！

**Hostess:** And now, (Trailblazer), please come up on stage and reenact that heart-stopping battle where you drove away the Lord Ravager!

> **主持人：**也请开拓者上台为我们重现那场驱逐绝灭大君，惊心动魄的战斗！

- ▸ Daybreak Squadron again?

> **曙光：**我们扮演的不是「仙舟的英雄」吗？为什么仙舟的英雄需要假他人之手来拯救自己的国度？

**Blazeweaver:** Same as us! Shouldn't the heroes of this world be able to protect our own people? Can't we be the ones to bring happy smiles to their faces?

> **赤焰：**就像我们，这个世界的英雄难道不能守护我们的人民吗？难道我们不能给人民带去幸福的笑容吗？

> **灰烬：**……

> ▸ 如果你们打算挑战我…

### It's time to keep going

> *"A mere trifle, it won't stop you from completing your work." That's what my editor always tells me, so don't sweat it. Just keep moving forward.*

[Recap] You told Evanescia the truth about the battle with Phantylia and how the High-Cloud Quintet had become a thing of the past. Evanescia thought the Abundance and the Elation made a pretty good pairing and suggested continuing on to check out the Penacony Exhibition Area.

**Evanescia:** So... don't tell me this whole Xianzhou story is also something Blurry made up...?

> **三月七：**至于云上五骁，那五个人建功立业的时代，已经是七百多年前了。

> **三月七：**听起来也许很离谱，仙舟人的寿命普遍都很长。你看过绒绒号的漫画，里面应该有介绍这些设定吧。

**Evanescia:** Yeah, immortality... Some see it as a curse. But personally, I don't think the Abundance is as terrifying as THEIR reputation suggests.

> **绯英：**有啊，长生不死…有人觉得那是诅咒。不过我却觉得，「丰饶」并没有祂的名声那么可怕。

> **开拓者：**差点忘了咱们现在已经不在仙舟了…敞开了谈谈丰饶，应该没问题吧。

> **三月七：**是没问题啦。但仙舟人对丰饶的态度也不算空穴来风——咱们和步离人交过手，和魔阴身打过架……

> **三月七：**啊，一想到要是当年那场仗没能打赢幻胧，宇审里可就诞生一个不死不灭的绝灭大君了。

> **绯英：**其实，在我看来，「丰饶」与「欢愉」的相性还挺不错的。

> **绯英：**毕竟，只有拥有无穷的生命，才能追求无穷的喜乐。

**March 7th:** Who exactly are you?

> **绯英：**和两位这一路，我玩得很开心。没想到，绒绒号背后的故事居然也这么有趣。

> **绯英：**怎么啦，美少女自带背景，不行啊？

**Evanescia:** I know you're never serious about anything, so I guess I'll just have to answer my own question.

> **绯英：**知道你这人没个正形，看来我只好自问自答了。

- ▸ Is it because of the person walking among the flowers?

> ▸ 因为一同走在花下的人？

**Evanescia:** You're quite the romantic, aren't you?

> **绯英：**你还蛮懂浪漫的嘛。

> **绯英：**不过，我要说，回~答~错~误~

**Evanescia:** I know you're never serious about anything, so I guess I'll just have to answer my own question.

**(Trailblazer):** You left your own special effects behind...

[Recap] Himeko thought that Evanescia's identity must be far from ordinary, given her ability to craft masks and turn people into Supplicants. The Daybreak Squadron's challenge wasn't over yet, and Pearl's people were on their way to the Penacony Exhibition Area to rendezvous with you.

**March 7th:** She's gone.

> **三月七：**她消失了。

**Himeko:** Unexpected people and unexpected things always make an appearance in the Phantasmoon Games... Although Miss Evanescia claims to be just a spectator...

> **姬子：**幻月游戏…总是会出现出乎意料的人，出乎意料的事情。虽然这位绯英小姐说她只是一个观众，但是……

**Himeko:** As someone who can create masks and allow others to become Supplicants... there's no way she's just some ordinary girl.

> **姬子：**对不起，是我太操心了。

**Himeko:** Alright, time for us to head out.

**Himeko:** Let's continue forward, to the next stop: the Penacony Exhibition Area.

**March 7th:** Darn it, this whole Daybreak Squadron business has got me on edge. Somehow I can't shake the feeling we're being watched.

> **三月七：**可恶，破晓战队这一出搞得我浑身不自在，总担心有人在偷偷盯着我们。

**Himeko:** Here's some good news: At least (Trailblazer) isn't completely without help. Pearl's people are on their way to the Penacony Exhibition Area to join up with us.

> **姬子：**说个好消息吧，至少开拓者不是孤立无援的，真珠派出的人正在赶往「匹诺康尼区」与我们汇合。

### Board the Express and head to "Penacony Area"

*(Enter Interplanar Jump!)*

**Express Announcement:** Welcome aboard, passengers! The Fluffy Shuttle Express is about to depart. Next stop: Penacony Exhibition Area.

> **列车广播：**乘客您好，绒绒号接驳列车即将起航。下一站，「匹诺康尼展区」。

**Livestream Chat:** Let's go, Astral Express!

**Express Announcement:** We're approaching the Penacony Exhibition Area. Please remain seated and hold on tight.

*(Exit Interplanar Jump!)*

[Recap] You arrived at the Penacony Exhibition Area, where the scene of the Express's collision with The Great Septimus was recreated. While searching for Pearl's contacts, your phone rang.

**Fervent Spectator:** Would I get run over by the Express if I lay down on the tracks?

**Dreamy Spectator:** Yep, and you'd get blasted straight into a comic book! Who knows, maybe you'll be reborn as some superhero or something!

**March 7th:** All the previous exhibitions were so hilarious, but they really nailed The Great Septimus part.

**March 7th:** So, where's the person Pearl sent?

[Recap] Sparxie called to remind you to check the news. You might think you scored a massive win in the Phantasmoon Games, but most people probably didn't see it that way.

**Sparxie:** Yoohoo~ How ya doing, Gray Hair?

> **开拓者：**我不会把面具还你的，死心吧你！

**Sparxie:** I heard about your big fight with the superheroes. And since I actually respect you as a rival, I thought I'd give you a heads-up.

> **火花：**我听说你和超级英雄们大打出手的事情啦，不过，出于惺惺相惜的对手情谊，我想给你提个醒——

**Sparxie:** You might think you scored a massive win in the Phantasmoon Games, but most people probably don't see it that way.

**Sparxie:** Anyway, the news is on right now. Why don't you go check it out?

[Recap] According to news reports, the Supplicant from Astral Express brutally attacked the Daybreak Squadron. Was using violent and unreasonable methods to strike down local guardians of the arcadia what you called "Trailblaze"?

**Poofy:** Just moments ago! An extremely violent incident broke out at the ongoing "Star Rail FEST" exhibition venue!

**Poofy:** The Astral Express Supplicant (Trailblazer) brazenly launched a brutal attack on the beloved local heroes, the Daybreak Squadron! The scene descended into total chaos!

**Snowie:** Oh my! This is terrifying! Didn't this Nameless just defeat Sparxie and save Graphia Academy?

[Recap] Sparxie said people online were calling you a violent maniac, and some were even accusing you of being the mastermind behind the bloodbath in Dovebrook and the second Herald of Death. You suspected someone's out to get you. Sparxie then sent you an interview video about you.

[Recap] Your interview with Fulwish TV was edited into a totally out-of-context hit piece. In the video, you trashed Planarcadia and said you'd rather use violence to take down the local Supplicants to protect the IPC's interests.

**Reporter:** Hi, (Trailblazer)! Sorry to bother you! We're with Fulwish TV! It's our lucky day running into you here! Do you have a moment?

> **记者：**你好，开拓者！打扰了！我们是满愿电视台的！真幸运能在这儿碰到您！您现在有空吗？

**(Trailblazer):** Keep it brief, I've got plans later.

> **开拓者：**简短点，我稍后还有安排。

**Reporter:** First question: Seeing as Planarcadia was the starting point of the Express's new voyage, how would you describe your impressions of this place?

> **记者：**第一个问题：作为列车重新启程的站点，我想先请您谈谈自己对二相乐园的感想。

**(Trailblazer):** Planarcadia... Everyone's chasing the latest trends, looking for fun.

> **开拓者：**二相乐园…人人都在追逐热点，寻找乐子。

**Reporter:** Interesting. Throughout the history of the Phantasmoon Games, it's been extremely rare to have such a renowned cosmic figure as yourself participating. As the Astral Express's Supplicant, what kind of changes do you hope to bring to this world in these Games?

> **记者：**有意思。幻月游戏的历史上，像您这样知名的银河大人物参与可是极其罕见的事。作为星穹列车的谒者，您在这场「游戏」中想要为这个世界带来怎样的改变？

**(Trailblazer):** Show everyone the strength of the Trailblaze, to change this world!

> **开拓者：**向大家展示「开拓」的力量，去改变这个世界！

**Reporter:** That's an impressive answer! Speaking of which, after joining the games, you immediately went to defeat Miss Sparxie, who represented the Masked Fools. What are your thoughts on potentially facing native Supplicants in the future?

> **记者：**令人印象深刻的回答！说到这个，您加入游戏后率先击败了代表假面愚者的火花小姐。对于未来可能面临的本土谒者的挑战，您作何感想？

**(Trailblazer):** The other native Supplicants... way too dangerous. I'd rather defeat them through violence.

> **开拓者：**其他的本土谒者…太危险了。我更愿意靠暴力打败他们。

**Reporter:** Speaking of local Supplicants, we've noticed that "Star Rail FEST" is Pearluxe Corp's way of rolling out the red carpet for the Express. There are rumors that the IPC is also sponsoring the Express. So, I have to ask: Are you representing the Express or the IPC in these Games?

> **记者：**说起本土的谒者，我们注意到「星铁FES」是由珠星财团为列车举办的欢迎仪式。有传言说公司也是列车的资助者，请问您这一次参与「游戏」代表的究竟是列车还是公司？

**(Trailblazer):** At the end of the day, this is a festival for the IPC's interests. Madam Pearl and Pearluxe extended such a warm invitation. I'm not one of the guests. I'm the master of this place.

> **开拓者：**说到底，公司的利益。真珠女士和珠星盛情邀请，我不是宾客，我是这里的主人。

[Recap] Ashveil and Kuchiba regrouped with you, claiming that Fulwish might be the mastermind behind both the Smiles Society and the Daybreak Squadron, and that she was plotting something for the Phantasmoon Games. Himeko revealed that Fulwish was her schoolmate, the only student to survive the Bloodstained Games at Graphia Academy fifteen years ago. Kuchiba suggested continuing to the top of Pom-Pom tower to face the Daybreak Squadron's second attack.

**March 7th:** W—Wait, what!? This... This is a totally malicious edit to frame (Trailblazer)! How shameless!

> **三月七：**啊这…这、这这这！这根本就是恶意剪辑栽赃开拓者吧！太不要脸了！

- ▸ Well, technically those were all my words...

> ▸ 虽然每个字都是我说的…

**(Trailblazer):** ...Indeed, every word came from my mouth. But when pieced together, it turned into a declaration of war.

> **开拓者：**…确实，每个字都出自我的嘴巴。但拼凑起来，就变成了宣战发言。

- ▸ They sure know how to twist other people's words...

> ▸ 他们可真会断章取义…

**Himeko:** Over these fifteen years, the nature of competition in the Phantasmoon Games has evolved. The Daybreak Squadron's taunts were just smoke and mirrors. The real game is being played in the shadows, where our opponents have mastered the art of weaponizing public opinion.

> **姬子：**十五年过去了，幻月游戏的竞争方式，发生了某种变化。破晓战队的挑衅，反倒成了烟雾弹…对手深谙如何操控舆论的利刃在台下出招。

**Himeko:** Come to think of it, it's only natural. After all, the true stars of the Games were never the Supplicants on stage... but rather the audience whose gazes they command.

> **姬子：**细想也是必然。毕竟这场「游戏」真正的主角，从来不是台上的谒者…而是那些目光所系的观众。

**???:** Indeed, the audience is all fired up, waiting to see you work your magic, (Trailblazer)...

> **？？？：**确实，观众们现在可是热血沸腾，等着看开拓者大显神威呢…

**Ashveil:** But you know what~ I bet what they really want to see deep down is the Daybreak Squadron taking the Astral Express down a peg or two.

> **不死途：**不过嘛~我敢打赌，他们心底更想看到的，是破晓战队狠狠挫一挫星穹列车的锐气。

**Ashveil:** Well, well, if it isn't the folks from the Astral Express. Fancy meeting you again.

> **不死途：**哟，星穹列车的各位，又见面了。

**Kuchiba:** This storm online... is several times more intense than the day you stopped SparxiCon.

> **朽叶：**网上的风暴…比你们阻止「火花大会」那一天，更猛烈数倍。

**March 7th:** Miss Kuchiba! You've seen all this fake news too, right?

> **三月七：**朽叶小姐！你也看到这些假新闻了吧？

- ▸ Aren't you guys a bit late for this?

> ▸ 你们来得有些晚了吧？

**Ashveil:** Sorry, got a bit... held up on the way. Since I entered the exhibition area, at least seven folks stopped me asking for the autograph of this guy called Welt.

> **不死途：**抱歉，路上…有点小耽搁。自打进了展区，至少遇到七个家伙堵着我索要那个叫「瓦尔特」的签名。

**Ashveil:** So, who is this Welt?

> **不死途：**所以，瓦尔特是谁？

- ▸ What are you here for?

> ▸ 你们来做什么？

**Kuchiba:** Pearl assigned us to investigate the Synwish Syndicate massacre... but every suspicious thread we followed led us from the Research Society of Happy Smiles straight to... the Daybreak Squadron.

> **朽叶：**真珠指派我们追踪共愿帮的灭门惨案…但种种可疑的蛛丝马迹，把线头从幸福微笑研究会一路牵上了…破晓战队。

**Ashveil:** And then there's the one pulling the strings behind the scenes: Fulwish.

> **不死途：**然后是那个在幕后编织蛛网的家伙——满愿。

**Himeko:** Fulwish...?

> **姬子：**满愿……？

**Ashveil:** Oh? You know that name? Now this is getting interesting.

> **不死途：**怎么，听过这个名字？那就有意思了。

**Himeko:** She was my junior at school. Fifteen years ago, she was also the only student who survived at Graphia Academy.

> **姬子：**她是我的学妹，十五年前，她也是绘世学院现场唯一生还的学生。

**Ashveil:** The good news is, your junior is living better than anyone else. She owns TV stations, charity organizations, has a huge business empire, and wields both wealth and power.

> **不死途：**好消息是，你这位学妹活得比谁都滋润。名下有电视台、慈善组织，家大业大，有钱有势。

**Ashveil:** As for the bad news... She's probably plotting something for the Phantasmoon Games, and I mean the worst kind of something. My gut tells me that the Synwish Syndicate massacre... well, that's probably on her tab too.

> **不死途：**坏消息嘛…她恐怕想在幻月游戏期间谋划点什么，最最糟糕的那种。我的直觉告诉我，共愿帮满门的血债…恐怕也得记在她的账上。

**Kuchiba:** Madam Pearl sent Mr. Ashveil to ensure everyone's safety... Of course, I understand that with the Nameless' skills, there's no real need for extra protection. But Madam Pearl didn't want to take any chances.

> **朽叶：**真珠女士把不死途先生派来负责诸位的安全…当然，我明白，以无名客的身手本不需要加派人手保护，但真珠女士不想冒任何风险。

**Ashveil:** Long story short, feel free to use me as muscle. No need to be shy about it.

> **不死途：**简而言之，请各位把我当黑手套来用就成，不必客气。

- ▸ I'm counting on you then.

> ▸ 那就拜托了。

**March 7th:** So, are we still heading to the top of Pom-Pom tower?

> **三月七：**所以我们还继续去帕姆塔顶吗？

**Kuchiba:** We should keep moving forward. If my guess is right, the Daybreak Squadron will launch their second attack there.

> **朽叶：**我们应该继续前进。如果我猜的没错，破晓战队还会在那里进行第二次袭击。

**Ashveil:** Forget about any fantasies of fairness and harmony. From this moment on, the Phantasmoon Games could follow in the footsteps of what happened fifteen years ago...

> **不死途：**抛下公平公正、和谐友好的幻想吧。打从此刻起，幻月游戏随时都可能要步十五年前的后尘了——

**Ashveil:** Once this battle turns bloody, fear will seep through the screen and become some Supplicant's Wishpower...

> **不死途：**一旦这场战斗向流血的方向发展，恐惧会透过屏幕，成为某位谒者的愿力……

**Ashveil:** See, that's the problem with modern society. Good news stays home while bad news spreads like wildfire.

> **不死途：**瞧，这就是现代社会的弊端，好事不出门，坏事传千里。

### Fuel up the Express

> *Look, the wheels are turning, passing through the plains deep in the valley. To the dawn, the wheels are singing, for an unfinished dream. Look, the Express is racing across the vast wilderness beneath the darkness. We run, we move forward... Let us play the horizon's echo from afar!*

*(Talk to March 7th, optional)*

**March 7th:** Daybreak Squadron... I thought they were fans of the Express too, but in the blink of an eye, we somehow became enemies.

> **三月七：**破晓战队…本以为他们也是列车的粉丝，没想到转眼间大家变成了敌人。

**Himeko:** They might not be the real enemy here. Someone could be pulling their strings from the shadows.

> **姬子：**也许这些人未必是敌人，他们可能受到了背后之人的挑唆。

**Himeko:** If possible, I'd rather persuade them to back down, since once a battle starts, it'll be like pouring fuel into a fire, stoking the audience's bloodlust...

> **姬子：**如果可以，我更希望说服他们退让——一旦开战，就会像火里倾倒燃油一样，在观众心中激起好战的情绪……

**Himeko:** A Phantasmoon Game like that would only descend into madness. And I certainly do not want to see what happened fifteen years ago repeat itself on these lands.

> **姬子：**这样的「游戏」只会陷入疯狂，我不希望十五年前的景象再度出现在这片土地上。

*(Talk to Ashveil, optional)*

**Ashveil:** Oh, I know about this exhibition area! Thanks to the comic Miss March gave me.

> **不死途：**啊，这个展区的内容我知道，得亏看了三月小妹妹让给我的漫画。

**Kuchiba:** Huh, wouldn't have pegged you for a comic book reader.

> **朽叶：**看不出来，你居然还看漫画。

**Ashveil:** I flipped through it on my way to pay respects to Caustide. Even though that volume's story was pretty random and all over the place, it was quite interesting.

> **不死途：**去祭拜恶水的路上顺道翻了翻，虽然那一册故事前不着村后不着店，倒是有趣的很。

**Ashveil:** A group of furry animals took over the SoulGlad soda factory to wake up the other animals in Penacony...

> **不死途：**一群毛茸茸的动物为了唤醒匹诺康尼的其他动物，占领了苏乐达汽水工厂……

**Ashveil:** Using SoulGlad as fuel for the Express... Talk about forced product placement.

> **不死途：**用苏乐达给列车补充燃料…这广告打得也太生硬了。

**Kuchiba:** Time to get to work, detective.

> **朽叶：**该开工了，名侦探。

*(Approach Interplanar Jump!)*

**Himeko:** To move forward, we'll need to "fuel up" the shuttle Express first.

> **姬子：**要想往上走，得先给接驳的列车「加足燃料」。

**March 7th:** C'mon, let's "fuel up" the Express!

> **三月七：**来吧，让我们给列车「补充燃料」吧！

**March 7th:** Ready to roll? The Express is about to depart!

> **三月七：**准备好了吗，列车要发车了！

That was because the Nameless wanted to replace the natives' fantasies of heroes with their own image, all to feed their vanity disguised as the "spirit of Trailblaze." Every Daybreak Warrior except Cinderborne had undergone conversion through "Happiness Surgery," swearing to reclaim the heroic image in the hearts of the local residents from these outsiders.

**Azurewing:** We will prove to the people that we are true heroes who can protect their happiness!

> **苍翼：**我们会向人们证明，我们才是能守护他们幸福的英雄！

> **赤焰：**所谓的「拯救银河」，也许只是公司的一个企划…就像曾经的我们一样，在电视里卖力地演出，努力想去安慰电视机前的孩子——

**Blazeweaver:** They replaced our fantasies of heroes with their own image, all to satisfy their vanity called "the Trailblazing spirit"!

> **赤焰：**他们把我们这些原住民对「英雄」的幻想，替换成了他们的模样，只为满足他们名叫「开拓精神」的虚荣心！

**Blazeweaver:** Tell me, (Trailblazer), have you ever, even for a moment, felt what it's like to tremble on blood-soaked ground at dusk...

> **现场观众：**破晓战队，把这群外星佬揍趴下！

> **灰烬：**我们是破晓战队，是四人同心、保护世界的战士，我不会让伙伴们还有那些支持我们的人失望。

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Lookout Cloud Station　|　角色：Ashveil; Trailblazer; Azurewing; Blazeweaver; Magnus; Dawnlance; Himeko; March 7th; Pearl; Kuchiba　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Simulacra and Simulation, or Even the Just'' completed

### Fall into the unknown

> *Fall into the abyss. Fall to the planet's core. Fall into that vast secret. No signal. No bearings. No identity. Nothing but an endless echo.*

## 182　Real Holy Laughter!　多么神圣的笑声！

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Pearluxe Tower　|　角色：Phantasmoon; Trailblazer; Magnus; Ashveil; March 7th; Himeko; Kuchiba; Pearl; Evanescia; Yao Guang; Silver Wolf; Fulwish; Blade; Sunday　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Echoes from a Sombre Empire'' completed

### Speak with Pearl

> *Am I... in Pearl's office? It feels like I've just gone through something horrifying, like something was about to tear me to pieces. Take a breath, and talk to your companions.*

[Recap] Pearl explained that the place you fell into was the "torn scroll" peeled off from the "scroll painting" when Planarcadia returned to reality. Ashveil's shadows tasted the sins of the three Daybreak Warriors and declared the Dovebrook murder case closed. However, the odd part was that all three were Supplicants bearing flesh of the Abundance. Pearl suspected Fulwish was the mastermind behind this whole series of events and must be closely monitored.

**Pearl:** We meet again, everyone.

> **真珠：**又见面了，各位。

**Pearl:** Fortunately, (Trailblazer)'s lucky chip helped me locate you.

> **真珠：**幸好，那枚象征好运的筹码常伴开拓者身侧，帮助我锁定到了你们的坐标。

**Pearl:** Please accept my sincerest apologies for not intervening in time and putting all of you in danger.

> **真珠：**未能及时出手，而令诸位身陷险境，我必须诚挚道歉。

- ▸ You were tracking me!
- ▸ Thanks, but please don't gift me any more tracking devices.

**Ashveil:** Actually, she put one in my pocket too, but I auctioned it online as a souvenir. Thank goodness you kept yours, otherwise we'd be done for.

> **不死途：**她其实也在我兜里放了，但我把那玩意儿当成纪念品放网上拍卖了。得亏你还留着它，不然咱们就完蛋了。

**Himeko:** Madam Pearl, where exactly were we just now...?

> **姬子：**真珠女士，我们刚才身处的地方究竟是……？

**Pearl:** The place where you fell is called a "torn scroll." It is a space that peeled from the "scroll painting" during Planarcadia's return to reality. These splintered spaces continuously rip at both reality and the World in Canvas.

> **真珠：**诸位坠落之处被称为：「残卷」，是二相乐园从画中复归现实的过程中从「画卷」上剥离的空间，这些碎片空间一刻不停地撕扯着现实与画中世界。

**Pearl:** The Department of Aberration Defense originally thought this kind of phenomenon had stabilized... but based on observation: During your encounter with the Daybreak Squadron, there was a sudden spike of disturbances from the "torn scroll." Unfortunately, the cause: unclear.

> **真珠：**异常防御部本以为类似的现象已趋于稳定…但根据观测：就在诸位与破晓战队交手时，「残卷」的扰动突然加剧了。很遗憾，原因：尚不明了。

**Pearl:** Therefore, I had to use the power of the Cornerstone to analyze the space, then mend the fissure with imagenesis in order to bring you out. The Department of Aberration Defense has initiated emergency protocols to reassure the public and handle the aftermath.

> **真珠：**为此，我不得不启用基石的力量解析空间构成，以幻造技法填补裂隙，才将你们带离彼处。异常防御部已启动应急程序安抚民众，善后相关事宜。

**Pearl:** However, I wasn't able to directly observe your situation after you'd fallen into the "torn scroll." Would you be so kind as to share what happened there?

> **真珠：**不过，在诸位坠入「残卷」后的情况我无法观测，还请和我分享情报。

**Ashveil:** We can close the New Herald of Death case. My "shadow" has devoured the guilty three and tasted their sins.

> **不死途：**「新告死魔」一案，可以结案了。我的「影子」吞下了那三人，尝到了他们的罪业。

**Ashveil:** They were responsible for the Synwish Syndicate massacre. Of that I'm certain.

> **不死途：**他们犯下了共愿帮的灭门血案，这一点确凿无疑。

**Pearl:** Was Fulwish the mastermind?

> **真珠：**指使者，是满愿吗？

**Ashveil:** I couldn't find any solid proof of it, but it hardly matters now that the culprits have been executed.

> **不死途：**我没能从中找到相关的证据。不过就算找到了也没有意义，犯人已被处决了。

**Pearl:** Worry not. Based on the intel provided by you and Miss Kuchiba, I have deduced the following:

> **真珠：**无妨。汇总你和朽叶小姐行动所获的情报，我的推演已经成型：

**Pearl:** As a survivor of the tragedy of the "Bloodstained Games", Fulwish founded the "Research Society of Happy Smiles" charity organization.

> **真珠：**作为十五年前那场「血涂游戏」的幸存者，满愿成立了慈善团体「幸福微笑研究会」。

**Pearl:** The organization has since been providing an unapproved medical treatment named "Happiness Surgery" to its members, which neutralizes their physiological capacity to feel pain. The procedure drew many more followers as a result.

> **真珠：**长久以来，该组织为入会成员提供名为「幸福手术」的非法医疗方案，抹除其感知痛苦的生理机能，由此吸引了众多拥趸。

**Pearl:** And the Daybreak Squadron, members of the Smiles Society, imitated the Herald of Death's modus operandi for their crimes, and challenged the Astral Express during the exhibition.

> **真珠：**而幸研会的成员「破晓战队」模仿告死魔犯案，并于展会期间向星穹列车发起了挑战。

**Pearl:** From the aforementioned facts, the predicted probability of Fulwish being the mastermind is... 92%.

> **真珠：**在上述事实中，预测满愿身为指使者的概率是…92%。

**Kuchiba:** If I may, Madam Pearl. Management in Planarcadia should be dictated by the chain of evidence rather than extrapolated probability.

> **朽叶：**请容我提醒您，真珠女士，二相乐园的管理决策应当由证据链而非概率推演决定。

**Pearl:** I understand. I will observe the human laws and customs.

> **真珠：**我明白，我会遵守人类的法律与习俗。

**Pearl:** In any case, we have achieved our goal through Star Rail FEST: Luring the Supplicant with ill intent out of hiding.

> **真珠：**无论如何，我们通过这场「星铁FES」达成了目标：诱使心怀恶意的谒者浮出水面。

- ▸ The Daybreak Squadron has been defeated.
- ▸ It's the end of the line for Fulwish.
- ▸ That's right, but please get someone else on the job next time!

**Pearl:** You've all fought and worked hard. Please, allow me to apologize again.

> **真珠：**各位一路奋战辛苦了，容我再次致歉。

**March 7th:** Since Fulwish's exposed now, and we've defeated her Supplicant... shouldn't we hurry and arrest her as soon as possible?

> **三月七：**既然这个满愿已经浮出了水面，咱们也把她的谒者击败了…那是不是该尽早把她抓起来？

**Ashveil:** I'm afraid it's not that simple. We've defeated Fulwish's Supplicant, yes, however...

> **不死途：**事情恐怕没这么简单，我们击败了满愿派出的谒者，这一点确实没错。但是……

**Ashveil:** All three Daybreak Warriors were Supplicants.

> **不死途：**这三名破晓战士，通通是谒者。

> **姬子：**这不合理…谒者应当只有佩戴面具，受幻月认可踏入游戏的那一个才对。

**Ashveil:** Those devoured by my "shadow" can no longer lie nor deceive. All three of them had worn the mask before.

> **不死途：**「影子」所吞之物，无法再有任何隐瞒和谎言。这三名破晓战士，每一人都曾佩戴过面具。

**Himeko:** If they were all Supplicants... that means it's still possible for Fulwish to intervene in the games.

> **姬子：**如果他们都拥有谒者的身份，那就意味着…满愿还有继续介入游戏的余裕。

**Ashveil:** What's even more terrifying is, when the "shadow" devoured them, I caught a taste of something familiar...

> **不死途：**更令我毛骨悚然的是，在「影子」吞下他们三人的瞬间，我尝到了一股似曾相识的味道——

**Ashveil:** It was the taste of Abundance that I'd once devoured as a Galaxy Ranger...

> **不死途：**那是我身为巡海游侠时，曾吞噬过的「丰饶」的滋味.

**March 7th:** ...Wuh?

> **三月七：**…啥？

- ▸ They were transformed by the Abundance?

**Ashveil:** Yes. I didn't think this world would go that far... but I was wrong.

> **不死途：**嗯，我本以为这个世界不会疯到这个地步…但我错了。

- ▸ Your shadow once ate the Abundance's flesh?

**Ashveil:** That's something that... happened a long, long time ago.

> **不死途：**那已经是…很久很久以前的事了。

**Ashveil:** Living in seclusion dulls one's senses... I should've realized it earlier, darn it.

> **不死途：**隐居生活，终究还是让人变迟钝了…我早该发觉的，可恶。

**Pearl:** Assessing the risk involved in this procedure...

**Pearl:** There is no point in beating yourself up over it. Therefore, the conclusion must be corrected: The Research Society's "Happiness Surgery" is a medical technology related to the Abundance, and not simply about relieving pain.

**Pearl:** ...Unable to extrapolate.

> **真珠：**……………无法推演。

**Himeko:** General Yao Guang told us that she foresaw a great calamity upon Planarcadia. According to her divinations, Blade, the Stellaron Hunter, could be its catalyst.

> **姬子：**爻光将军曾告知我们，她在占卜时预见二相乐园将面临一场巨大灾厄。按她的解读，星核猎手刃可能是这场灾难的起点。

**Pearl:** So that's why she asked me for the authority to extradite Blade. Regardless, none of us had anticipated this: Planarcadia's calamity had already taken root, long before the arrival of the Stellaron Hunter.

> **真珠：**难怪她曾向我索要引渡刃的权力。但也许超出我们所有人想象的是：二相乐园的灾劫早就悄然根植，远比星核猎手的到来要更早。

- ▸ Then, let's strike Fulwish where it hurts?
