**Pearl:** These options imply three different strategies. Regardless of the approach, the respective timing and investment must be considered.

> **真珠：**那意味着三种截然不同的战术。无论选择哪条道路，都要考虑相应的时机与代价。

- ▸ What does Fulwish really want?

**Himeko:** Does she want revenge against the long-dead Herald of Death?... Or is she trying to erase the memories of that tragedy?

> **姬子：**她是想对早已死去的告死魔进行复仇吗…还是想抹去那段悲剧历史？

**Pearl:** It's frivolous to speculate her motives. Unlike Intellitrons, humans may weave an intricate plan for a mad reason at any cost, yet execute it with impeccable rationality at every step. What's important is that she is stopped.

> **真珠：**揣测她的动机毫无意义。比起智械，人类可能因为一个疯狂的理由而不惜代价编织某个计划，而执行计划的每一步却又充满了无懈可击的理性。重要的是，阻止她。

**Pearl:** Thank you for your "surprise" intel, Mr. Ashveil. You have just saved many more lives.

> **真珠：**不死途先生，感谢你带给我的「惊喜」情报。于无形中，你挽救了许多人的性命。

**Pearl:** Henceforth, the Department of Aberration Defense will place Fulwish and the members of the Research Society of Happy Smiles on a special watch list. Given Fulwish's control over media outlets, I recommend closer monitoring of her dissemination channels.

> **真珠：**由此刻起，「异常防御部」必须将满愿和幸研会的成员列入重点监视名单。基于满愿本人掌控着媒体，我希望更密切关注其发布信息的渠道。

**Kuchiba:** Understood. We will also implement pre-disaster measures at the Calamity-level standard per the Safety Response Protocol Against Aberrant Threats.

> **朽叶：**明白，同时我们也会按照《异常危害安全应对法》，以「祸」级标准进行灾前对策准备。

**Pearl:** Fulwish is not working alone. For normal people to be able to get an Abundance Transmutation, that implies far more sinister supporters behind the scenes. These are enemies not to be easily contended with...

> **真珠：**满愿绝非一人在行动。能令普通人获得丰饶转变，意味着在她背后还有其他怀有巨大恶意的支持者。那应该不是本土力量可以轻易抗衡的敌人……

**Pearl:** Therefore, as the Administrator, I beseech you all: Please, lend your aid and help Planarcadia through this crisis!

> **真珠：**在此，我以世界管理者的名义向各位郑重请求：请帮助二相乐园度过眼前的危机！

**Pearl:** This has escalated beyond mere rivalry for the Aeonic game. Fulwish's conspiracy has crossed the line. This situation's a hundred times more dire than the Herald of Death incident fifteen years ago.

> **真珠：**无关星神游戏的胜负，满愿的阴谋已跨过了界限——这比十五年前的告死魔事件严重百倍。

- ▸ I won't allow something like that to happen.
- ▸ I should have read the small print of this "vacation"...
- ▸ I have Skott's score to settle with her.

**Himeko:** Please, Madam Pearl. This is my home world, and I won't allow the tragedy to repeat itself. Besides, there may be an even greater conspiracy behind all this... As a Nameless, it is my duty to see it through to the end.

> **姬子：**真珠小姐言重了，这里是我的故乡，我不会坐视十五年前的悲剧重演。更何况…眼前所面临的危机背后也许潜藏着更大的阴谋，作为无名客，我本就有义不容辞的责任。

**Ashveil:** ...Hmph. Indeed, there are old scars and fresh wounds to settle. For the way she toys with life, and the taste of that flesh... Fulwish must be held accountable once and for all.

> **不死途：**…哼。新仇旧恨，确实该好好算算了。她玩弄生命的方式，还有那份血肉的味道…都需要一次彻底的清算。

**Pearl:** You have my eternal gratitude. I won't forget this bond from our shared trials. Now then...

> **真珠：**感激不尽。这份共同进退的情谊，我会铭记于心。那么……

**Pearl:** I shall prepare an action plan as well as share relevant information with General Yao Guang. The Seed of Abundance planted by Fulwish must be uprooted and eradicated as soon as possible.

> **真珠：**我会着手开始制定计划，同时，我也会将相关情报和爻光将军分享——满愿种下的丰饶之种，必须尽快连根拔起。

*(Unlock Achievement Lucky Chip)*

*(Listen to Himeko and March 7th, optional)*

**Himeko:** Fulwish's partner.... Perhaps someone far more dangerous has already set their sights on this round of the Phantasmoon Games.

**March 7th:** Oh no, they are definitely up to no good.

[Recap] Pearl believed that humanity's profound understanding of "negative feedback" was one of the wellsprings of artistic power. The Happiness Surgery obliterated humanity's perception of pain, thereby stripping away human complexity.

*(Talk to Pearl)*

**Pearl:** Is there something else I can help you with, (Trailblazer)?

> **真珠：**开拓者，还有什么想问的吗？

- ▸ The Smiles Society has been around for a while...

**Pearl:** I understand. Your inquiry: How did they manage to operate for so many years unnoticed?

> **真珠：**我明白，你想问：幸研会发展成如今的样子，难道你们对此毫无察觉？

- ▸ How are you unaware of what they've been doing?

**Pearl:** I had put them on a temporary watch list before, due to significant decreases in crime rates in certain regions.

> **真珠：**我曾因部分地区的犯罪率显著降低，而将幸研会列入短期观察。

**Pearl:** But now, we finally know how they achieved such a miracle.

> **真珠：**但现在我们都知道了，他们是通过什么手法创造这一奇迹的。

**Pearl:** Personally speaking, I cannot fault the citizens who joined the Society, nor do I wish to fixate on the accountability of those who governed at the time.

> **真珠：**仅以我个人视角而言，我无法苛责那些加入幸研会的公民，也不想追究当时管理者的过失。

**Pearl:** After all, for the corporation, the Smiles Society is a tool for maintaining stability, and the members are simply following the human instinct to avoid suffering.

> **真珠：**毕竟对群体而言，幸研会是维持稳定的称手工具。而对会众来说，逃避痛苦是人类的天性。

**Pearl:** You've been to Penacony, (Trailblazer). You should know: There, the/The Family's members lull guests to sleep with power of the Harmony and soothe their pain. From a human ethics point of view, it is well within people's rights to avert pain in moderation.

> **真珠：**开拓者，你去过匹诺康尼，应当知晓：在那里，家族成员会以「同谐」的力量催人入梦，安抚人们的痛苦。从人类伦理上来说，适度的逃避痛苦是无可厚非的权利。

**Pearl:** Humans cannot turn off their innate "negative feedback" like Intellitrons do. I understand and deeply sympathize with that.

> **真珠：**人类无法像智械一样关闭与生俱来的「负反馈」，我深深理解并同情这一点。

**Pearl:** My assigned theme is "Analytical Art." But at the same time, I am fortunate to be appointed governor of a world.

> **真珠：**作为一名智械，我被分配的母题是「解析艺术」。但同时，我又有幸得到了治理这个世界的任命。

**Pearl:** The portrayal and sublimation of pain in human works of art shine among humanity's greatest treasures. Tragedy, elegy, and lost epics... Their soul-stirring power springs from a profound understanding of this "negative feedback."

> **真珠：**在人类的艺术作品中，对痛苦的描绘与升华，是最为璀璨的瑰宝之一。悲剧、挽歌，还有失落的史诗…它们震撼灵魂的伟力，来自于人类对「负反馈」的深刻理解。

**Pearl:** This so-called "Happiness Surgery" does more than just obliterate people's perception of pain. It strips away human complexity, and turns what was dynamic and brilliant into a monotonous blotch.

> **真珠：**所谓的「幸福手术」，抹去的不仅仅是人类痛苦的感知。它篡改了人类的复杂性，将人类这幅斑斓的画作简化成了单一的色块。

**Pearl:** To me, governing this world is like creating a painting. My vision is to maintain the diversity of lifeforms and preserve their potential for growth and change. The Research Society has disrespected my work.

> **真珠：**对我来说，管理世界即是作画——维持其上生命形态的丰富与演化潜力。幸研会亵渎了我的作品。

**Pearl:** Thus, my duty and theme have become one and the same: I will do all that I can to cleanse the misguided strokes of the Research Society from this land.

> **真珠：**因此，我的职责与母题在此刻合为一题：我会尽我所能清除幸研会在这片土地上留下的错笔。

**Pearl:** Thank you again for lending your aid, Nameless. I will begin extrapolation of our next steps now.

> **真珠：**再次感谢你们愿意伸出援手，无名客。接下来，我将开始进行之后的决策推演。

- ▸ I want to go over things with everyone else.

**Pearl:** Please, be my guest.

> **真珠：**请各位自便。

- ▸ See you later, Madam Pearl.

**Pearl:** I hope you all get some proper rest before moving out again.

> **真珠：**望诸位能在行动前好好休息。

[Recap] Kuchiba said the Department of Aberration Defense would interrogate Cinderborne and dig up intel on the Smiles Society. As for dealing with Fulwish from here on out... that's all you, hero of the Express.

*(Talk to Kuchiba, optional)*

**Kuchiba:** Leaving now, (Trailblazer)? We're counting on you, hero of the Express.

- ▸ You look fairly relaxed about it all.

**Kuchiba:** Because it's just another Phantasmoon Games for me. Check the records in the Ministry, and you'll see. Save the mythological times, they always end up in a huge mess, without fail.

> **朽叶：**要走了吗，开拓者？接下来的事情就拜托你了，列车的英雄。

**Kuchiba:** Maybe that's precisely why Aha designed it this way, so THEY can watch humanity stumble over arbitrary obstacles we impose on ourselves and others. Otherwise, THEY'd probably die from the boredom of an orderly universe.

> **朽叶：**因为我见多识广。翻开史料记录，刨除神话时代，哪一次幻月游戏不是一地鸡毛？

- ▸ How will you deal with Cinderborne?

**Kuchiba:** Maybe interrogate him for a few days. Get a strong light, render him unable to eat or sleep well, and he will probably spill everything he knows about the Smiles Society.

> **朽叶：**也许阿哈设计这场游戏的目的，正是为了看人类毫无必要地给自己和同胞的命运加点料，要不然祂会死于整个宇宙无聊的运转。

**Kuchiba:** And once we've picked his brain, we'll let him go.

> **朽叶：**也许会审讯个好几天，打上个强光灯，让他睡不好吃不香，然后他应该就会说说自己知道的幸研会内幕。

- ▸ I should go, Miss Kuchiba.

**Kuchiba:** It's almost time. I should get back to work too.

> **朽叶：**等什么都挖不出来，就放了他。

*(Subsequent interactions)*

**Kuchiba:** It's almost time. I should get back to work too.

> **朽叶：**时间差不多了，我也该去办正经事了。

[Recap] Cinderborne couldn't believe that his companions were the culprits behind the bloodshed, and Fulwish was secretly plotting something terrible. Cinderborne thanked you for your words of comfort, musing that he had thought of himself as a hero, but in the end, he was just a mere actor.

*(Talk to Cinderborne, optional)*

**Cinderborne:** ...So it's true, the captain and the rest really did kill all those people from the Synwish Syndicate. Miss Fulwish is also plotting something terrible...

**Cinderborne:** This is absurd. It's been fifteen years since my life turned into a montage of nightmarish amalgams... and it's still going.

- ▸ You need to rest.
- ▸ Let me handle the rest.

**Cinderborne:** Thank you, (Trailblazer). Mr. Ashveil is right. This is... not the same as playing the hero on a stage.

> **灰烬：**…所以，真的是队长他们杀害了共愿帮的人，满愿小姐也在暗中谋划什么可怕的事情……

**Cinderborne:** In the end I... I'm just an actor.

*(Subsequent interactions)*

**Cinderborne:** Thank you, (Trailblazer). Mr. Ashveil is right. This is... not the same as playing the hero on a stage.

> **灰烬：**开拓者前辈，谢谢你。不死途先生说得对，这和舞台上扮演英雄…不一样。

**Cinderborne:** In the end I... I'm just an actor.

> **灰烬：**到头来，我…只是个演员啊。

[Recap] Ashveil apologized for stealing your spotlight at the exhibition and invited you to join forces in hunting down Fulwish. Ashveil recognized that you had what it took to become a Galaxy Ranger, but also hoped you would never need to walk the path of Rangers.

*(Talk to Ashveil, optional)*

**Ashveil:** Terribly sorry, Nameless. It was your exhibition and your enemies, and there I was, stealing your spotlight.

> **不死途：**真抱歉啊，无名客。你的展会，你的敌人，最后我却抢了你的风头。

**Ashveil:** Curious. I wonder how much Wishpower my appearance this time accumulates for the mask...

> **不死途：**真好奇，我这一回出场到底能给这张面具攒下多少愿力呢……

- ▸ You don't sound sorry at all.
- ▸ I'll remember this.
- ▸ Fine. You can have this one for free.

**Ashveil:** Oh, don't be like that. You can tell them that I'm Welt. We already look similar enough. Who knows? Maybe that'll redirect some Wishpower toward your "account" instead?

> **不死途：**别这样嘛，你可以对外宣称我是「瓦尔特」，反正我们俩的造型差不离。也许听到这个，民众的愿力就会流入你的「户头」里也说不定。

- ▸ Have you had dealings with Xianzhou before?

**Ashveil:** We fought side by side in the past, and I know a few faces. But as they say, the honeymoon phase never lasts.

> **不死途：**并肩作战过，也认识几个熟人。不过你懂，蜜月期总不会太久。

**Ashveil:** I heard some of theirs were still looking for me before I went into my last cryosleep. Ahem... I'm counting on you to keep this a secret.

> **不死途：**我上一次进入冷冻休眠前，还听说他们的人在找我。你可要替我保密啊。

- ▸ Why are you going incognito?

**Ashveil:** Some people want the head on my shoulders, while others want the shadow in my arm.

> **不死途：**有人想要我脖子上的脑袋，有人想要我手臂里的影子。

**Ashveil:** I'm old, and I just want some peace and quiet. That's all there is to it.

> **不死途：**人老了，只想图个清静，就这么简单。

- ▸ Will you continue participating in the Phantasmoon Games?

**Ashveil:** What, itching to get rid of me already? You're not that hasty, are you?

> **不死途：**怎么，迫不及待想要把我当对手给料理了？没这么心急吧？

**Ashveil:** An even greater prey just joined the game. Sure, she could be small fry by your standards, but in terms of viciousness, she might surprise you yet.

> **不死途：**眼下有个更庞大的猎物在蠢蠢欲动——或许不比你过去的敌人强大，但论歹毒，说不定更甚一筹。

**Ashveil:** Before the Supplicants go their separate ways, Fulwish is definitely a good hunting target for us to align our goals.

> **不死途：**在谒者分道扬镳前，满愿绝对是个值得大家团结一致狩猎的好目标。

- ▸ I will defeat Fulwish.
- ▸ Don't steal my spotlight this time.
- ▸ I will avenge Skott.

**Ashveil:** Such confidence... Heh, can't help but smile at that.

> **不死途：**这自信的眼神，还真是…让人忍不住嘴角上翘啊。

**Ashveil:** For some reason, you really remind me of a young man named Tiernan. You Nameless bunch just love jumping headfirst into fire pits, don't you?

> **不死途：**不知道为什么，你让我想起一个叫铁尔南的小伙子。你们这些无名客，就爱往火烧得最旺的地方跳对吧？

**Ashveil:** Well, given your skills, would you be interested in joining the Galaxy Rangers if you're still alive by the time the Phantasmoon Games conclude?

> **不死途：**看你身手不错，在幻月游戏结束后，如果还活着，有没有兴趣加入巡海游侠？

- ▸ I will always be a part of the Astral Express.

**Ashveil:** Good answer. I apologize for my presumptuousness. After all, becoming a Ranger comes with loss, suffering, and vengeance.

> **不死途：**不错的回答。是我冒昧了，毕竟成为游侠，意味着失去、痛苦和复仇。

- ▸ I thought you'd retired?

**Ashveil:** Good question. I guess old habits die hard when I see someone with potential.

> **不死途：**问得好，看到有潜力的苗子，想拉人入伙的老毛病犯了。

**Ashveil:** Being a Ranger holds little appeal, really. It is a path of loss, suffering, and vengeance.

> **不死途：**成为游侠也不是什么吸引人的事情。毕竟，那条路意味着失去、痛苦和复仇。

- ▸ I'd rather follow Tiernan's example.

**(Trailblazer):** Tiernan is also a senior member of the Express that I respect. I will follow in his footsteps.

> **开拓者：**铁尔南也是我敬重的列车前辈，我会追随他的脚步前进的。

**Ashveil:** Forgive me for being presumptuous. Being a Ranger holds little appeal, really. It is a path of loss, suffering, and vengeance.

> **不死途：**冒昧了。成为游侠也不是什么吸引人的事情。毕竟，那条路意味着失去、痛苦和复仇。

**Ashveil:** In that case, I will take back my words. Young Nameless, may you never have to tread the path of a Ranger.

> **不死途：**那让我收回刚才的话，年轻人，祝你永远无需踏上游侠的道路。

*(Subsequent interactions)*

**Ashveil:** Before the Supplicants go their separate ways, Fulwish is definitely a good hunting target for us to align our goals.

> **姬子：**满愿背后的支持者…这场游戏，或许早就被更危险的人物盯上了。

### Leave Pearl's office

> *More and more factions are getting dragged into the Phantasmoon Games. Beyond the Fools and the IPC, Fulwish and her Smiles Society have now brought the Abundance into Planarcadia, plunging the situation into deeper complexity and peril. Just as expected, vacations only last for the first little bit of any journey. Before pushing on, might as well hit the shops and take a breather.*

*(Talk to Pearl again, optional)*

**Pearl:** I hope you all get some proper rest before moving out again.

> **三月七：**哎，来者不善啊。

[Recap] Pearl secretly assisted Ashveil in his investigation to confirm her suspicions about the Smiles Society. Pearl promised that if they won the Phantasmoon Games, she would grant his wish: "To let a Galaxy Ranger die peacefully in his bed."

*(Go to mission location)*

**Ashveil:** You know, I'm not here to be an ornament.

> **不死途：**我杵在这儿，不是为了当个摆件。

**Pearl:** Assessment: You have something to ask me, Mr. Ashveil.

> **真珠：**判断：你有话想问我，不死途先生。

**Ashveil:** Lately, I've been on an extremely lucky streak. Be it Fulwish or Magnus, the leads practically gained sentience and came to me one by one.

> **不死途：**这段日子，我总觉得自己的行动格外幸运。满愿也好，真弘也罢，线索就像自己长了腿，一个个地往我跟前靠。

**Pearl:** Luck is also an important part of one's toolkit. Wouldn't you agree?

> **真珠：**运气也是实力最重要的一部分，不是吗？

**Ashveil:** I'm beginning to think you're the one behind it all. You tasked me to investigate the Society and protect the Astral Express, which all led to me unleashing my shadow on those people... just so you can confirm your hypothesis.

> **不死途：**我在想，这一切都是你干的吧？派我去调查幸研会，保护星穹列车，最终将一切导向让我解放影子吞掉那几人…以验证你对幸研会的猜想。

**Ashveil:** ...You had everything planned out since the beginning, hadn't you?

> **不死途：**——这一切，都是你安排的，对吧？

**Pearl:** If I answer "Yes," would it wound your ego?

> **真珠：**如果我说「是」，会伤到你的自尊心吗？

**Ashveil:** ...Perhaps.

> **不死途：**…也许吧。

**Pearl:** Yes. I paved the way for your investigation, leading you straight to the suspects.

> **真珠：**是。是我为你铺平了调查的路，让你笔直走到了疑犯面前。

**Pearl:** Through you, I confirmed my suspicions about the Research Society. However, the question of Abundance Transformation remains a thorny issue for me.

> **真珠：**透过你，我证实了自己对幸研会的猜想。但「丰饶转变」这个答案，仍令我感到棘手。

**Pearl:** Fortunately, there's a ranger who specializes in confronting such abominations, having lived in long seclusion within Planarcadia.

> **真珠：**好在，二相乐园隐居着一位隐退许久的游侠之首，他对处理这类凶煞孽物很有经验。

**Ashveil:** Hmph...

> **不死途：**哼……

**Ashveil:** But you know the "shadow" sealed inside my arm... can be very dangerous.

> **不死途：**但你是知道的，封印在我手臂中的「影子」…那东西实在很危险。

**Ashveil:** Simply removing the nail can cause it to stir and resonate with the fragment and fissure.

> **不死途：**光是拔出钉子，「残卷」和裂隙就和它产生了共鸣，躁动了起来。

**Ashveil:** Had I not kept it under control at that time, the fight would have ended with all of us buried in that bottomless abyss. Is that part of your plans too, Pearl?

> **不死途：**如果当时我没能制住它，这场战斗会把我们所有人葬送在那个深不见底的空间里——真珠，这也是你想要的吗？

**Pearl:** Humans fear the worst, but to the Intellitrons, such fears are merely irrational deterrents.

> **真珠：**人类会为更坏的假设而恐惧。但对智械而言，那是非理性的干扰。

**Pearl:** Wasn't the Mask of Elation I gave you enough to pin down the "shadow"?

> **真珠：**我交给你的那张「欢愉假面」，不是已经给予了你足够力量钉住那道「影子」了吗？

**Pearl:** That Gift you found through the guidance of the Express Navigator, "Starseer", Isee.

> **真珠：**——那位列车领航员…「观星者」我见指点你寻获的「馈赠」。

**Pearl:** That very shadow that devoured the Swarm and Lord Ravager Zulo ten Amber Eras ago.

> **真珠：**——那道在十个琥珀纪前，吞下过虫群与绝灭大君「诛罗」的影子。

**Pearl:** That very shadow that devoured Shuhu, the Emanator of Abundance, hundreds of years ago.

> **真珠：**——那道在数百年前，吞下了丰饶令使「倏忽」的影子。

**Ashveil:** You won the bet. You sure know a lot about this.

> **不死途：**你赌赢了。你知道的也实在不少。

**Pearl:** Please allow me to correct you. My ethical parameters prohibit engagement in probabilistic gambits. Accordingly, I operate by acquiring sufficient information to ensure zero margin for error.

> **真珠：**我必须纠正你：我的伦理观念不允许我以人类的性命进行概率游戏。所以，我一直以来的习惯是：掌握最充分的信息，确保万无一失。

**Pearl:** Then again, you are the species that brought "art" into existence as a form of expression. That seed of creation exists even in the most ignorant of humans' minds, and for that, I love and admire you.

> **真珠：**毕竟，你们是开创了「艺术」这一形式的种族。即便在最无知的人类的意识中，依然蕴有创造的种籽。为此，我怜爱、羡慕你们。

**Pearl:** Whether you believe or not, I will honor our initial agreement, Mr. La Mancha. At the moment of victory in the Phantasmoon Games, I will free you from all sufferings and fulfill your wish—

> **真珠：**无论你相信与否，拉曼查先生，我会遵守你我之间最初的协议：在幻月游戏胜利之刻，我会免除你曾受的一切苦楚，达成你的心愿——

**Pearl:** "To let a Galaxy Ranger die peacefully in his bed."

> **真珠：**「令一位巡海游侠安然老死床榻」。

**Ashveil:** In that case, I will continue to fight for you, my lady.

> **不死途：**那么，我将继续为你奋战，女士。

**Ashveil:** Oh, right.

> **不死途：**啊，对了。

**Ashveil:** You mentioned several times wanting to replicate the works of Graphia. I don't know much about art...

> **不死途：**你一直说自己想复现绘世的创作，我是不太懂艺术之类的玩意啊……

**Ashveil:** But I was considering that, despite the lack of artistic quality in your strokes when sealing those fissures today, to the people of this world...

> **不死途：**但我在想，今天封印那些裂隙时，你落下的每一笔虽然并无艺术性可言，但对这个世界的人们来说……

**Ashveil:** Your creation is just as important as the works of Graphia. Not a single innocent soul was lost on this day because of your protection.

> **不死途：**你刚才的创作绝不比绘世的画作拙劣。因为在今天，没有一个无辜的人死去，是你成功保护了他们。

**Ashveil:** Looking back to the ancestral painter of the Cindearth Age, do you truly think she cared about the artistic value of each stroke when she created World in Canvas?

> **不死途：**遥想烬土纪元时的那位画师之祖，她在创造画中世界时，难道会在意手中挥出每一笔的艺术价值吗？

**Pearl:** ...

> **真珠：**……

**Ashveil:** Tsk, but what do I know... These are just crude observations from an amateur, so pay them no mind. I'll be on my way!

> **不死途：**啧，我在胡说什么呢…一点艺术门外汉的锐评，别放心上。我走了！

[Recap] Silver Wolf met with Ashveil and called in a favor, asking him to lend the Stellaron Hunters a hand for old times' sake. Blade reunited with Ashveil after a long separation. They addressed each other as "Yingxing" and "La Mancha."

Moments later, in some corner of the city...

**Silver Wolf:** We meet again, Ashen Detective.

> **银狼：**又见面了啊，不死神探。

**Silver Wolf:** It's hard to believe that I came back for you, especially after learning that you wanted to sell me out to Pearl...

> **银狼：**我也实在很难相信自己居然会回头找你，尤其是在知道你想把我卖给真珠后……

**Ashveil:** Gotta thanks nature's gift. The 6.1 billion credits reward has appeared again.

> **不死途：**感谢大自然的馈赠，行走的六十一亿赏金又出现了。

**Silver Wolf:** Fortunately, I bypassed you and struck a deal with that Intellitron instead. Regrettably, my current bounty stands at zero.

> **银狼：**幸好，我跳过了你这中间商，和那个智械达成交易了。很遗憾，我现在的悬赏是，零。

**Silver Wolf:** Do you always turn your customers over to the police? How impressively short-sighted. No wonder your clientele is so small.

> **银狼：**你每次都会把上门的主顾卖给警方吗？真是把路走窄了，难怪客人那么少。

**Ashveil:** Speaking of short-sightedness, no one rivals the Stellaron Hunters, a.k.a. Destiny's Slaves, isn't it? You are all fated to march toward the Finality, and nothing can stop you.

> **不死途：**论把路走窄，谁也比不上为「命运的奴隶」奔走的星核猎手，不是吗？你们可是一条道奔着「终末」的方向，谁都拉不回来啊。

**Silver Wolf:** On the contrary, there are many paths for us, as long as that person can see.

> **银狼：**恰恰相反，我们的路有许多条——只要那家伙看得见。

**Ashveil:** In other words, he can't see the trajectory of your destiny in this world during this mission?

> **不死途：**也就是说，这趟行动，他看不见你们在这个世界的命运轨迹？

**Silver Wolf:** Take a guess.

> **银狼：**你猜？

**Ashveil:** I've heard what befell Amphoreus. The war among the Aeons is now inevitable... Whether it's the IPC or the Express, they are fighting for an uncertain future.

> **不死途：**翁法罗斯的消息我听说了。「列神之战」已成不可避免的定局…公司也好，列车也罢，都在为这无法预测的未来奔走。

**Ashveil:** And in the world where the Elation is the Center of Attention, there will eventually be more than one Aeon interfering. Even a Destiny's Slave would not be able to see the path ahead.

> **不死途：**而在这个被欢愉瞩目的世界里，也将面临不止一位星神的干涉。所以，就连命运的奴隶也无法看清前路。

**Silver Wolf:** Keep guessing. We're getting pretty close.

> **银狼：**接着猜，很接近了。

**Ashveil:** Enough of your games. I'd say it's not too late to rename the planet Adlivun.

> **不死途：**猜个屁，要我说这颗星球立刻改名亚德丽芬还来得及。

**Silver Wolf:** It's still too early to despair, old man. I came prepared... but I could use a helping hand.

> **银狼：**别这么绝望嘛，大叔。我可是有备而来的…但我也需要人搭把手。

**Ashveil:** 30 million credits. Pay or die.

> **不死途：**三千万信用点，先款后活。

**Silver Wolf:** Come on, give your friend a discount, will you? Say... zero credits?

> **银狼：**不是吧，看在老朋友的面子上，打个折呗。0信用点，如何？

**Ashveil:** I ain't your friend.

> **不死途：**谁和你是老朋友！

**Silver Wolf:** I meant him.

> **银狼：**我说的老朋友，是他。

**Ashveil:** ...

> **不死途：**……

**Ashveil:** It's been a while, Yingxing.

> **不死途：**应星，好久不见了。

**Blade:** It's been too long, Mr. La Mancha.

> **刃：**太久了，拉曼查先生。

[Recap] Yao Guang arrived at the Phantasmoon Courtyard and recognized its ancient tree as an Ambrosial Arbor. Evanescia appeared, introducing herself as the arbitrator presiding over the outcomes of the Phantasmoon Games. Above her head, Yao Guang observed no Weal or Woe fortunes.

**Yao Guang:** ...I knew it. There must be an explanation for the unusual fate of this world.

> **爻光：**…我就说嘛，占卜所得这个世界异于常情的「凶命」，一定是有解释的。

**Yao Guang:** Though I lost sight of the sparrow, I discovered a mysterious tree. I've finally found the heart of the Phantasmoon Games, thanks to this little birdie.

> **爻光：**循踪失雀影，得窥灵木春。多亏了这只小鸟，这不就找到了幻月游戏的根柢所在了吗？

**Yao Guang:** (Mr. Sunday, do you sense me?)

> **爻光：**（星期日先生，你能感应到我吗？）

**Yao Guang:** There's no reaction from the bird. What's wrong with it?

> **爻光：**这鸟儿到底是怎么了，毫无反应？

**Yao Guang:** Hello? Is anyone there? Can I get somebody here?

> **爻光：**喂，有人吗？来个人好吗？

**Resonance All Around:** No one's here... No one—

> **四周的回响：**没人——没人——

**Yao Guang:** That's a lie. I saw a pretty girl hiding right here through the bird's eyes. Who are you?

> **爻光：**骗人，我明明透过鸟儿的眼睛瞧见这儿躲着个漂亮姑娘啊。你是谁？

**Resonance All Around:** That's none of your business... None of your business...

> **四周的回响：**与你无关——与你无关——

**Yao Guang:** If that's how it's going to be, then I'm done playing nice here.

> **爻光：**既然这样，那就别怪本座说话不客气了。

**Yao Guang:** If my eyes weren't playing tricks on me, the ancient tree here must be the Ambrosial Arbor of Abundance, yes?

> **爻光：**如果我所见无误，扎根在此的这株古木，应该就是丰饶「建木」吧？

**Yao Guang:** Little girl, are you going to keep playing hide-and-seek, or will you come out and have a proper conversation with me?

> **爻光：**小姑娘，要继续躲起来装神弄鬼，还是好好出来和我谈谈？

**Evanescia:** The Department of Aberration Defense often comes here to record game progress, and now my movements have been exposed to complete outsiders... I suppose it's time to leave.

> **绯英：**「异常防御部」的人三天两头地往这儿跑，记录游戏进展也就罢了。现在连我的行藏都被无关的外人发现了…我想，我真的应该搬家了。

**Evanescia:** You sound unpleasant to talk to, but since you have eyes keen enough to tell what I am at a glance, I'll have a short chat with you.

> **绯英：**你这人说话口气凶巴巴的，但念在你眼光不坏，能一眼瞧出我来历的份上，我就好好同你聊上几句。

**Evanescia:** I will answer three questions, so ask away. Once you have your answers, I'm going to show you the door.

> **绯英：**我可以回答你三个问题，开口问吧，三个问题一过，我就要动手逐客咯。

**Yao Guang:** I wanted to question why the Joyseeker would permit the Ambrosial Arbor of Abundance to take root in a world they had blessed, but given their carefree nature, there was probably never any restriction to begin with.

> **爻光：**我本想问…为何常乐天君会默许一株丰饶建木在所赐福的世界里生根发芽，但料想以百无禁忌的性格，也没什么允不允许的。

**Yao Guang:** On my second question, are you a Faekind native to Ahatopia, or are you... one of the long-life species shaped by the Ambrosial Arbor?

> **爻光：**那么第二个问题，你究竟是这哈托彼亚土生土长的灵物，还是…由建木赋形点化的长生种？

**Evanescia:** Aren't you good at divination? Can you help me take a look whether the slip on my head says weal or woe?

> **绯英：**你不是很擅长卜卦望气嘛，能替我看看吗？我头上悬着的签是吉是凶？

**Yao Guang:** There's nothing on your head except your ears.

> **爻光：**你的头顶除了那对耳朵，空无一物。

**Evanescia:** And that itself is the answer.

> **绯英：**这就是答案。

**Evanescia:** I am the arbitrator who determines the outcome of the Phantasmoon Games. I am the raging waves of joy and the living pulse of this world.

> **绯英：**我是主持「幻月游戏」胜负的仲裁者。我是这世界上汹涌的欢欣之力，亦是它的生命脉动本身。

**Evanescia:** Diviner of the Xianzhou, the weal and woe you behold are but a single petal fallen from my hand. It blooms with the morning and withers by evening, reducing to dust in a fleeting moment. My true form is the Panarch Elwood, the embodiment of "instant" and "eternity."

> **绯英：**仙舟的卜者，你所见吉凶，不过是我掌中飘落的一瓣落花——朝生暮死，转瞬成尘。而真正的我，乃是容纳这「一瞬」与「永恒」的…镇世神木。

**Yao Guang:** Interesting. Took you quite some time to come up with those words, huh?

> **爻光：**有趣，想出这些词儿花了不少时间吧。

**Evanescia:** The one who created me did say most people wouldn't believe me. Tch, turns out THEY were right...

> **绯英：**创造我的那家伙说了，这些话说出来多半没人会信。啧，还真被祂说中了……

**Yao Guang:** I believe you. I feel the same way, for many do not trust the words of a diviner. Besides, no weal or woe has befallen you, which means your fate cannot be determined like that of ordinary mortals. You're not the first one I've met with this condition since my arrival here.

> **爻光：**我信。我和你同病相怜。因为卜者的话，从来也是信者寥寥。何况你的额顶并未悬着吉凶签，这意味着——你的命数超越凡人的吉凶之变。自打来到这儿，我已见着一例。

**Evanescia:** You're left with one question.

> **绯英：**你还剩下最后一个问题。

**Yao Guang:** Since you admit to being an abomination of Abundance... Shouldn't you explain the fate of this world's residents...?

> **爻光：**既然你自认是丰饶孽物…那是否该好好解释一下，这个世界居民头上所悬的凶命……

**Yao Guang:** ...Unless they are your doing? If your answer fails to convince me...

> **爻光：**…莫非是你的手笔？若你口吐的答案不能教我信服——

**Yao Guang:** It may be too late by the time I summon the Lux Arrow from the sky with the Reignbow.

> **爻光：**等我一支帝弓箭符召来天上的光矢，一切可就来不及咯？

**Evanescia:** Chill out! You shouldn't make jokes like this.

> **绯英：**冷静，冷静！这样的玩笑可开不得。

**Evanescia:** I'm not as skilled at divination as you are. When you suddenly speak of the great weal or woe and interrogate me, I get confused too...

> **绯英：**我没你这般能掐会算的本事，你突然说起什么大吉大凶的事，又问这问那的，我也很茫然啊……

**Evanescia:** After all, isn't every rule and every variable of the Phantasmoon Games dictated by the impulse of some god?

> **绯英：**毕竟，这「幻月游戏」背后的种种规则、变数，不都是某位神明一时兴起的结果嘛……

**Evanescia:** As for the brewing storm... let's settle down and discuss...

> **绯英：**要说眼下这风雨欲来的局势…我们不妨好好聊聊——

**Evanescia:** Whether the turmoil once again is the result of the Laughter's mischief?

> **绯英：**——会不会是某个混蛋乐子神又唯恐天下不乱了？

[Recap] Himeko believed you must inform your companions of this intel as soon as possible to stop Fulwish from winning the Phantasmoon Games. During a brief moment of peace, March 7th suggested the two of you take a stroll through the streets.

**March 7th:** This entire day has felt... unreal.

> **三月七：**感觉，经历了好不真实的一天。

**March 7th:** We had situations lined up one after another, and a growing crisis on our hands.

> **三月七：**事情一件一件地发生，需要解决的危机也越来越大。

**Himeko:** The Research Society is insane to meddle with the power of Abundance. There's no telling how many have already undergone the Happiness Surgery...

> **姬子：**幸研会竟然真的疯狂到了染指丰饶的力量。也不知到底有多少人接受了「幸福手术」……

**Himeko:** Fulwish witnessed the tragedy caused by the Herald of Death fifteen years ago, and now she herself has assumed that dreadful mantle.

> **姬子：**满愿，明明她也亲眼目睹了告死魔带来的惨剧，但十五年后，她却成为了新告死魔的制造者。

**Himeko:** We have to relay this information to Dan Heng, Sunday, and General Yao Guang as soon as possible.

> **姬子：**我们得尽快将情报告诉丹恒、星期日和爻光将军。

- ▸ Does Fulwish really think she could win?
- ▸ Fulwish is declaring war against this world.

**Himeko:** Fulwish is definitely aware of the enemies she's facing.

> **姬子：**自己究竟在面对什么样的敌人，满愿不可能不清楚这一点。

**Himeko:** That is why we must win an audience with Aha. At the very least, we need to stop Fulwish from winning the game.

> **姬子：**事到如今，我们更要赢得谒见阿哈的机会，至少要阻止她赢得游戏的胜利。

**March 7th:** Yeah. The thought of the Research Society of Happy Smiles going through with their plan gives me the chills...

> **三月七：**是啊，要是幸福微笑研究会成功了…光是想想就觉得那画面很可怕。

**Himeko:** It's been a long day, and all of you must be very tired. Take this chance to get some proper rest. I'll go meet Welt back on the Express. March, (Trailblazer), what about you?

> **姬子：**忙碌了一天，大家应该都累了，抓紧这短暂的和平时间，好好休息一下吧。我要先回列车和瓦尔特汇合了，小三月、开拓者，你们呢？

**March 7th:** The best way to recharge is to waste time, so I'm staying outside for some fresh air.

> **三月七：**疲惫的时刻，最好的充电方式就是虚度时间，我先留在外面呼吸点新鲜空气吧。

**(Trailblazer):** Sounds good. I was thinking of heading to the streets for a walk.

> **开拓者：**正好，我也想在街头转转。

### Take a stroll through the streets

*(Talk to March 7th, optional)*

**March 7th:** Hey, I'm too tired to join in the fun. Go ahead if you want to play. I'll wait here for you.

> **三月七：**哎，我已经累得连凑热闹的劲儿都没了，你要是想玩就去吧，我在这儿等你。

**Bustling Crowd:** No, I heard it... You must have misheard...

> **熙熙攘攘的人群：**——我真的听见了——是你听错了吧？

**Bustling Crowd:** I wasn't kidding. The moon was laughing... I'm just here to join the fun... I bet you misheard it...

> **熙熙攘攘的人群：**——真的，刚才月亮发笑了——我只是来凑凑热闹的——你听错了吧？

**(Trailblazer):** What's happening?

> **开拓者：**怎么回事？

*(While approaching the mission location)*

**Confused Onlooker:** What happened?

**Baffled Onlooker:** Look at the moon in the sky... Something is definitely wrong with it!

**Phantasmoon:** Hey there! All the smarties and dummies, children and adults, Fools and entertainers, can you all hear me?

> **幻月：**喂喂，地上的聪明人和笨蛋们，小朋友和大朋友们，愚者和伶人们，大家能听见吗？

*(Go to mission location)*
